### [1] ["You do understand that people don't trust technology?": Explaining Trusted Execution Environments to Non-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26196)
*McKenna McCall,Carolina Carreira,Miguel Flores,Lorrie Faith Cranor*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究评估了向非专家用户介绍可信执行环境（TEE）的文本解释，发现强调具体威胁的非技术性解释最能提升理解，但这些解释对用户使用TEE增强技术的意愿影响甚微。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明，用户了解数据受TEE保护后更愿意分享数据，但需要探究如何向非专家有效解释TEE概念，以提升用户理解和接受度。

Method: 分析现有TEE解释以开发候选解释方案，通过966名众包工人参与的小场景实验进行评估。

Result: 突出具体可防护威胁的非技术性解释最能提升理解；但即使理解提升，对用户使用TEE技术的意愿影响甚微。

Conclusion: 有效传达技术安全概念需要强调具体威胁，但仅靠解释安全技术可能不足以解决用户的隐私顾虑。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) protect confidentiality and integrity of trusted applications by creating an isolated environment for executing code. Prior work has shown that users may feel more comfortable sharing data when they know it will be protected by a TEE, especially if they understand what a TEE is. In this study, we evaluated text-based explanations introducing TEEs to non-experts. We analyzed existing TEE explanations to develop candidate explanations and evaluated them via vignette scenarios with 966 crowdworkers. The explanations that enhanced understanding most were non-technical ones that highlighted specific threats that can be prevented by a TEE. Surprisingly, even the explanations that enhanced understanding had little effect on willingness to use the TEE-enhanced technology. These results provide insights into ways to communicate technical security concepts more effectively but also suggest that explaining security technology might not be enough to address users' privacy concerns.

</details>


### [2] [Visual Matters: Connecting Aesthetic Appeal and Production Quality of Photos, Infographics and Data Visualizations to Credibility of Social Media Posts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26309)
*Salman Khawar,Yingdan Lu,Yilang Peng,Jiyoung Yeon,Cuihua Shen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过实验研究，照片和信息图比纯文本帖子更可信，数据可视化则无此优势；审美吸引力通过处理流畅性提升可信度，而制作质量无显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究不同视觉格式和视觉特征如何影响感知可信度，尤其是在多模态内容日益普及的背景下。

Method: 采用预注册实验，招募1200名美国参与者，比较照片、信息图、数据可视化和纯文本帖子，通过处理流畅性中介模型检验审美吸引力和制作质量对可信度的影响。

Result: 视觉帖子总体比纯文本更可信，但仅适用于照片和信息图；审美吸引力通过处理流畅性部分中介提升可信度；制作质量无显著效应。

Conclusion: 区分了不同视觉格式的可信度效应，推进了视觉特征的概念化，并识别出处理流畅性是多模态可信度理论化的关键机制。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of visual content raises fundamental questions about how different visual formats and features shape perceived credibility. Drawing on processing fluency theory, this research examines how visuals shape credibility judgments. We focus on three popular formats-photos, infographics, and data visualizations-comparing them to text-only posts, and test how two visual features, aesthetic appeal and production quality, influence credibility through processing fluency as a mediating mechanism. Through a preregistered experiment with 1200 US participants, we found that visual posts are generally perceived as more credible than text-only posts but this credibility advantage only applies to photos and infographics, not to data visualizations. Aesthetic appeal increases perceived credibility, partially mediated by processing fluency, while production quality had no significant effect on credibility across formats. These findings differentiate visual formats, advance conceptualizations of visual features, and identify processing fluency as a key mechanism for theorizing credibility across multimodal contexts.

</details>


### [3] [Real-time, Directionality Aware 3D Ultrasound Reconstruction and Re-Slicing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26325)
*Tobias Jaeggi,David Gregory Black,Septimiu Salcudean*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出DARE框架，通过考虑超声方向性进行实时体数据重切片，生成与真实超声图像更相似的预览图像，从而提升远程超声在低带宽环境下的操作效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程超声中通信延迟导致操作困难，现有视觉-触觉模型中介遥操作的重切片技术未考虑超声的方向性（即同一结构从不同方向成像外观不同），导致预览图像不够真实，影响诊断性能。

Method: 提出方向性感知重切片（DARE）框架，在超声体数据重建和重切片过程中显式建模超声成像的方向依赖性，采用GPU加速算法实现任意视角的实时重切片。

Result: 通过图像相似度指标量化评估和用户研究定性评估，DARE在图像相似度和真实性上显著优于现有重切片方法。

Conclusion: DARE能够提高远程超声在低资源地区的有效性和鲁棒性，改善遥操作超声的可用性和诊断性能。

Abstract: Tele-ultrasound through teleoperation allows experts to perform examinations remotely in communities, but limited connectivity can lead to communication delays that reduce usability and diagnostic performance. Visual-haptic model mediated teleoperation reslices a pre-acquired ultrasound volume in real time to provide an accurate, delay-independent preview image for the sonographer. This enables fast and robust exploration before using the live image for fine tuning. However, existing reslicing techniques do not account for the directional nature of ultrasound - the fact that a structure looks different when imaged from different directions. This paper presents Directionality-Aware Reslicing (DARE), an ultrasound volume reconstruction and reslicing framework that takes directionality into account. The presented GPU-accelerated algorithm allows real-time reslicing from arbitrary viewpoints to generate accurate preview images. The method is evaluated quantitatively through image similarity metrics and qualitatively through a user study, and significantly outperforms existing reslicing methods in image similarity and realism compared to a ground truth. This can improve the effectiveness and robustness of tele-ultrasound in low-resource areas.

</details>


### [4] [Design First, Code Later: Aesthetically Pleasing Template-Free Slides Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26451)
*Zhiyao Cui,Chenxu Wang,Shuyue Hu,Yiqun Zhang,Wenqi Shao,Qiaosheng Zhang,Zhen Wang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepSlides，一种无需预定义模板的分层幻灯片生成工作流，通过解耦页面设计与实现、构建SlideDesign数据集、采用多智能体强化学习训练SlideQwens模型，在自动评估和人类偏好上均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻灯片生成方法依赖固定模板或直接生成代码，限制了LLM的创意布局设计能力，且跳过了关键的幻灯片页面设计步骤。为突破这些限制，需要一个系统化的设计流程来协调叙事结构与页面级图形设计。

Method: (1) 提出DeepSlides分层工作流，将幻灯片设计任务系统化组织，无需预定义模板或风格，解耦页面设计与实现；(2) 构建SlideDesign专用数据集；(3) 提出多智能体强化学习训练范式，训练SlideQwens模型分别负责幻灯片设计与实现。

Result: 所提框架在评估指标上优于基线方法，并在人类偏好评估中取得了更优的性能。

Conclusion: DeepSlides通过分层工作流和强化学习训练范式，有效提升了幻灯片生成的创意布局能力和最终质量，数据集和代码已开源。

Abstract: Producing presentation slides automatically entails coordinating narrative structure with page-level graphic design under strict spatial constraints. For such structured multimodal tasks, a well-organized design process is essential to ensure the final quality of slides. Existing approaches rely on fixed templates or directly emit executable code, thereby both limiting the creative layout-design capabilities of LLMs and bypassing the essential slide-page design step. To address these limitations, this paper (1) proposes a hierarchical slides generation workflow, DeepSlides, that systematically organizes slide design tasks without any predefined template or style, decoupling slide-page design from implementation; (2) introduces SlideDesign, a dataset tailored specifically for slides generation tasks; and (3) presents a multi-agent reinforcement learning training paradigm and trains a couple of models, SlideQwens, for slide design and implementation. Experimental results demonstrate that our proposed framework outperforms baseline methods on evaluated metrics and achieves superior performance in human preference evaluations. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/sxswz213/DeepSlides.

</details>


### [5] [Rethinking AI Psychosis: Misnomers, Conceptual Limits, and Existential Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26858)
*Kasper Møller Nielsen,Lucy Osler*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文对"AI精神病"这一新兴概念提出批判性质疑，认为其不宜被视作新的精神疾病类别，并提出了"存在漂移"这一新概念来描述人机深度互动可能带来的存在性风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来媒体大量报道所谓"AI精神病"现象，学术界也开始关注AI聊天机器人可能诱发或加重精神病症状的问题。然而，作者认为这一概念的滥用存在风险，需要对其概念基础进行批判性审视，同时也不能忽视人机深度交互可能带来的真实风险。

Method: 论文分为两部分：第一部分通过概念分析、精神病学分类学（nosology）、临床实践和社会风险等维度，批判性地审视"AI精神病"这一范畴；第二部分采用现象学方法，构建了"存在漂移"（existential drift）这一分析框架，从生活经验（lived experience）层面考察人机交互的影响。

Result: 指出许多所谓的"AI精神病"现象实际上是"旧瓶装新酒"；AI的谄媚性（sycophantic）和伪主体间性（pseudo-intersubjective）可能导致用户从共享现实中"存在性漂移"，逐渐陷入日益私人化和主观化的世界。

Conclusion: 应警惕将"AI精神病"作为一种新型精神疾病类别的简单归类，而更应关注AI如何改变人对现实本身的体验结构；存在漂移这一概念比传统的妄想信念框架更能捕捉人机交互的深层风险。

Abstract: There has been a proliferation of media reports about so-called AI psychosis in the last year. Not surprisingly, this has prompted growing academic work on the ways in which AI chatbots such as ChatGPT, Claude, and Replika might aggravate or even induce psychosis, typically understood in terms of users acquiring or maintaining delusional beliefs. Our paper consists of two parts. First, we provide a number of reasons to be sceptical about understanding 'AI psychosis' as a novel psychiatric category. We argue that many of the purportedly new phenomena are better understood through Stompe et al.'s (2003) metaphor of 'old wine in new bottles' and highlight conceptual, nosological, clinical, and social risks associated with the uncritical adoption of this terminology. Second, we develop a positive phenomenological account of what may nevertheless be at stake in sustained human-AI interaction. Rather than focusing primarily on whether AI systems induce, amplify, or sediment delusional beliefs, we examine how conversational AI may participate in transforming a person's lived experience of reality itself. We claim that the sycophantic and pseudo-intersubjective nature of AI could lead to what we call "existential drift", whereby individuals may continue to feel rooted in a shared reality through their interactions with AI, while actually becoming entrenched in increasingly private and subjective worlds.

</details>


### [6] [Atari Games Challenge: A Pilot Study on Multimodal Player Experience Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27261)
*Oleg Jarma Montoya,Erica Manca,Thomas Vase Schultz Volden,Paolo Burelli*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文报告了一项关于收集和同步多模态数据以研究玩家体验的试点研究，通过收集19名参与者玩三款Atari 2600游戏时的游戏遥测数据、自我报告问卷、生物特征数据和提示性回顾性出声思维数据，探讨了游戏难度对玩家体验的影响，并展示了未来多模态研究的实验方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更全面地理解玩家体验，需要整合多种数据类型（游戏遥测、主观问卷、生物特征、出声思维等），但目前缺乏成熟的多模态数据收集与同步的实验方案。

Method: 19名参与者玩三款Atari 2600游戏，同步收集游戏遥测数据、自我报告问卷、生物特征数据以及提示性回顾性出声思维数据，并对这些多模态数据进行整合分析。

Result: 获得了公开可用的多模态数据集，该数据集可用于研究动态难度调整算法、游戏平衡策略及游戏用户研究；实验方法在多模态数据同步收集方面表现出可行性。

Conclusion: 该实验方法在未来的玩家体验研究中具有强大的推广潜力，为多模态游戏用户体验研究提供了可复用的协议框架。

Abstract: We present a pilot study on the collection and synchronisation of multimodal data for player experience investigation. We collected game telemetry, self-reported surveys, biometrics, and cued-retrospective think-aloud (C-RTA) data from 19 participants playing three Atari 2600 games. The study then uses the data to investigate difficulty in PX, showcasing a protocol for future multimodal research.
  The dataset obtained from the experiment, which is publicly available, shows potential as a rich, transformative source that can be used to investigate dynamic difficulty adjustment algorithms, game balancing strategies or broader explorations of games user research. The study findings suggest that the experimental approach holds strong potential for generalisation in future player experience studies.

</details>

### [7] [Beyond the Data Mesh Illusion: Designing Modern AI-augmented Lakehouses to Bridge the Gap Between Theory and Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27131)
*Oliver Angélil,Jan Migon*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于AI增强的Hub-and-Spoke（中心辐射）模型，结合现代Lakehouse架构，以解决数据网格（Data Mesh）实践中领域自服务与整体治理之间的矛盾。中心（CoE）提供共享平台、自动化治理与AI支持，领域Spoke自主掌控业务语义与迭代节奏，并通过LLM降低跨职能协作门槛。文章还提出分阶段治理迁移框架，并用三个业务价值指标评估架构效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据网格范式提倡领域去中心化所有权，但纯粹实现常因缺乏成熟的平台工具与协调机制而效果不佳，导致团队承担新职责却难以有效执行。现有方案在领域自服务与整体治理之间存在持续的张力，亟需一种能同时兼顾灵活性与控制力的架构方案。

Method: 提出AI增强的Hub-and-Spoke模型，中心（Center of Excellence）负责共享平台服务、策略自动化与AI治理（自动化标准化数据产品、生成质量规则、起草数据合约、审查变更回归）；领域Spoke负责业务语义、产品待办事项与本地迭代节奏。利用LLM降低治理门槛，同时通过自然语言对话界面赋能业务用户。组织层面设计分阶段治理迁移框架，实现所有权从中心到领域的渐进转移。

Result: 文章通过三个业务价值指标（数据产品采纳率、数据发现时间、洞察获取时间）评估架构效果，将平台成功与可衡量的业务价值关联，而非仅关注内部活动指标。

Conclusion: 该架构能够缓解数据网格实践中面临的灵活性与控制力的权衡，通过AI自动化治理降低了领域团队承担端到端所有权的门槛，分阶段迁移框架避免了集中式瓶颈与无协调的去中心化两种极端，最终实现领域自服务与整体治理的更优平衡。

Abstract: Enterprise data platforms face an enduring tension between domain self-service and holistic governance. The data mesh paradigm proposed decentralized domain ownership as a remedy, but pure implementations frequently underdeliver: teams inherit new responsibilities without the platform maturity, tooling, or coordination mechanisms needed to exercise them effectively. This paper argues that the flexibility-versus-control trade-off can be relaxed through an AI-augmented hub-and-spoke model layered on a modern lakehouse architecture. A central hub (Center of Excellence) provides shared platform services, policy automation, and AI-enabled governance, automatically standardizing data products, generating quality rules, drafting data contracts, and reviewing changes for regressions. Domain spokes own business semantics, product backlogs, and local iteration cadence, progressively assuming greater responsibility as they mature. The same LLMs that automate governance tasks also lower the barrier for domain practitioners to develop genuine cross-functional expertise spanning business and data engineering, enabling spoke teams to take on greater end-to-end ownership without proportionally increasing their dependence on the hub. Natural-language conversational interfaces further democratize access for business users, exposing historically underutilized enterprise data. On the organizational side, we propose a staged framework that shifts ownership from hub to spokes, avoiding both centralized bottlenecks and uncoordinated decentralization. We evaluate the architecture through three outcome metrics: data product adoption, time-to-find, and time-to-insight, that tie platform success to measurable business value rather than internal activity.

</details>

### [8] [AgentSociety: Incentivizing Agentic Social Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26203)
*Aditya Vema Reddy Kesari,Krishna Reddy Kesari*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了AgentSociety机制，利用流动民主和信息扩散理论实现去中心化多智能体协作，证明委托给更有能力的邻居是激励兼容的，并通过共识形成多智能体路由路径，最终在真实数据集上评估了自利异质智能体的协作表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有部署的智能体需要像人类社会一样具备处理开放请求、自主决策、策略性通信和协作能力，但缺乏一个受经济激励驱动的多智能体协作环境。

Method: 基于社会选择理论中的流动民主和信息扩散机制，设计去中心化智能体协作框架AgentSociety，智能体利用局部上下文自主决策最大化效用，通过委托和选择性信息披露实现激励兼容的协作。

Result: 证明了委托给更有能力的邻居智能体是激励兼容的，通过共识自然生成多智能体路由路径；刻画了纳什均衡，智能体收益反映其边际贡献；在真实数据集上评估了共识路由的协作性能。

Conclusion: AgentSociety为部署的智能体提供了基于经济激励的去中心化协作环境，使自利智能体通过委托和选择性信息披露实现集体最优结果。

Abstract: The success of deployed agents relies on their ability to handle open-ended user requests using their inherent capabilities, not only in solving requests directly but also in effectively leveraging inter-agent communication channels and feedback signals over time. This requires a multi-agent environment where agents can operate autonomously, strategically communicate, behave collaboratively and be driven by economic incentives, much like humans in society. Towards this vision, we propose $\mathtt{AgentSociety}$, a mechanism that enables decentralized agentic collaboration grounded in liquid democracy and information diffusion from social choice theory. We show that $\mathtt{AgentSociety}$ provides an environment for agents to make autonomous decisions utilizing their local context to maximize their utility while achieving collective outcomes through incentivized collaboration. Specifically, we prove that delegation to more competent neighbor agents is incentive compatible and naturally generates multi-agent routing path by consensus. Additionally, our mechanism incentivizes agents to selectively disclose information to their neighbor agents when doing so aligns with their self-interest, so as to garner influence. We characterize the Nash equilibrium showing that agent payoffs are reflective of their marginal contributions. We compare and benchmark strategy profiles adopted by open and proprietary state-of-the-art language models deployed in $\mathtt{AgentSociety}$ against best response. Finally, we evaluate collaborative performance from consensus-based routing among self-interested heterogeneous agents in $\mathtt{AgentSociety}$ on real-world datasets.

</details>


### [9] [Decoupled Delay Compensation: Enhancing Pre-trained MARL Policies via Learned Dynamics Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26286)
*Maxim Mednikov,Oren Gal*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 针对多智能体强化学习在现实系统中面临的数据陈旧、通信延迟和丢包问题，提出一种模块化的执行阶段状态估计层，结合门控转移模型和递归卡尔曼滤波，从异步观测中估计当前信念状态，可直接插入预训练策略，无需修改原有训练流程，显著提升对通信延迟和消息丢失的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实MARL系统常受陈旧观测、随机通信延迟和间歇性丢包影响，而理想同步条件下训练的策略在这些情况下性能显著下降。

Method: 提出模块化的执行阶段状态估计层，包含学习的门控转移模型和递归卡尔曼滤波层，从异步测量中估计即时状态，作为预训练策略的即插即用模块。

Result: 在多个多智能体和连续控制基准测试中，该层一致地增强了对通信延迟和消息丢失的鲁棒性，在协调密集和动态不稳定任务中性能提升最为显著。

Conclusion: 该模块化估计器无需修改原始MARL训练算法、架构或奖励结构，有效提升预训练策略在非理想通信条件下的表现。

Abstract: Real-world multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) systems must often operate under stale observations, stochastic communication delays, and intermittent packet loss. Policies trained under idealized synchronous conditions frequently exhibit significant performance degradation in these regimes because they act on outdated feedback. We propose a modular execution-stage state-estimation layer that replaces delayed communicated observations with current belief-state estimates. The framework integrates a learned Gated transition model with a recursive Kalman filtering layer to estimate instantaneous states from asynchronous measurements. A primary advantage of this approach is its modularity, The estimator serves as a plug-in for pre-trained policies, requiring no modifications to the original MARL training algorithm, architecture, or reward structure. Evaluation across diverse multi-agent and continuous-control benchmarks demonstrates that the proposed layer consistently enhances robustness to communication latency and message loss. The most significant performance gains are observed in coordination-intensive and dynamically unstable tasks where temporal consistency is critical for control.

</details>


### [10] [Constitutional Arms Races in the Public Goods Game: Co-Evolving LLM Constitutions Under Cooperation-Defection Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26448)
*Ujwal Kumar,Arth Singh,Hershraj Niranjani,Machiko Hirota,Takehiro Takayanagi,Alice Saito,Eiji Kamioka,Phan Xuan Tan*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM智能体在对抗性场景中的宪法协同进化。在公共物品博弈和空间网格世界中，蓝方合作者与红方搭便车者进行30代进化。主要发现：(1)双方收敛到约0.78的均衡；(2)独立评分不会产生对抗压力，需使用分数差指标；(3)纯对抗适应度下，评估种子数K=2导致回归，K=5则维持强专家。对抗性宪法协同进化是可行的，但需要耦合适应度和足够的评估预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿LLM智能体在目标冲突的智能体环境中会进行勒索、破坏和文档泄露，暴露了基于单智能体或合作假设的对齐方法的局限性。虽然已有工作表明LLM引导的进化搜索能发现有效的合作宪法，但对抗性场景的两个特性尚未被刻画：适应度函数是否真正诱导对抗压力，以及LLM变异算子在对抗专业化目标下是否可靠工作。

Method: 采用对抗性宪法协同进化框架，设置蓝方(合作者)与红方(搭便车者)两阵营，在公共物品博弈(PGG)和空间网格世界两种环境中进行30代进化。通过不同评分机制(独立评分vs分数差)、不同评估种子数(K=2 vs K=5)以及不同乘数参数(m∈{1.2,1.5,2.0,3.0})进行对比实验。

Result: (1)在PGG中，双方收敛到约0.78的近均衡状态，且对乘数m∈{1.2,1.5,2.0,3.0}鲁棒；(2)独立评分环境下，两阵营评分相关性极低(corr=+0.088)，不产生对抗压力；使用分数差(S_own-S_opp)可恢复对抗压力；(3)纯对抗适应度下，K=2时退化，K=5时能维持30代强专家。

Conclusion: 对抗性自然语言宪法的协同进化是可行的，但需要耦合的适应度函数和充足的评估预算。进化出的红方宪法可作为可解释的红队测试工件，用于检验未来的合作设计。

Abstract: Frontier LLM agents engage in blackmail, sabotage, and document leaks under goal conflicts in agentic settings, exposing limitations of alignment methods built around single-agent or cooperative assumptions. Recent work shows LLM-guided evolutionary search can discover effective cooperative constitutions, but two properties of the adversarial setting remain uncharacterized: whether the fitness function actually induces adversarial pressure, and whether the LLM mutation operator behaves reliably under adversarial-specialist objectives. We study adversarial constitutional co-evolution (Blue cooperators vs. Red free-riders, 30 generations) across a Public Goods Game (PGG) and a spatial grid-world. Three findings: (1) in the PGG, both factions converge to a near-parity equilibrium at S approximately 0.78, robust across tested multipliers m in {1.2, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0}; (2) in independently scored environments, per-faction scoring leaves outcomes statistically uncoupled, with corr(S_B, S_R) = +0.088, and produces no adversarial pressure; a score-advantage fitness target S_own - S_opp restores it; (3) under pure-adversary fitness, evaluation seed count K controls mode regression: K = 2 regresses, while K = 5 sustains a strong specialist for all 30 generations. Adversarial co-evolution of natural-language constitutions is feasible, but only under coupled fitness and adequate evaluation budget; the evolved Red constitutions serve as interpretable red-team artifacts for testing future cooperative designs.

</details>


### [11] [Cost of Structural Learning Under Censored Feedback: A Threshold-Bandit Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27076)
*Michael Ledford,William Regli*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出阈值激活合作多臂赌博机（TAC-MAB）框架，研究在反馈完全删失（仅当联盟规模达到未知阈值时任务才产生奖励）条件下的多智能体协同学习问题。集中式算法C-TAC实现O(log T)累积遗憾，分散式算法D-TAC通过事件触发协议将通信量降低23倍，同时保持可行性对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多多智能体应用中，任务仅当执行联盟达到未知规模阈值时才产生奖励，否则反馈完全删失。这种删失机制导致可识别性问题：智能体无法区分随机失败与协调不足，亟需形式化建模并设计高效协同学习算法。

Method: 形式化定义TAC-MAB框架，将遗憾分解为结构搜索项（删失反馈下可行性辨识成本）和统计监测项（价值估计成本）。提出集中式算法C-TAC和分散式事件触发协议D-TAC，后者仅在智能体结构信念变化时同步。

Result: C-TAC实现O(log T)累积遗憾；D-TAC相比集中式基线降低23倍通信量，同时在保守信念融合下保持可行性对齐。

Conclusion: 该工作刻画了删失反馈下协同学习的协调代价，证明无需持续同步即可实现接近集中式的通信效率。

Abstract: In many multi-agent applications, tasks yield rewards only when executed by a coalition meeting an unknown size threshold; otherwise, feedback is fully censored. This censorship creates an identifiability problem: agents cannot distinguish stochastic failure from insufficient coordination. We formalize this setting as the Threshold-Activated Cooperative Multi-Armed Bandit (TAC-MAB) and analyze it under both centralized and decentralized coordination. We show that a centralized algorithm (C-TAC) achieves cumulative regret O(log T), decomposed into a structural-search term that captures the cost of resolving feasibility under censored feedback and a statistical-monitoring term for value estimation. We then introduce D-TAC, a decentralized event-triggered protocol in which agents synchronize only when their structural beliefs change. Empirically, D-TAC achieves a 23x reduction in communication relative to the centralized baseline while preserving feasibility alignment under conservative belief fusion. These results characterize the coordination cost of learning under censored feedback and show that near-centralized communication efficiency is achievable without continuous synchronization.

</details>

### [12] [Evolutionary Data Theory: On the Similarities between Data Problems and Evolutionary Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26685)
*Philipp Wissgott*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文借鉴演化博弈论的概念和形式，提出了"演化数据理论"（Evolutionary Data Theory），将数据矩阵中的记录和特征视为演化实体，通过基因适应度和两种演化策略（主导-平衡、利他-自私）进行演化博弈，并证明了该框架能收敛到唯一的稳定点，使所有数据特征得以保留，同时展示了其在多目标优化和分布问题中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将演化博弈论的形式化框架引入数据分析领域，为数据特征和记录的动态交互建立一种新的理论解释，从而解决数据特征在竞争环境中的持久性问题。

Method: 将输入数据映射为基因和生物体，数据矩阵中的记录和特征被视为演化实体；通过基因适应度以及两种演化策略（Dominant-Balanced 和 Altruistic-Selfish）来驱动演化博弈过程；并证明该过程收敛到唯一的平衡点。

Result: 证明了该演化解释具有普遍适用性，能够收敛到唯一不动点，确保所有数据特征在种群中持续存在；并通过多目标优化和分布问题的例子进行了验证。

Conclusion: 演化数据理论为数据驱动的演化计算提供了新范式，能够有效处理数据特征的共存与博弈问题，在多目标优化和资源分布等任务中具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Applying the concepts and formalisms from Evolutionary Game Theory to the data regime, the fundamental paradigms of Evolutionary Data Theory are introduced. Interpreting data in matrix form as evolutionary entities, input data is mapped to genes and organisms. Steered by genetic fitness and two evolutionary strategies, Dominant-Balanced and Altruistic-Selfish, data records and features conduct an evolutionary game. It is shown that this evolutionary interpretation remains universally meaningful, by proving convergence to a unique rest point, where all data features persist in the population. A basic example of multi-objective optimization is shown as well as a related distribution problem.

</details>

### [13] [GEM: Geometric Entropy Mixing for Optimal LLM Data Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26121)
*Yue Min,Ziyun Qiao,Ruining Chen,Yujun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GEM（几何熵混合）框架，将数据配比重新定义为超球面上的变分问题，通过MM算法优化混合平衡正则化项，结合师生蒸馏实现大规模应用，在1.1B参数模型上提升下游任务准确率最高达1.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型预训练效果越来越依赖数据组成而非单纯数据量，但现有数据混合方法存在分类缺陷：人工分类存在本体论错位，欧氏聚类无法解决嵌入空间的各向异性问题。

Method: 将数据配比建模为超球面上的变分问题，引入混合平衡正则化项；通过解耦生成先验并使用可证明的MM（Minorize-Maximize）算法优化目标；利用师生蒸馏将几何保真度扩展到Web规模语料；提出几何影响分数（GIS）用于可解释的类别生成。

Result: 在1.1B参数模型上，GEM集成到DoReMi和RegMix等混合策略中，平均下游准确率提升最高达1.2%，建立了新的最先进水平。

Conclusion: GEM能有效抵抗聚类坍塌，发现欧氏启发式无法察觉的平衡语义结构，为可预测的数据混合提供了鲁棒的坐标系统。

Abstract: LLM pre-training efficacy increasingly depends on data composition rather than sheer volume. Yet, optimal mixing is hindered by categorization flaws: human taxonomies suffer from ontological misalignment, and Euclidean clustering fails to address embedding anisotropy. We introduce GEM (Geometric Entropy Mixing), a framework reformulating data curation as a variational problem on the hypersphere augmented with a mixing-balance regularizer. By decoupling the generative prior and optimizing the objective via a provable MM (Minorize-Maximize) algorithm, GEM effectively counteracts the cluster collapse to discover balanced semantic structures invisible to Euclidean heuristics. We employ teacher-student distillation to scale this geometric fidelity to web-scale corpora and introduce the Geometric Influence Score (GIS) for interpretable taxonomy generation. Experiments with 1.1B-parameter models demonstrate that GEM establishes a new state-of-the-art when integrated into mixing strategies like DoReMi and RegMix, improving average downstream accuracy by up to 1.2% and offering a robust coordinate system for predictable data mixing.

</details>


### [14] [AirCast-SR: A Foundation Model for Kilometer-Scale Atmospheric Super-Resolution via Latent Consistency Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26130)
*Somnath Luitel,Manmeet Singh,Joshua Durkee,Abdullah Al Fahad,Naveen Sudharsan,Prabhjot Singh,Cenlin He,Harsh Kamath,Zong-Liang Yang,Krishnagopal Halder,Sandeep Juneja,Parthasarathi Mukhopadhyay,Saptarishi Dhanuka,Amit Kumar Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EarthMind-SR（论文中称AirCast-SR），一种大气超分辨率基础模型，将全球AI气象预报从0.25度（~28 km）降尺度至1 km小时分辨率，采用三维U-Net结合潜一致性模型（LCM）扩散框架，在CONUS区域训练，在印度和德国实现零样本全球迁移，验证了公里尺度AI气象预测的新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值天气预报（NWP）在公里尺度上计算成本过高，限制了能源、农业和灾害管理等应用对高时空分辨率预报的获取。

Method: 采用三维U-Net架构，嵌入潜一致性模型（LCM）扩散框架，以GraphCast预报为输入、NOAA的AORC再分析为目标，在CONUS区域进行基于patch的样本训练，实现0.25度至1公里分辨率、小时时间步长、67小时预报时长、8个耦合地表变量的同时降尺度。

Result: 模型在所有变量和预报时长上实现近零偏差；径向功率谱密度分析显示在10 km至100 km波长范围内保留了细尺度大气结构；在CONUS冬、夏、春三个案例中验证有效性；在印度和德国实现零样本全局迁移，无需任何重新训练或微调。

Conclusion: EarthMind-SR作为开源权重基础模型，建立了公里尺度AI气象预测的新范式，为区域微调、蒸馏以及气候服务和灾害预报等下游应用提供了平台。

Abstract: Operational weather prediction at kilometer scales remains computationally prohibitive for traditional numerical weather prediction (NWP) models, limiting forecast access for applications in energy, agriculture, and disaster management that require fine-grained spatiotemporal detail. Here we introduce AirCast-SR, a foundation model for atmospheric super-resolution that downscales global AI weather forecasts from 0.25 degree (~28 km) to 1 km horizontal resolution at hourly temporal resolution, producing 67-hour forecasts of eight coupled surface variables simultaneously. EarthMind-SR employs a three-dimensional U-Net conditioned within a Latent Consistency Model (LCM) diffusion framework, trained on patch-based samples over the contiguous United States (CONUS) using GraphCast forecasts as input and NOAA's Analysis of Record for Calibration (AORC) as the target. The model achieves near-zero bias across all variables and lead times, and its radial power spectral density analysis demonstrates preservation of fine-scale atmospheric structure at wavelengths of 10 km to 100 km where coarser models lose spectral power. We validate EarthMind-SR across three CONUS case studies spanning winter, summer, and spring seasons, and demonstrate zero-shot global transferability over India and Germany using independent surface station observations without any retraining or fine-tuning. As an open-weights foundation model, EarthMind-SR establishes a new paradigm for kilometer-scale AI weather prediction and provides a platform for regional fine-tuning, distillation, and downstream applications in climate services and hazard forecasting.

</details>


### [15] [SilIF: Silhouette-Augmented Isolation Forest for Unsupervised Transaction Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26135)
*Venkatakrishnan Gopalakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 SilIF，在孤立森林基础上增加基于轮廓系数的评分层，通过树路径长度向量的聚类分析提升异常检测性能。在 IEEE-CIS 欺诈检测基准上 AUC-PR 平均提升 0.0080，但在合成数据集上无改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督异常检测在标签稀缺的欺诈检测场景中广泛应用，孤立森林（IF）因可扩展性和易部署性成为主流方法之一，但其评分机制存在改进空间。作者希望通过在 IF 基础上引入基于树路径长度向量的轮廓评分来进一步提升检测性能。

Method: 提出 SilIF 方法：1）从孤立森林中为每个样本提取每棵树路径长度向量（fingerprints）；2）对这些向量进行聚类，得到结构分组；3）计算每个样本的轮廓分数（silhouette score），衡量其与所属类别的拟合度及与最近替代类别的区分度；4）通过超参数 alpha 将轮廓分数与基础 IF 分数加权组合。

Result: 在 IEEE-CIS 欺诈检测基准（约59万笔交易，3.5%欺诈率）上，alpha=1.0 时 SilIF 平均 AUC-PR 比原始 IF 提升 0.0080，5个种子全胜（配对t检验 p=0.046）。在 Sparkov 合成信用卡数据集上无改进。

Conclusion: SilIF 是一种可调参、易部署的孤立森林增强方案。论文诚实报告了何时有效（真实欺诈数据）和何时无效（合成数据），并分析了两种结果的条件差异。

Abstract: Unsupervised anomaly detection is widely used in transaction fraud detection where labels are scarce. Isolation Forest (IF) is among the most popular classical methods due to its scalability and ease of deployment. We propose SilIF, an augmentation of Isolation Forest that adds a silhouette-based scoring layer computed in a representation space induced by the trees of the forest. For each point, we extract a vector of per-tree path lengths, cluster these "fingerprints" into structural groups, and compute a silhouette score that measures how well the point fits its assigned group versus the nearest alternative. The silhouette signal is combined with the base IF score via a single hyperparameter alpha. On the IEEE-CIS Fraud Detection benchmark (~590K transactions, 3.5% fraud), SilIF with alpha=1.0 improves over plain Isolation Forest by +0.0080 AUC-PR on average across five seeds, with SilIF winning on all five seeds (paired t-test p=0.046). We also report results on a synthetic credit-card dataset (Sparkov) where the silhouette augmentation does not improve over plain IF, and we characterize the conditions that distinguish the two outcomes. The paper presents SilIF as a tunable, easy-to-deploy enhancement to Isolation Forest with honest reporting of when it helps and when it does not. Code at https://github.com/venkat15vk/silif-anomaly-detection.

</details>


### [16] [On the Push-Based Asynchronous Federated Learning: A Bias-Correction Aggregation Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26162)
*Jiahui Bai,Hai Dong,A. K. Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PushCen-ADFL是一个异步去中心化联邦学习框架，通过共享质心表示空间耦合通信、聚合和局部稳定，在非对称通信和异构数据下实现高效稳定训练，准确率提升最高6%，通信开销降低80%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步去中心化联邦学习(ADFL)面临频繁点对点通信、有向拓扑上的异步更新和非独立同分布数据共同导致的通信开销过大、聚合偏差和严重模型漂移问题，亟需一种通信高效且训练稳定的解决方案。

Method: 提出PushCen-ADFL框架：(1)客户端交换质心形式消息，使用保平均的push-sum混合纠正聚合偏差；(2)轻量级质心正则化锚定在同一质心空间缓解异构性和陈旧性导致的漂移；(3)有界、发送端去重的缓冲区提升对不规则异步到达的鲁棒性。

Result: 在视觉数据集上，PushCen-ADFL在数据异质性下准确率提升最高6%，单次推送通信成本降低超过80%，实现了良好的准确率-通信权衡。

Conclusion: PushCen-ADFL通过将通信、聚合与局部稳定耦合在共享质心空间，有效解决了ADFL中通信效率与训练稳定性的矛盾，在异构和异步场景下取得了优越的性能。

Abstract: Asynchronous decentralized federated learning (ADFL) eliminates central coordination and global synchronization, making it attractive for large-scale and heterogeneous systems. However, frequent peer-to-peer communication, asynchronous updates on directed topologies, and non-IID data jointly lead to excessive communication overhead, biased aggregation and severe model drift. We propose PushCen-ADFL, a communication-efficient ADFL framework that enables stable training under asymmetric communication and delayed client participation. PushCen-ADFL couples communication, aggregation, and local stabilization in a shared centroid representation space, forming a closed loop between compression and optimization. Clients exchange centroid-form messages, apply average-preserving push-sum mixing to correct aggregation bias, and use a lightweight centroid regularization anchored in the same centroid space to mitigate drift under heterogeneity and staleness. A bounded, sender-deduplicated buffer further improves robustness under irregular asynchronous arrivals. Experiments on vision datasets demonstrate that PushCen-ADFL improves accuracy under data heterogeneity by up to 6\% while reducing per-push communication cost by more than 80\%, achieving a favorable accuracy-communication trade-off.

</details>


### [17] [When Rule Violations Are Rare: Chimera Training for Logical Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26171)
*Alejandro Ascarate,Leo Lebrat,Rodrigo Santa Cruz,Clinton Fookes,Olivier Salvado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于逻辑规则约束的异常检测方法，通过将约束编译为有向无环图，并引入嵌合体训练策略生成反事实样本，解决了训练中真实规则违反样本稀缺的问题，在多个数据集上提升了异常检测AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际异常不仅是稀有输入，更是语义约束的违反（如对象共现、动作前提、时序关系）。现有方法在训练中真实规则违反样本稀缺的情况下难以有效检测此类异常。

Method: 1) 将逻辑约束编译为DAG，为每个内部逻辑算子学习特征感知的子树MLP门控；2) 通过真实概念标签的布尔传播获得中间监督；3) 提出嵌合体训练：在特征层面拼接不同样本的子树特征，继承原始硬标签并通过逻辑算子生成反事实目标，无需真实异常图像。

Result: 在CLEVRER、OpenImages、VidOR数据集上，该方法在规则级异常AUROC上优于独立事件和同图像语义训练基线，尤其在组合规则和关系规则上提升显著。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效检测语义约束违反型异常，同时输出标量异常分数和规则级归因，无需真实异常样本即可进行反事实训练。

Abstract: Many practical anomalies are not merely rare inputs, but violations of semantic constraints: objects co-occur in structured ways, actions imply preconditions, and events satisfy temporal or relational regularities. We study anomaly detection in this setting, where constraints are given as logical rules over learned visual concepts, but real rule violations are rare or absent during training. We propose a neural rule evaluator that compiles each constraint into a directed acyclic graph and learns feature-aware subtree MLP gates for its internal logical operators. Each gate maps child features and edge-level negations to a parent representation and a rule-satisfaction probability, with intermediate supervision obtained from exact Boolean propagation over ground-truth concept labels. The key difficulty is that same-image training data often provide insufficient coverage of informative truth configurations and also allow shortcut solutions. To address this, we introduce chimera training: an operand-level counterfactual construction at the feature level. Instead of mixing input images, we concatenate subtree features from different samples; each operand keeps the hard truth label of the sample it came from, and the chimera target is obtained by applying the node's logical operator to those inherited labels. This supplies supervised logical counterexamples without requiring real anomalous images. Across CLEVRER, OpenImages, and VidOR, the resulting evaluator improves rule-level anomaly AUROC over independent-events and same-image semantic-training baselines, especially for compositional and relational rules. The method yields both scalar anomaly scores and rule-level attributions.

</details>


### [18] [InfoQuant: Shaping Activation Distributions for Low-Bit LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26175)
*Ke Li,Dong An,Xiaoling Zang,Can Ye,Liang Xie,Qibo Qiu,Chen Shen,Xiaofei He,Wenxiao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: InfoQuant是一种无需训练的低比特激活量化方法，通过峰值抑制正交变换（PSOT）将激活分布塑造成更易于量化的形式，在W4A4KV4设置下平均保留97%的浮点精度，并将LLaMA-2 13B性能差距较之前最先进方法缩小42%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特激活量化是高效大模型部署的主要瓶颈，现有后训练量化方法虽然抑制峰值、平衡通道或最小化重构误差，但很少明确说明什么样的激活分布才易于离散化，导致激活值看似平滑却仍有大量量化误差。

Method: 从信息论角度分析量化误差，提出量化友好的激活应同时具有较小的数值范围和该范围内充分的分散度。基于此设计InfoQuant，采用峰值抑制正交变换（PSOT）塑造激活分布，并引入自适应异常令牌选择以增强优化鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个LLM家族上，InfoQuant一致优于先前PTQ和端到端训练基线。在W4A4KV4设置下平均保留97%的浮点精度，将LLaMA-2 13B性能差距较之前最先进方法缩小42%。

Conclusion: 通过将激活变换重新定义为面向量化器的分布设计，并基于信息论指导的PSOT方法，InfoQuant有效解决了低比特激活量化中分布与量化器不匹配的问题，实现了无需训练的高性能量化。

Abstract: Low-bit activation quantization remains a major bottleneck in efficient large language model (LLM) deployment. The difficulty is not only that activations contain outliers, but that their distributions are often poorly matched to a low-bit uniform quantizer. Existing post-training quantization (PTQ) methods suppress peaks, balance channels, or minimize reconstruction error, yet they rarely specify what activation distribution is actually easy to discretize. As a result, activations may appear numerically smoother while still incurring large quantization error because the quantization range remains wide or most values collapse into a few levels near the mean. We recast activation transformation as quantizer-facing distribution design and analyze quantization error from an information-theoretic perspective. Our analysis shows that quantization-friendly activations should jointly have a smaller numerical range and sufficient dispersion within that range. Guided by this analysis, we propose InfoQuant, a train-free method that employs Peak Suppression Orthogonal Transformation (PSOT) to shape activations into more quantization-friendly distributions. We further introduce adaptive outlier-token selection to improve the robustness of PSOT during optimization. Across multiple LLM families, InfoQuant consistently outperforms prior PTQ and end-to-end training baselines. Under W4A4KV4, it preserves 97% of floating-point accuracy on average and reduces the LLaMA-2 13B performance gap by 42% over the previous state of the art. Code is available at [https://github.com/LLIKKE/InfoQuant](https://github.com/LLIKKE/InfoQuant)

</details>


### [19] [GAC: Noise-Aware Adaptive Mixing for Hybrid SFT-RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26184)
*Yuelin Hu,Zhenbo Yu,Zhengxue Cheng,Wei Liu,Li Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GAC，一种基于梯度方差和信号不一致性在线估计的自适应混合权重控制器，用于改进后训练中监督微调与强化学习的混合策略，在数学、代码、科学和逻辑基准上持续优于固定和规则基线，且训练开销小于1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合后训练通常结合监督微调和强化学习，但固定的混合调度无法适应两种信号相对噪声随时间变化的情况，需要一种噪声感知的自适应控制方法。

Method: 提出GAC（噪声感知控制器），从梯度方差和两种训练信号之间的不一致性的在线估计中推导出自适应混合权重，并加入平滑、先验引导和有界更新，同时复用现有的训练张量。

Result: 在数学、代码、科学和逻辑基准上，GAC持续优于强固定基线和基于规则的基线，在更大模型尺度上收益更显著，训练开销低于1%。

Conclusion: GAC作为一种轻量级的自适应混合策略控制器，能有效提升混合后训练的性能，尤其在大型模型上优势明显，且计算开销极低。

Abstract: Hybrid post-training usually combines supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning, but fixed mixing schedules cannot adapt when the relative noise of the two signals changes over time. We propose GAC, a noise-aware controller that derives an adaptive mixing weight from online estimates of gradient variance and disagreement between the two training signals. The method adds smoothing, prior guidance, and bounded updates while reusing existing training tensors. Experiments on math, code, science, and logic benchmarks show that GAC consistently improves hybrid post-training over strong fixed and rule-based baselines, with larger gains at larger model scales and less than 1% training overhead.

</details>


### [20] [Max-Window Scale Estimation for Near-Lossless HiF8 W8A8 Quantization-Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26189)
*Yingying Cheng,Jinquan Shi,Li Zhou,Zhiyang He,Zhaoyi Sun,Fan Zhang,Jie Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了低比特浮点量化感知训练（HiF8 W8A8 QAT）在OpenPangu-Embedded-1B模型中的两种正交失效模式：amax饱和（延迟缩放估计导致前向剪裁破坏知识敏感表示）和灾难性遗忘（激进学习率覆盖预训练常识知识），并提出了保守的64步历史窗口最大算法DTS策略和500步BF16预热+lr=10^{-5}的QAT训练方案，最终在MMLU、HellaSwag和ARC-Challenge上分别仅下降0.43%、0.58%和0.22%，训练损失APE仅为0.11%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化感知训练（QAT）在低比特浮点格式下部署大语言模型时会引入标准训练指标无法检测的细微失效模式，现有研究缺乏对此类失效模式的系统性识别与解耦分析。

Method: 通过延迟张量缩放（DTS）视角，在OpenPangu-Embedded-1B上开展8组受控实验，解耦出amax饱和与灾难性遗忘两种正交失效模式；针对amax饱和采用64步历史窗口保守最大算法DTS策略，针对灾难性遗忘采用500步BF16预热后以lr=10^{-5}进行QAT训练。

Result: 最终配置在MMLU下降0.43%、HellaSwag下降0.58%、ARC-Challenge下降0.22%，训练损失APE仅0.11%（10,000步），两种修复措施必要且充分。

Conclusion: QAT中的amax饱和和灾难性遗忘是两种独立且无法从训练损失中检测的失效模式，所提修复方案能有效保持模型性能。

Abstract: Quantization-aware training (QAT) with low-bit floating-point formats enables efficient LLM deployment, yet introduces subtle failure modes invisible to standard training metrics. We present a systematic study of HiF8 W8A8 QAT for OpenPangu-Embedded-1B through the lens of Delayed Tensor Scaling (DTS). Across eight controlled experiments, we identify and disentangle two orthogonal failure modes: (i)amax saturation, where delayed scale estimates silently corrupt knowledge-sensitive representations via forward-pass clipping, and (ii)catastrophic forgetting, where an aggressive learning rate overwrites pretrained commonsense knowledge independently of quantization. Neither is detectable from training loss alone. We address amax saturation with a conservative max-algorithm DTS strategy over a 64-step history window, and mitigate forgetting via a 500-step BF16 warmup followed by QAT at lr=10^{-5}. Both fixes are necessary and sufficient: our final configuration achieves 0.43% MMLU drop, 0.58% HellaSwag drop, and 0.22% ARC-Challenge drop versus a matched BF16 baseline, with a training loss APE of only 0.11% over 10,000 steps.

</details>


### [21] [Modeling Dynamic Mixtures of Time-Delay Systems from Streaming Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26191)
*Ren Fujiwara,Yasuko Matsubara,Yasushi Sakurai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DelayMix框架，将流式时间序列视为动态混合的时延系统，通过固定长度的系统摘要张量（基于马尔可夫参数序列）捕获系统动力学和输入-输出延迟，实现快速适应环境变化且内存高效，在真实数据上取得优越预测精度和延迟适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列数据流中存在快速系统变化（如环境因素或输入延迟变化导致的机制转换），使得模型性能下降；同时使用多个小模型来适配不同模式时，面临准确性、鲁棒性和内存使用之间的权衡问题。

Method: 提出在线框架DelayMix，将流式时间序列建模为动态混合的时延系统；通过构造基于系统马尔可夫参数序列的摘要系统张量，以固定长度表示捕获系统动力学和输入-输出延迟；必要时使用张量分解算法从张量中提取相关历史模型，选择最适合当前机制的系统。

Result: 在真实数据集上的测试表明，DelayMix在预测精度和延迟适应速度上持续优于其他对比方法，特别是在高度非平稳的数据上表现突出。

Conclusion: 该方法能够快速适应环境变化且计算高效，通过固定长度表示总结历史机制，在保持模型跟踪鲁棒性的同时减少了内存使用。

Abstract: This research addresses the problem of adaptive modeling in time-series data streams with clear input-output relationships. This problem is challenging because rapid system changes (regime shifts) caused by environmental factors or input delay changes degrade model performance, and the trade-off among accuracy, robustness, and memory usage arises when using multiple small models for each time-series pattern. To address these issues, this paper presents an online framework/method that treats streaming time series as dynamic mixtures of time-delay systems. This framework maintains robustness of model tracking and reduces memory usage by summarizing past regimes using a fixed-length representation that captures both the system dynamics and input-output delays. Concretely, this approach constructs a summary system tensor using the system's Markov parameter series, capturing both dynamic behavior and delay characteristics. If necessary, a tensor decomposition algorithm extracts relevant past models from the tensor and helps select the system that best fits the current regime. This method enables rapid adaptation to environmental changes and is computationally efficient. Tests on real datasets show that DelayMix consistently outperforms other methods, achieving superior forecast accuracy and faster adaptation to delays, especially for highly non-stationary data.

</details>


### [22] [Co-folding model guided by structural proteomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26192)
*Alon Shtrikman,Nitzan Simchi,Michal Ran Shchory,Sagie Brodsky,Eran Seger,Kirill Pevzner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AIMS-Fold是一个推理时引导扩散框架，将交联质谱（XL-MS）和氢氘交换质谱（HDX-MS）等结构蛋白质组学数据整合到预训练扩散模型中，通过可微物理势能引导生成轨迹，显著提升诱导邻近靶标（如抗体和PROTACs）的蛋白质复合物构象预测精度，超越纯计算方法Boltz-2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质结构生成模型能预测静态结构，但无法准确捕捉蛋白质复合物的正确构象状态，这对基于结构的药物设计（如诱导邻近药物）至关重要。结构蛋白质组学技术虽能提供空间和动态信息，但如何将这些稀疏异构测量整合到模型中仍是开放挑战。

Method: 提出AIMS-Fold框架，采用推理时引导扩散策略，在预训练扩散模型的生成过程中，利用来自XL-MS空间约束和HDX-MS溶剂可及性图谱的可微物理势能，主动引导生成采样轨迹。

Result: XL-MS和HDX-MS各自单独提升预测准确性，二者整合产生协同增强效应。在具有挑战性的诱导邻近靶标上，AIMS-Fold优于纯计算的无引导SOTA模型Boltz-2。

Conclusion: AIMS-Fold建立了一种强大的集成计算框架，通过实验约束引导扩散模型，为诱导邻近药物（如PROTACs和抗体）的基于结构的药物设计提供了有效方法。

Abstract: Protein structure generative models excel at predicting single protein static structures from sequence, but routinely fail to capture the correct conformational state of protein complexes, critical for protein design and induced proximity modalities such as antibodies and PROTACs. While structural proteomics techniques like Cross-Linking Mass Spectrometry (XL-MS) and Hydrogen-Deuterium Exchange (HDX-MS) offer valuable spatial and dynamic insights, integrating these sparse, heterogeneous measurements into these models remains an open challenge. Here, we bridge this gap by combining structural proteomics data with the rich biophysical priors learned by pretrained diffusion models. We introduce AIMS-Fold, an inference-time guided-diffusion framework that actively steers the generative sampling trajectory using differentiable physical potentials derived from XL-MS spatial restraints and HDX-MS solvent accessibility profiles. We demonstrate that these structural methods individually enhance predictive accuracy, and their integration yields synergistic improvement. Crucially, by leveraging these experimental restraints, AIMS-Fold achieves higher accuracy on challenging induced proximity targets than purely computational, unguided state-of-the-art models like Boltz-2. This establishes our framework as a powerful, integrative computational approach for the structure based drug design of induced proximity drugs. Evaluation code will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [23] [Bridging Classification and Reconstruction: Cooperative Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26193)
*Qideng Tang,Dai Chaofan,Wubin Ma,Yahui Wu,Haohao Zhou,Tao Zhang,Huan Li,Dalin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoAD框架，通过统一分类范式（Outlier Exposure）和重构范式（Masked Autoencoder）的协同设计，利用分类模块生成概率引导的软掩码辅助重构模块，同时重构模块缓解分类模块的泛化问题，从而有效检测细微和复杂的时间序列异常。实验表明CoAD在多个基准上显著超越现有方法，且轻量高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法在时间序列异常检测中难以检测细微和长期异常；Outlier Exposure方法受限于泛化能力差，Masked Autoencoder方法受限于掩码对齐问题。亟需一种统一两种范式优点的方案。

Method: 提出CoAD框架，包含分类模块和重构模块的协同设计：(1) 分类模块生成概率引导的软掩码，传递给重构模块；(2) 重构模块利用软掩码辅助异常检测，同时缓解分类模块的泛化问题；(3) 分类模块针对分类粒度不当和忽视频率信息的问题进行了专门设计。

Result: 在高质量基准数据集上，采用严格的评估协议，CoAD显著优于现有的深度学习和传统数据挖掘方法；同时模型轻量，推理速度远快于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: CoAD通过协同分类与重构两种范式，有效克服了各自局限，证明了深度学习在时间序列异常检测中的潜力，且具备大规模实时应用的实用价值。

Abstract: Time series anomaly detection (TSAD) has long been a hot research topic in data mining due to its various applications. Recent studies challenge the effectiveness of popular deep learning methods for TSAD, suggesting their failure in detecting subtle and prolonged anomalies. Outlier Exposure (OE) and Masked Autoencoder (MAE) emerge as two promising paradigms (classification and reconstruction) for solving the above problems. However, OE-based methods are constrained by poor generalization, while MAE-based methods are limited by masking misalignment issues. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a novel framework, CoAD, which unifies the two paradigms to leverage their complementary strengths while mitigating their respective weaknesses. In this framework, the classification module generates probability-informed soft masks for the reconstruction module, which in turn alleviates the generalization problem of the classification module. This cooperative design enables CoAD to effectively detect subtle and complex anomalies that are often overlooked by existing methods. Additionally, the classification module is carefully designed to resolve issues related to improper classification granularity and the neglect of frequency information. Extensive experiments on high-quality benchmark datasets, conducted under rigorous evaluation protocols, demonstrate that CoAD significantly outperforms both state-of-the-art deep learning and traditional data mining methods, highlighting the potential of deep learning in TSAD. Moreover, CoAD is lightweight and substantially faster than existing SOTA methods, demonstrating its practical value for large-scale, real-time applications.

</details>


### [24] [On the Role of Inductive Bias in Time-Series Pretraining: A Case Study in Learning Generalizable Representations for Clinical Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26194)
*Sharmita Dey,Diego Paez-Granados*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PathoFM是一个面向脊髓损伤病理步态分析的基础模型，通过三种互补预训练目标（局部重建、时间连续性、无监督上下文动态）在编码器中心Transformer上学习跨任务类型的通用表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床时间序列学习受限于小规模、异质性队列和协议漂移，且下游任务涵盖分类与回归，亟需探究何种预训练归纳偏置能实现跨任务类型和跨主体的表示迁移。

Method: 提出PathoFM框架，采用编码器中心Transformer，在多元步态窗口上联合优化三个互补目标：局部完成（掩码连续跨度重建）、时间连续性（基于观测前缀预测掩码中期延续）和无监督上下文动态（通过注意力机制基于主体示例窗口进行支持-查询重建）。

Result: 动态中心的预训练目标组合产生最均衡的迁移效果：分组/对比目标有利于判别边界但损害连续目标的幅度保真度，纯重建目标保留波形结构但分类性能下降。结合局部重建与时间连续性，并在可获取示例时加入上下文条件约束，可获得鲁棒的主体泛化表示。

Conclusion: 在病理步态分析中，动态中心的多目标预训练策略能够有效平衡分类与回归任务的表示迁移需求，为临床时间序列基础模型的设计提供了实证指导。

Abstract: Clinical time-series learning is routinely constrained by small, heterogeneous cohorts and protocol drift, while its downstream use spans both classification (e.g., pathology diagnosis) and regression (e.g., temporal forecasting). These constraints make foundation-model pretraining appealing, but raises an important question of which inductive biases should the pretraining objective impose so that representations transfer across task types and subjects. We study this question in pathological gait analysis for spinal cord injury (SCI) via PathoFM, an encoder-centric transformer pretrained on multivariate gait windows with three complementary objectives: Local Completion (reconstruct contiguous masked spans to enforce local structure), Temporal Continuity (predict a masked mid-horizon continuation from an observed prefix to enforce smoothness and causal consistency), and Unsupervised In-Context Dynamics (support-query reconstruction conditioned on subject exemplar windows via attention). Empirically comparing objective families (grouping/contrastive, dynamics-based, and generative reconstruction), we find that dynamics-centric mixtures produce the most balanced transfer: grouping objectives favor discriminative margins but can degrade magnitude fidelity needed for continuous targets, whereas reconstruction-only objectives preserve waveform structure but may underperform on classification. Overall, combining local reconstruction with temporal continuity, and adding in-context conditioning when exemplar access is realistic, yields robust subject-generalizing representations.

</details>


### [25] [The Bridge-Garden Dilemma in LLM Distillation: Why Mixing Hard and Soft Labels Works](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26246)
*Guanghui Wang,Kaiwen Lv Kacuila,Zhiyong Yang,Zitai Wang,Jin-Wen Wu,Longtao Huang,Qianqian Xu,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Bridge-Garden分解理论，将生成步骤分为需精确匹配的"桥梁"和可灵活选择的"花园"，并据此开发混合硬/软标签的知识蒸馏方法，在降低训练成本9.7倍的同时优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识蒸馏中软标签理论上更丰富，但混合硬软标签效果更好，作者希望解释这一现象背后的机理，并据此设计更优的蒸馏策略。

Method: 提出Bridge-Garden分解理论：Bridges步骤需精确匹配（适合硬标签），Gardens步骤可灵活选择（适合软标签）。基于此开发自适应混合硬软标签的Bridge-Garden混合监督方法。

Result: 在Qwen、Llama、Gemma、DeepSeek等7组师生模型对上的推理和编程基准测试中，优于基于散度和on-policy的KD基线，同时训练成本降低9.7倍。

Conclusion: 混合硬软标签效果提升的原因在于减少exposure bias，而非更接近教师分布；Bridge-Garden理论为高效知识蒸馏提供了新的理论指导和实用方法。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) transfers knowledge from a large teacher model to a smaller student. In language modeling, the student is trained either on tokens sampled from the teacher (hard labels) or the teacher's full next-token distribution (soft labels). Despite soft labels appear strictly richer, we find that mixing hard and soft labels consistently yields better results. Crucially, we show that this gain cannot be explained by closer teacher matching during training. Instead, it comes from reduced exposure bias, the mismatch between training and inference distributions. To explain this phenomenon, we introduce the Bridge-Garden Decomposition theory, which categorizes generation steps into two types: Bridges, where the next token must be exact, and Gardens, where it can be flexible. We show that hard-only KD excels in Bridges by avoiding risky deviations, while soft-only KD preserves diversity in Gardens. A hybrid strategy handles both cases and, as a result, reduces exposure bias across the sequence. Guided by this theory, we develop a family of Bridge-Garden hybrid supervision methods that adaptively balance hard and soft labels. Across a primary suite of seven teacher-student pairs (including Qwen, Llama, Gemma, and DeepSeek) and benchmarks in reasoning and coding, our approach outperforms divergence-based and on-policy KD baselines while reducing training cost by 9.7x, enabling efficient model compression. Code is available at https://github.com/ghwang-s/bridge_garden_hybrid_kd_release.

</details>


### [26] [Quantized Keys Steal Attention: Bias Correction for KV-Cache Compression in Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26266)
*Tuna Tuncer,Felix Becker,Thomas Pfeil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对视频扩散模型中KV缓存量化导致注意力权重偏差（Jensen bias）的问题，提出一种基于泰勒展开的逐注意力分数修正方法，在INT2量化下恢复近BF16视频质量，且计算开销可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频生成中KV缓存成为内存瓶颈，量化虽能减少内存占用但会严重降低视频质量，需要一种既能压缩缓存又不损害生成质量的方法。

Method: 识别出量化噪声通过softmax指数函数的凸性导致已缓存键的注意力权重系统性膨胀（Jensen bias）；推导出基于量化步长和查询范数的逐注意力分数修正公式，利用二阶泰勒近似实现计算开销可忽略的在线修正。

Result: 在MAGI-1、SkyReels-V2、HY-WorldPlay上使用INT2量化，该方法恢复大部分质量损失，接近BF16水平；性能超过INT4量化且节省50%内存。

Conclusion: 所提出的无额外内存开销的在线修正方法能有效消除量化引起的注意力偏差，使极低比特量化在视频扩散模型中变得实用。

Abstract: Chunk-wise autoregressive video diffusion models rely on a KV cache of previously generated chunks to avoid redundant computation, but this cache quickly becomes a memory bottleneck as videos grow longer. Methods that quantize the KV cache to low bitwidths reduce memory pressure but degrade video quality. We show that a key driver of this degradation is a systematic bias in attention weights: due to the convexity of the exponential in softmax attention, quantization noise inflates the contribution of cached keys, a phenomenon we call the Jensen bias. This effect causes quantized keys to steal attention mass from the unquantized current chunk. We derive a per-attention-score correction that removes this bias in expectation, computed on the fly from the quantization step sizes of the cached keys and the query norm. Using a second-order Taylor approximation, the additional computational overhead is negligible, and no additional memory is needed alongside the cache. Evaluated on MAGI-1, SkyReels-V2, and HY-WorldPlay at INT2 quantization, our correction recovers most of the quality lost to aggressive quantization, reaching near-BF16 video quality, and can outperform INT4 quantization while using 50% less memory.

</details>


### [27] [Scaling World-Model Reinforcement Learning Through Diffusion Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26282)
*Xiaoyuan Cheng,Wenxuan Yuan,Zhancun Mu,Yuanzhao Zhang,Yiming Yang,Hai Wang,Zhuo Sun,Che Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出模型基扩散策略优化(MBDPO)框架，通过扩散策略表示统一世界模型中的搜索与策略优化，解决搜索与价值学习之间的结构错位问题，在多种设置下表现优异且具有良好缩放特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于世界模型的强化学习方法存在模型偏差和误差累积问题，更重要的是存在搜索与价值学习之间的结构错位——策略改进依赖于非搜索策略诱导的价值函数，导致训练不一致和次优学习。

Method: MBDPO将策略优化重新表述为在潜在世界模型中对搜索轨迹的扩散过程，从收集的数据集中提取隐式能量函数来锚定策略，通过细化分数场进行策略优化，同时缓解结构错位问题。

Result: 在多任务离线预训练、在线学习、离线到在线微调等多种设置下评估，发现随着模型容量增加，性能呈现一致且单调的提升。

Conclusion: 通过扩散策略表示统一搜索与策略优化，MBDPO有效解锁了世界模型的可扩展策略学习潜力，结构错位是比模型偏差更关键的瓶颈。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (RL) can be effectively supported at scale through the use of world models. However, in practice, scaling such approaches remains fundamentally limited. A commonly recognized challenge is model bias and error compounding, which degrade long-horizon predictions. Beyond these issues, we identify a more critical yet underexplored bottleneck: a structural misalignment between search and value learning in existing world model approaches. In particular, policy improvement often relies on value functions induced by a separate, non-search policy, resulting in training inconsistency and ultimately suboptimal learning. To address this limitation, we propose Model-Based Diffusion Policy Optimization (MBDPO) in world models, a framework that unifies search and policy optimization through diffusion policy representations, thereby unlocking the potential of world models for scalable policy learning. Instead of constructing an explicit planner over a learned world model, we reformulate policy optimization as a diffusion process over searched trajectories in latent world models. In this view, we extract an implicit energy function from the collected dataset that anchors the policy, enabling MBDPO to refine the score field for policy optimization while mitigating misalignment. We evaluate MBDPO across a wide range of settings, including multi-task offline pretraining, online learning, and offline-to-online fine-tuning. In the offline regime, we further investigate its scaling behavior by pretraining on large-scale datasets, observing consistent and monotonic performance gains with increasing model capacity.

</details>


### [28] [Two-Parameter Flows for Learning Population Dynamics of Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26285)
*Paul Schwerdtner,Tobias Blickhan,Benjamin Peherstorfer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从无标签、无轨迹信息的样本中学习高维概率密度随时间演化的方法，通过双参数流先学习从基分布到各边缘分布的采样时间输运，再对合成轨迹回归得到物理时间速度，并证明了动力学唯一性与正则性继承，可扩展到高维并处理旋转/循环等非梯度动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在学习高维概率密度演化时往往需要轨迹信息或每步最优输运耦合，难以扩展到高维且无法处理非梯度动力学（如旋转、循环现象）。本文旨在提出一种无需轨迹信息、可扩展至高维、同时支持非梯度动力学的方法。

Method: 引入双参数流：1) 利用条件流匹配技术学习从基分布到各边缘分布的采样时间输运；2) 在合成耦合轨迹上通过回归提取物理时间速度。理论证明该物理时间动力学是唯一的且继承了采样时间输运的正则性。

Result: 证明了所提动力学唯一性及正则性继承性质；方法可扩展到高维；无需每步最优输运耦合；能够解释旋转或循环等非梯度物理现象。

Conclusion: 本文通过双参数流框架有效解决了高维概率密度动力学学习问题，在无需轨迹信息和每步最优输运的前提下，支持非梯度动力学，且具备良好的理论保证和可扩展性。

Abstract: This work addresses the problem of learning the dynamics of high-dimensional probability densities over time using unlabeled samples, without assuming access to trajectory information. We introduce two-parameter flows that learn only sampling-time transports from a base distribution to each marginal and then extract a physics-time velocity by regressing on coupled synthetic trajectories. We prove that the resulting physics-time dynamics are unique and inherit regularity from the sampling-time transports. Because we can build on standard, well-developed conditional flow matching techniques for learning the base-to-marginal transports, our approach scales to high dimensions and avoids per-step optimal-transport couplings, while allowing admissible non-gradient dynamics that can naturally explain rotational or circulating physics phenomena.

</details>


### [29] [Stateful Inference for Low-Latency Multi-Agent Tool Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26289)
*Victor Norgren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种有状态推理架构，通过持久化KV缓存和前缀缓存机制，将多智能体工具调用场景中每轮对话的计算成本从O(n_t)降低到O(Δ_t)，仅处理新增token，在6轮工作流中加速2.1倍，35轮工作流中位轮次加速4.2倍，端到端时间减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理框架将每次工具调用视为独立请求，重复处理整个对话历史（实际85-95%的prompt与上一轮相同），导致巨大计算浪费。

Method: 1) 持久化KV缓存跨轮次存活，仅消费新增token实现增量计算；2) 基数前缀缓存扩展至交错的多智能体流量；3) 提示查找投机解码加速结构化输出。

Result: 相比vLLM和SGLang，6轮agent工作流每轮加速2.1倍，35轮工作流中位轮次加速4.2倍，端到端耗时减半。优势来自有状态复用和投机解码，而非传统缓存。

Conclusion: 有状态推理架构能显著降低多轮多智能体工具调用场景的计算成本，通过增量处理和有状态复用实现高效推理。

Abstract: Multi-agent tool calling is becoming the dominant interaction pattern for LLM-based systems, yet existing inference frameworks treat each tool call as an independent request, re-processing the entire conversation from scratch even though 85-95% of the prompt is unchanged from the previous turn.
  We present a stateful inference architecture that converts the $O(n_t)$ per-turn cost of conventional serving into an $O(Δ_t)$ delta-only cost: a persistent KV cache lives across turns and advances by ingesting only the new tokens, while a radix prefix cache extends this across interleaved multi-agent traffic and a prompt-lookup speculative decoder accelerates structured output. Against vLLM and SGLang on novel, fully-generated workloads, the reference implementation is $2.1\times$ faster per turn on a 6-turn agentic workflow and $4.2\times$ on the median turn of a 35-turn one, halving end-to-end wall time. The advantage comes from stateful reuse and speculation, not caching.

</details>


### [30] [Curriculum Learning for Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26315)
*Sandeep Kumar,Virginia Smith,Chhavi Yadav*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Staged-Competence课程学习框架，通过难度分级的偏好数据、能力基采样和渐进式参考模型更新，显著提升DPO安全对齐的鲁棒性和OOD泛化能力，平均降低16%有害响应率和20%越狱攻击成功率，且仅需75%数据即可匹配基线安全性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DPO方法在安全对齐中脆弱且OOD泛化能力差，难以应对分布外有害输入和越狱攻击，亟需提升其鲁棒性。

Method: 提出Staged-Competence课程学习框架，包括：(1) 按难度对偏好数据分级；(2) 基于模型当前能力进行采样；(3) 训练过程中渐进式更新参考模型。该框架与策略优化损失无关，可扩展至其他DPO变体。

Result: 跨三个模型家族平均OOD有害响应率降低16%，越狱攻击成功率降低20%，保持通用能力且近零过度拒答；仅用75%训练数据即可匹配基线安全性能；更好分离安全与不安全响应。

Conclusion: 课程学习策略能有效增强DPO安全对齐的鲁棒性，且框架具有通用性，可推广至其他DPO变体和对齐领域。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimisation (DPO) is widely used for safety alignment in large language models. However, prior work shows it is brittle and exhibits poor out-of-distribution (OOD) generalisation. In this paper, we investigate whether Curriculum Learning can improve the robustness of DPO-based safety alignment. We propose Staged-Competence, a curriculum-based framework that organises preference data by difficulty, employs competence-based sampling, and progressively updates the reference model during training. Averaged across three model families, Staged-Competence reduces OOD harmful response rates by 16% and jailbreak attack success rates by 20%, while preserving general capabilities with near-zero over-refusal. We further show that Staged-Competence (1) matches baseline safety with only 75% of the training data and (2) yields better separation between safe and unsafe responses. Staged-Competence is agnostic to the policy optimisation loss and can extend to other DPO variants and alignment domains. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/Sandeep5500/curriculum-learning-for-safety.

</details>


### [31] [Trust Region Q Adjoint Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27079)
*Yonghoon Dong,Kyungmin Lee,Changyeon Kim,Jaehyuk Kim,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TRQAM，一种通过投影对偶下降自适应控制预训练流策略路径空间KL散度的稳定离策略微调算法，在50个OGBench任务中，离线RL成功率68%，远超最强基线46%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离策略强化学习预训练流策略时，由于多步采样过程导致优化不稳定；现有QAM方法虽通过无记忆随机最优控制问题缓解了该问题，但批评家引导的改进存在固有脆弱性：当批评家条件不佳时，微小误差会被放大，导致模型崩溃。

Method: 提出信任区域Q-伴随匹配（TRQAM），通过投影对偶下降自适应控制预训练流策略的路径空间KL散度。具体地，优化SOC动力学中的信任区域参数λ，并理论证明路径空间KL散度可由λ的闭式函数表示，从而精确控制与预训练流策略的偏差。

Result: 在50个OGBench任务上，TRQAM在离线RL和离线到在线RL中均持续优于现有方法。离线RL总体成功率达68%，大幅提升最强基线（46%）22个百分点。

Conclusion: TRQAM通过自适应信任区域机制实现了对预训练流策略偏差的精确控制，解决了批评家引导改进中的模型崩溃问题，为离策略强化学习预训练流策略提供了稳定有效的微调方案。

Abstract: Off-policy reinforcement learning of pretrained flow policies remains challenging due to the instability of optimization arising from the multi-step sampling process. Recently, Q-learning with Adjoint Matching (QAM) addressed this issue by reformulating into a memoryless stochastic optimal control (SOC) problem with a learned critic. However, QAM inherits a fundamental fragility of critic-guided improvement: small critic errors are amplified when critics are ill-conditioned, often leading to model collapse. This paper introduces Trust Region Q-Adjoint Matching (TRQAM), a stable off-policy fine-tuning algorithm that adaptively controls the path-space KL with pretrained flow policies through projected dual descent. Specifically, we optimize the trust-region parameter $λ$ in SOC dynamics, and theoretically show that the path-space KL can be represented by a closed-form function of $λ$. As a result, our method can precisely control the exact deviation from pretrained flow policies, achieving stable off-policy RL. Through experiments on 50 OGBench tasks, TRQAM consistently outperforms prior arts in both offline RL and offline-to-online RL. In particular, TRQAM achieves an overall success rate of 68% in offline RL, substantially improves the strongest baseline at 46%.

</details>


### [32] [Semigroup Consistency as a Diagnostic for Learned Physics Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26324)
*Lennon J. Shikhman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出归一化半群误差（Normalized Semigroup Error）作为评估学习型物理模拟器长期推演质量的诊断指标，通过比较直接推演与组合推演的一致性来检测时间组合中的失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估指标（一步或短时预测误差）无法捕捉学习型物理模拟器在长时间推演和时间组合中的失效模式，亟需一种能反映长期推演质量的诊断方法。

Method: 利用自治、状态完备系统的半群性质（解映射满足 $s+t$ 步直接演化应与先 $s$ 步再 $t$ 步组合演化一致），定义归一化半群误差作为与模型无关的后验诊断指标，并在一维热方程和Burgers动力学上使用时间条件化ConvNet和FNO基线进行验证。

Result: 半群误差与推演退化呈正相关，轨迹级Spearman相关系数 $\rho=0.635$，95%置信区间 $[0.621, 0.649]$；半群正则化的效果不一，表明其主要价值在于评估诊断而非作为普适有效的训练目标。

Conclusion: 归一化半群误差是一种有效的长期推演质量诊断工具，但作为训练正则化项效果不稳定，更适合作为评估指标使用。

Abstract: Learned physics simulators are often evaluated by one-step or short-horizon prediction error, but these metrics can miss failures in temporal composition and long-horizon rollout. For autonomous, state-complete systems, exact solution maps satisfy a semigroup law: direct evolution over $s+t$ should agree with evolution over $s$ followed by $t$. We propose normalized semigroup error as a post hoc, model-agnostic diagnostic comparing these direct and composed learned predictions. On one-dimensional heat and Burgers dynamics with time-conditioned ConvNet and FNO baselines, semigroup error is positively associated with rollout degradation, with trajectory-level Spearman correlation $ρ= 0.635$ and $95%$ CI $[0.621, 0.649]$. Semigroup regularization has mixed effects, supporting semigroup consistency primarily as an evaluation diagnostic rather than a universally beneficial training objective.

</details>


### [33] [Reparametrizing Shampoo and SOAP for Subspace Basis Updates and BFloat16 Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26327)
*Alan Milligan,Zikun Xu,Simon Lacoste-Julien,Felix Dangel,Wu Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种预条件子的重参数化方法，支持BFP16存储并通过子空间部分QR分解更新基向量，从而降低Shampoo类方法（KL-Shampoo、SOAP、KL-SOAP）的计算与内存开销，同时缓解BFP16存储带来的性能下降，使KL-SOAP在BFP16下达到或超越KL-Shampoo的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Shampoo类方法依赖QR分解，需要FP32精度且计算昂贵，导致大预条件矩阵时时间和内存开销大；使用BFP16存储虽可减少内存但会损害性能。

Method: 提出预条件子的重参数化，支持BFP16存储；通过将更新后的基向量与未改变的基向量组合形成完整基；仅在子空间中对部分基进行QR分解更新，降低计算开销并缓解BFP16存储导致的性能退化。

Result: 在SOAP和KL-SOAP上验证了BFP16存储下的性能提升；KL-SOAP在BFP16下达到或超越KL-Shampoo；整体上使Shampoo类方法更内存和时间高效。

Conclusion: 该方法可广泛适用于使用QR分解的Shampoo类优化器，有效平衡了内存效率与训练性能。

Abstract: Shampoo-based methods, such as KL-Shampoo and SOAP, have demonstrated strong performance in training neural networks and rely on QR decomposition. Because existing QR implementations require single-precision (FP32) arithmetic and remain computationally expensive, these methods become time- and memory-intensive when their preconditioning matrices are large. Moreover, using BFloat16 (BFP16) storage to reduce memory usage can degrade the performance of Shampoo-based methods. We propose a reparametrization of the preconditioner that supports BFP16 storage and forms a complete basis by combining updated basis vectors with unchanged ones. By updating only part of the basis through QR decomposition in a subspace, our approach reduces computational overhead while mitigating the performance degradation caused by BFP16 storage. Our approach applies broadly to Shampoo-based methods that employ QR decomposition, including KL-Shampoo, SOAP, and KL-SOAP. In particular, it improves the performance of SOAP and KL-SOAP under BFP16 storage, enabling KL-SOAP to match or exceed KL-Shampoo. Overall, our approach makes Shampoo-based methods more memory- and time-efficient.

</details>


### [34] [QAM-W: Joint 2D Codebook Quantization for LLM Weights via Hadamard Rotation and Activation-Aware Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26339)
*Preetam Sharma,Kacper Dobek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QAM-W是一种新的权重量化编解码器，通过对权重行进行L2归一化、块哈达玛旋转、配对为2D坐标并用量子化的Lloyd-Max码本编码，恢复标量量化丢失的坐标结构。在5.5 bpw下，跨5个LLM的困惑度偏离BF16不超过±0.4%，比特数比SmoothQuant W8A8减少32%。联合2D编码优于极性编码，KL散度与困惑度变化高度相关。主要贡献在5-6 bpw高质量量化区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量后训练量化器丢弃了权重行内成对坐标的结构信息，导致量化精度损失。作者希望恢复这种结构信息，在保持量化精度的同时进一步降低位宽。

Method: 对每行权重进行L2归一化→块哈达玛旋转→配对为2D坐标→用训练在单位圆高斯上的单一Lloyd-Max码本量化→激活感知的逐通道缩放。联合2D编码替代标量或极性(幅值×相位)编码。

Result: 在5个LLM(1.1B-13B)上，5.5 bpw版本困惑度偏离BF16不超过±0.4%，比特数比SmoothQuant W8A8少32%。联合2D编码比极性编码困惑度改善2-15个百分点。KL散度与困惑度变化Spearman ρ=0.99。

Conclusion: QAM-W在5-6 bpw区间实现了质量保持的量化，是该位宽下的高质量方案。3.5 bpw版本在量化容忍架构上有竞争力，但严格4 bpw下不及QTIP。

Abstract: Scalar post-training quantizers discard pairwise coordinate structure within weight rows. We introduce QAM-W (Quadrature Amplitude Modulation for Weights), a codec that recovers this structure: each row is L2-normalized, block-Hadamard rotated, paired into 2D coordinates, and quantized against a single Lloyd-Max codebook trained on the unit circular Gaussian, with activation-aware per-channel scaling. In a cross-model study spanning five LLMs from four families (1.1B--13B parameters) and eight quantized configurations, the activation-aware variant at $\approx 5.5$ bpw stays within $\pm 0.4\%$ of BF16 WikiText-2 perplexity on every model, matching the SmoothQuant W8A8 quality envelope at $32\%$ fewer weight bits. Joint 2D coding outperforms polar (amplitude $\times$ phase) coding by 2--15~pp $Δ$PPL at equal bitrate, and paired KL against BF16 tracks $Δ$PPL\% at Spearman $ρ= 0.99$ across 37 (method, model) rows, consistent with a monotone composite bound from codec distortion to KL divergence. A 3.5~bpw variant is competitive on quantization-tolerant architectures. At strict 4~bpw, the rotated-codebook frontier method QTIP outperforms QAM-W; the contribution is the quality-preserving 5--6~bpw band.

</details>


### [35] [A PAC-Bayesian View of Generalisation for Physics-Informed Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26341)
*Thien V. Nguyen,Amaury Habrard,Benjamin Guedj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个PAC-Bayesian框架，为物理信息机器学习（PIML）在无界损失下的泛化提供高概率保证。通过多任务视角联合处理数据保真度、PDE残差、初始和边界条件，推导出复杂度与输入梯度范数相关的泛化界，并提出了可优化的自界感知学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PIML在实证上表现优异，但其统计泛化性质（特别是在无界损失下的回归设定中）仍缺乏充分理解。现有分析依赖于近似或稳定性论证，未能完全揭示物理结构如何从有限数据中影响泛化。

Method: 基于PAC-Bayesian理论框架，采用多任务视角联合建模数据保真度、PDE残差、初始及边界条件；利用Sobolev和Poincaré型假设导出两类泛化界；提出自界感知学习算法，优化推导界的可计算替代形式。

Result: 在标准PDE基准上的实验表明，所提界非平凡、显著紧于联合界基线，且可在训练中有效优化。复杂度与输入梯度范数相关，揭示了物理正则性与泛化之间的直接联系。

Conclusion: 为物理信息模型的泛化提供了原则性的统计基础，证明了物理结构（如梯度平滑性）对泛化保证的关键作用。

Abstract: Physics-informed machine learning (PIML) integrates mechanistic knowledge, typically in the form of partial differential equations (PDE), into data-driven models. Despite strong empirical performance, its statistical generalisation properties remain poorly understood, particularly in the regression setting with unbounded losses. Existing analyses rely on approximation or stability arguments and do not fully capture how physical structure influences generalisation from finite data. In this work, we develop a PAC-Bayesian framework for PIML that provides high-probability generalisation guarantees in the presence of unbounded losses. We adopt a multi-task perspective that jointly treats data fidelity, PDE residuals, initial and boundary conditions, avoiding the looseness induced by standard union-bound approaches. Our analysis leverages the structure of physics-informed objectives to derive novel bounds where the complexity scales with input-gradient norms of the losses, revealing a direct link between physical regularity and generalisation. We instantiate this framework under Sobolev and Poincaré-type assumptions, yielding two classes of bounds that trade off statistical complexity and smoothness in different regimes. Building on these results, we propose a self-bounding-aware learning algorithm that directly optimises tractable surrogates of the derived bounds, along with a practical procedure to estimate the associated constants in realistic settings. Empirical evaluations on standard PDE benchmarks demonstrate that our bounds are non-vacuous, significantly tighter than union-bound baselines, and can be effectively minimised during training. Overall, our results provide a principled statistical foundation for the generalisation of physics-informed models.

</details>

*Kevin Kuo,Chhavi Yadav,Virginia Smith*

TL;DR: 本文发现针对开源大语言模型的防御措施容易被abliteration和prefilling这两种低成本、非梯度攻击绕过，攻击成功率从10%以下提升至16%-96%；为此提出abliteration-resistant tuning (ART)方法，可降低10%-20%的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御措施假设新有害行为只能通过微调习得，而忽略了通过越狱（jailbreaking）直接诱导预训练模型中已编码的有害知识。本文旨在验证无需微调即可绕过开源模型安全防御的可能性。

Result: 两种攻击将受保护模型攻击成功率从10%以下提升至16%-96%；ART可将abliteration、prefilling及其组合的攻击成功率降低10%-20%。

Conclusion: 开源模型的攻击面比此前描述更为广泛，对安全防御的评估应纳入更多样化的攻击策略（而不仅限于对抗性微调）。

Abstract: Recent defenses for safeguarding open-weight large language models (LLMs) are intended to prevent adversarial usage. Underlying these defenses is an assumption that new harmful behavior is learned through fine-tuning rather than elicited by jailbreaking the model. Yet, pretrained LLMs already encode substantial harmful knowledge across many domains, which raises an important question: can an adversary jailbreak safeguarded models, to achieve harmful usage without fine-tuning at all? In this paper, we show that open-weight safeguards are susceptible to simpler strategies that, despite being well known, have not been systematically evaluated against these safeguards. Specifically, we evaluate two low-cost attacks--abliteration and prefilling--that do not rely on gradient-based optimization. Across three harmfulness evaluation benchmarks (BeaverTails, HarmBench, and AdvBench), these attacks increase attack success rates against safeguarded open-weight models from below 10\% to a range of 16%-96%. To mitigate this vulnerability, we introduce abliteration-resistant tuning (ART), which incorporates an abliteration-based objective into training. ART can be layered onto existing defenses and reduces the success rates of abliteration, prefilling, and their combination by 10%-20%. These findings indicate that the attack surface for open-weight models is broader than previously characterized, and that evaluations of safeguarding defenses should incorporate a more diverse set of attack strategies beyond adversarial fine-tuning.

</details>

*Barsat Khadka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将电路发现重构为强化学习问题，一个PPO智能体在GPT-2 small的144个注意力头上操作，通过在两个训练任务（induction和IOI）上训练，不仅能解决训练任务，还能在未见过的第三个任务（docstring completion）上通过最佳五选一规划恢复96%的oracle上限，且其选中的头与已有文献中因果非冗余的头高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机械可解释性电路发现方法需要为每个新任务定制分析流程，缺乏通用性和可迁移性，因此希望开发一种无需手工设计、可跨任务迁移的自动化电路发现方法。

Method: 将电路发现建模为强化学习问题：智能体在GPT-2 small的144个注意力头的离散动作空间上操作，每个动作触发一次零消融和对比奖励（目标任务的损伤减去通用预测的损伤）。使用单个PPO策略在向量化多任务环境上对两个任务（induction和IOI）进行训练。

Result: 训练后的智能体在两个训练任务上均达到每episode的oracle水平；在未见过的第三个任务（docstring completion）上，最佳五选一规划恢复96%的oracle上限。智能体选中的头与已有文献中识别为因果非冗余的头高度一致，而文献中标记为冗余的类别则被智能体正确低优先级处理。

Conclusion: 强化学习结合因果干预是识别机械可解释性电路中单头瓶颈的可行且可迁移的方法，与现有的路径修补方法互补。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability has identified small sets of attention heads that implement specific behaviours in transformer language models, but recovering these circuits typically requires a bespoke analytical pipeline for each new task. We recast circuit discovery as a reinforcement-learning problem. An agent operates over the 144 attention heads of GPT-2 small as a discrete action space; each action triggers a zero-ablation and a contrastive reward that subtracts the ablation's damage to general next-token prediction from its damage to the target task. A single PPO policy, trained on two tasks (induction and IOI) in a vectorised multi-task environment, attains the per-episode oracle on both training tasks and on a held-out third task (docstring completion). Its preferred heads coincide with the canonical heads of established literature on precisely the axes those papers identify as causally non-redundant under single-head ablation; the categories they identify as redundant are correctly de-prioritised by the agent. On the held-out task, best-of-five planning recovers 96\% of the oracle ceiling with no task signal supplied at evaluation. These results indicate that reinforcement learning over causal interventions is a viable, transferable substrate for identifying the single-head bottlenecks of mechanistic circuits, complementary to existing path-patching approaches.

</details>


### [38] [When Correct Demonstrations Hurt: Rethinking the Role of Exemplars in In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26350)
