While these optimizers can be effective, their development is challenging, limiting the exploration of interactions between different optimization strategies and restricts experts from continuously improving the optimizers -- such as by incorporating additional custom or general pattern-based equivalences over time.
  We present a programmable pattern matching query optimizer called Geo, which automatically manages the interactions between various equivalences, ensures the optimizations maintain correctness of results, and simplifies the management of substructure equivalences. Geo exposes a simple but flexible language for expressing pattern equivalences as rewrite rules. By maintaining canonical representations of generated patterns during equality saturation, Geo avoids issues arising from syntactic differences in isomorphic patterns. Additionally, we develop embedded reconstructablility (EmRec) that tracks provenance across equivalences to ensure various reconstructability needs of desired outputs. Our evaluation demonstrates that Geo can discover novel query equivalences through complex composition of various rewrite rules, enabling our optimized queries to achieve a cost reduction of up to 99% compared to the queries in prior work. We further test Geo's effectiveness at speeding up practical graph mining problems by using it in two representative case studies -- approximate pattern matching and quasi-clique mining, and find it is highly effective at optimizing these tasks, enabling cost reductions of up to 71%.

</details>


### [112] [Pacing Types for Asynchronous Stream Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26635)
*Florian Kohn,Arthur Correnson,Jan Baumeister,Bernd Finkbeiner*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为pacing types的类型系统，用于RTLola流式监控框架，以检测并防止异步数据流中由于用户指定的同步注释导致的时间不一致性问题，并给出了形式化语义和机器验证的可靠性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式监控中数据源异步到达导致同步困难，RTLola允许用户通过pacing注释精细指定数据同步策略，但用户可能写出不一致的策略（明确要求同步却无法实现），需要一种机制来自动检测此类时序不一致性。

Method: 提出pacing types类型系统，为RTLola核心片段中pacing注释赋予形式化语义，并给出该类型系统的可靠性证明，同时使用Rocq证明助手对可靠性证明进行机器检查。

Result: 实现了pacing types类型系统，能够确保异步流监控器不存在时序不一致性，且在Rocq中完成了可靠性证明的机器验证。

Conclusion: Pacing types为流式监控中的时序一致性提供了形式化保证，提高了RTLola框架的可靠性，机器验证进一步增强了可信度。

Abstract: Stream-based monitoring is a runtime verification approach where a monitor aggregates streams of input data from sensors and other sources to give real-time statistics and assessments of a system's health. One of the central challenges in designing reliable stream-based monitors is to deal with the asynchronous nature of data streams: in concrete applications, the different sensors being monitored produce values at different speeds, and it is the monitor's responsibility to correctly react to the asynchronous arrival of different streams of values. To ease this process, modern frameworks for stream-based monitoring such as RTLola enable users to finely specify data synchronization policies via a system of pacing annotations. While this feature simplifies the design of monitors, it can also lead users to write inconsistent policies, where synchronization between two streams is explicitly requested via annotations, but cannot always be achieved. To mitigate this issue, this paper presents pacing types, a novel type system implemented in RTLola to ensure that monitors for asynchronous streams are free of timing inconsistencies. We give a formal semantics to pacing annotations for a core fragment of RTLola, and present a soundness proof of the pacing type system. For an additional level of guarantees, we machine-checked the soundness proof using the Rocq proof assistant.

</details>


### [113] [ProDebug: An Automated Debugging System for Prolog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27124)
*Ricardo Brancas,Vasco Manquinho,Ruben Martins*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出了ProDebug工具，首次将大语言模型与频谱和变异分析技术相结合，用于自动调试Prolog编程作业。该工具能自动识别学生Git提交中的错误并提出修复方案，在1499个有错误的学生作业上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Prolog作为声明式编程语言缺乏传统调试机制，学生在学习时遇到困难；大型班级中难以及时提供个性化反馈。

Method: 结合三种故障检测方法（频谱分析、变异分析、LLM推理）和两种修复生成方法（变异技术、LLM），构建自动化调试工具ProDebug。

Result: 在1499个有错误的本科生Prolog作业上进行了评估，验证了LLM增强的自动化反馈系统在声明式编程教育中的扩展潜力。

Conclusion: ProDebug展示了结合大语言模型与传统调试技术自动化支持声明式编程教育的可行性，有助于大规模课堂中提供及时个性化的学生反馈。

Abstract: Prolog is a well-known declarative programming language commonly used in introductory courses on logic and reasoning. However, many students find Prolog challenging because it lacks the familiar debugging mechanisms found in imperative languages. In large classes, this difficulty is exacerbated by the challenge of providing timely and personalized feedback to students.
  In this work, we introduce ProDebug, the first tool to combine Large Language Models (LLMs) with spectrum-based and mutation-based techniques for automated debugging of Prolog assignments. ProDebug automatically identifies faults and proposes bug repairs for student Git submissions. Faults are detected using three approaches--spectrum-based, mutation-based, and LLM reasoning--while repairs are generated using mutation-based techniques and LLMs. Our evaluation on 1499 buggy student submissions from a bachelor's level programming class demonstrates the potential of automated, LLM-augmented feedback systems to scale support for declarative programming education.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [Device Context Protocol: A Compact, Safety-First Architecture for LLM-Driven Control of Constrained Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26159)
*Dongxu Yang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 针对模型上下文协议(MCP)无法覆盖微控制器设备且存在安全隐患的问题，本文提出设备上下文协议(DCP)：典型帧小于50字节，将能力范围检查、类型校验、预演评估和单位即类型作为协议层原语，并引入主机端Bridge拦截异常调用前。参考固件在ESP32上仅27.6 KB闪存/0.6 KB RAM，五项LLM对抗提示测试显示DCP拒绝100%能力升级尝试和78%提示注入尝试，而Raw MCP和IoT-MCP为0-1%，固件开销比OpenAPI 3小三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MCP协议面向内存充裕的软件服务，无法下探到占物理设备绝大多数的微控制器；同时MCP缺乏安全防护机制，无法防范LLM幻觉或提示注入导致的直接硬件控制风险。

Method: 设计极简帧结构(6字节头+CBOR负载+可选16字节HMAC)、将能力范围/类型校验/预演评估/单位即类型内建为协议层原语、引入主机端Bridge作为安全网关，提供Python Bridge、ESP32固件和语言无关一致性测试套件。

Result: 固件体积27.6 KB闪存/0.6 KB RAM；五款LLM对抗测试中，DCP拒绝100%能力升级尝试和78%提示注入尝试，对比Raw MCP和IoT-MCP的0-1%；表达能力等价于格式良好的OpenAPI 3但开销小三个数量级。

Conclusion: DCP填补了MCP向企业SaaS连接演进与物理设备之间的空白层，为LLM控制硬件提供了轻量级且安全的协议基础。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as orchestrators of external tools via the Model Context Protocol (MCP), but MCP is built for software services with megabytes of memory and does not descend to the microcontrollers that dominate the long tail of physical devices. Recent work (IoT-MCP) ports MCP to edge gateways at 74 KB peak memory; this still excludes the smallest commodity MCUs and, critically, does not address the safety problem of giving an unreliable caller (an LLM that may hallucinate or be prompt-injected) direct control of physical hardware. We present the Device Context Protocol (DCP): a sub-50-byte typical frame (6-byte header + CBOR payload + optional 16-byte HMAC), a manifest schema in which capability scoping, range and type checks, dry-run evaluation, and units-as-types are protocol-layer primitives, and a host-side Bridge that rejects malformed or hallucinated calls before any byte reaches the device. Reference firmware measures 27.6 KB flash / 0.6 KB RAM on ESP32; the Python Bridge, ESP32 firmware, and a language-neutral conformance suite are MIT-licensed and public. An empirical study -- 675 tool calls produced by five LLMs across four vendors (DeepSeek, Alibaba, Zhipu, MiniMax) against six categories of adversarial prompts, with the injection category instantiating AgentDojo's attack templates -- shows DCP rejects 100% of capability-escalation attempts and 78% of prompt-injection attempts, versus 0--1% for Raw MCP and IoT-MCP, matching the expressiveness of a well-formed OpenAPI 3 schema at three orders of magnitude less firmware footprint. We position DCP as the missing layer between MCP (which is moving toward enterprise SaaS connectivity) and the physical devices it does not reach.

</details>


### [115] [Birkhoff Decompositions and Photonic Interconnects Wait! Don't Forget the Compute!](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26845)
*Eliezer Amponsah,Vamsi Addanki*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 针对MoE模型中的全互联通信，本文揭示了经典BvN分解在调度中的两大缺陷（非双随机性导致调度气泡、过多匹配导致计算碎片化），提出了一种简单的贪心最大权重分解策略，在减少匹配数量的同时保持大批次大小，显著提升了通信-计算重叠效率并降低了计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可重构光子互连的通信优化方案孤立地看待通信，忽略了通信与后续专家计算之间的交互。MoE模型的all-to-all通信中，dispatch-compute-combine结构对经典BvN调度提出了根本性挑战，需要重新审视电路调度策略。

Method: 提出贪心最大权重分解（greedy max-weight decomposition）策略：在每次迭代中选择权重最大的匹配，限制匹配总数同时保留每个匹配的大批量大小，从而避免BvN分解产生的过多匹配和计算碎片化问题。

Result: 该简单策略显著提升了通信-计算重叠效率，减少了计算开销，性能接近理想的无拥塞全互联通信。

Conclusion: 重新审视MoE的电路调度问题表明，针对MoE通信模式的特殊性设计调度策略比直接套用经典分解方法更为有效，贪心策略在此场景下能够取得接近理想化的性能。

Abstract: The growing demand for efficient communication in distributed training and inference has sparked significant interest in reconfigurable photonic interconnects across both academia and industry. Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models, with their highly skewed communication patterns, present a natural opportunity for such circuit-switched fabrics. However, existing approaches largely optimize communication in isolation, overlooking the interaction between communication and the expert computation that follows.
  In this paper, we revisit circuit scheduling for all-to-all communication in MoE execution. We show that the dispatch--compute--combine structure fundamentally challenges classical scheduling techniques such as Birkhoff--von Neumann (BvN) decomposition. First, MoE communication matrices are rarely doubly stochastic, introducing significant scheduling bubbles in BvN-based schedules. Second, while decomposition enables communication--compute overlap, the excessive number of matchings produced by BvN fragments execution into small batches, leading to severe compute inefficiencies due to fixed execution overheads. Motivated by these observations, we explore a simple greedy max-weight decomposition strategy that bounds the number of matchings while preserving large batch sizes per matching. Despite its simplicity, the approach significantly improves overlap efficiency, reduces compute overheads, and approaches the performance of an ideal congestion-free all-to-all.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [A proof-theoretic approach to abstract interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26591)
*Vijay D'Silva,Alessandra Palmigiano,Apostolos Tzimoulis,Caterina Urban*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于证明论的抽象解释框架，通过将逻辑系统与有限抽象对应起来，生成与抽象格同构的Lindenbaum-Tarski代数，并建立相应的证明规则与公理，涵盖笛卡尔积及非笛卡尔抽象（如八边形）的逻辑结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为抽象解释建立一个系统的证明论基础，将抽象解释与逻辑系统建立形式化关联，从而用逻辑方法分析程序抽象。

Method: 基于抽象的内部逻辑，提出生成逻辑系统的通用程序，使得Lindenbaum-Tarski代数与给定抽象格同构；通过识别具体化映射所保持的逻辑连接词，导出相应证明规则和公理；并推广到笛卡尔积和多变量情形，以及非笛卡尔抽象（如八边形）。

Result: 在适当条件下建立了可靠性和完备性结果；将框架扩展到笛卡尔积和多变量设置；揭示了八边形等非笛卡尔抽象的逻辑结构。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了抽象解释、证明论和代数逻辑之间的新联系，为程序抽象的系统性逻辑分析提供了基础。

Abstract: This paper develops a proof-theoretic framework for abstract interpretation by systematically associating logical systems with finite abstractions. Building on earlier work on the internal logics of abstractions, we propose a general procedure for generating a logic whose Lindenbaum-Tarski algebra is isomorphic to a given abstract lattice. The approach identifies logical connectives preserved by the concretization map and derives corresponding proof rules and axioms. The paper establishes soundness and completeness results under suitable conditions, extends the framework to Cartesian products and multi-variable settings, and investigates the logical structure of non-Cartesian abstractions such as octagons. These observations suggest new connections between abstract interpretation, proof theory, and algebraic logic, providing a foundation for a systematic logical analysis of program abstractions.

</details>


### [117] [Almost Fair Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26698)
*Arthur Correnson,Iona Kuhn,Bernd Finkbeiner*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文针对带Büchi公平性条件的转移系统，提出了一系列"几乎公平模拟"关系，这些关系通过可控的不动点交替简化了传统公平模拟的复杂性，并配备了直观的推理规则用于证明公平迹包含，最后在Rocq证明助手中进行了机械化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统公平模拟关系涉及复杂的归纳与共归纳嵌套，难以用于交互式验证。本文旨在提出更强但更易使用的公平模拟变体，降低其在交互式定理证明中的使用门槛。

Method: 从已知公平模拟技术出发，逐步构建一族针对Büchi公平性条件的"几乎公平模拟"关系，为每种关系设计直观的演绎推理规则，并在Rocq证明助手中机械化实现并证明其可靠性。

Result: 提出了一系列易于使用的几乎公平模拟关系及其对应的演绎系统，并在Rocq中完成了形式化验证和可靠性证明，通过示例展示了其应用。

Conclusion: 通过引入更可控的不动点交替，可以简化公平模拟关系的使用，使其不仅适用于自动化验证，也适用于交互式定理证明中的公平迹包含推理。

Abstract: It is well known that liveness properties cannot be proven using standard simulation arguments. This issue has been mitigated by extending standard notions of simulation for transition systems to fairness-preserving simulations for systems equipped with an additional fairness condition modeling liveness assumptions and/or liveness requirements. In the context of automated verification of finite-state systems, proofs by simulation are an appealing method as there exist efficient algorithms to find a simulation between two systems. However, applications of fair simulation to interactive verification have been much less studied. Perhaps one reason is that the definitions of fair simulation relations typically involve non-trivial nestings of inductive and coinductive relations, making them particularly difficult to use and to reason about. In this paper, we argue that in many cases, stronger notions of fair simulation involving more controlled alternations of fixed points are sufficient. Starting from known fair simulation techniques, we progressively build up a family of almost fair simulation relations for transition systems equipped with a Buechi fairness condition. The simulation relations we present can all be equipped with intuitive reasoning rules, leading to elegant deductive systems to prove fair trace inclusion. We mechanized our simulation relations and their associated deductive systems in the Rocq proof assistant, proved their soundness, and we demonstrate their use through a selection of examples.

</details>


### [118] [From Actions to Obligations: A Deontic Action Model Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26739)
*Giorgio Cignarale*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为道义行动模型逻辑(DAML)的动态模态框架，用于在多智能体系统中对行动义务进行推理。DAML通过引入道义评估机制，基于行动结果的期望道义价值来推导义务，支持规范约束下的多智能体系统中原则性行动选择，并提供了公理化及完备性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的形式化框架难以处理多智能体系统中涉及策略交互和不完全信息的条件和上下文敏感的义务推理，缺乏一个能够结合行动结果合意性和可能性来评估行动并推导义务的动态逻辑框架。

Method: DAML扩展了认知行动模型逻辑(epistemic Action Model Logic)，引入道义评估机制，通过最大化期望道义价值来决定义务的产生，从而在行动逻辑框架内推导义务。

Result: 提供了DAML的公理化系统，并证明了其相对于语义的可靠性和完备性。

Conclusion: DAML是首个利用行动模型逻辑机制推导义务的框架，通过在矿工谜题等多智能体道义场景中的应用展示了其表达能力和实用性。

Abstract: We introduce the Deontic Action Model Logic (DAML), a dynamic modal framework for reasoning about obligations over actions in multi-agent systems. DAML extends the epistemic Action Model Logic by incorporating deontic evaluation mechanisms that assess agents' actions in terms of both the desirability and the likelihood of their outcomes. Obligations arise for those actions that maximize expected deontic value among an agent's available alternatives at a given decision point, yielding a formal account for reasoning about conditional and context-sensitive obligations in settings involving strategic interaction and incomplete information. DAML supports principled action selection in norm-governed multi-agent systems, and is the first such framework to derive these obligations using the action model logic machinery. We provide an axiomatization of the logic and prove soundness and completeness with respect to its semantics. Finally, we demonstrate the expressive power of our framework through applications to the Miners' Puzzle and other multi-agent deontic scenarios.

</details>


### [119] [mstlo: Efficient Online Monitoring of Signal Temporal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26847)
*Andreas Kaag Thomsen,Niels Viggo Stark Madsen,Valdemar Tang Evans,Thomas David Wright,Lukas Esterle,Peter Gorm Larsen*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: mstlo是一个基于Rust的高性能信号时序逻辑在线监控库，提供统一的多语义接口、增量监控算法和嵌入式领域特定语言，在性能上优于现有工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有STL监控工具在处理大时间深度和深层嵌套公式时性能不足，需要一个高效、可扩展的在线监控方案。

Method: 采用Rust实现，提供统一接口支持多种STL语义（RoSI和带早期判决的布尔评估）；基于自底向上动态规划的增量监控算法，包含逐算子缓存和时序算子的流式极值计算；嵌入STL领域特定语言，Rust端使用过程宏实现静态语法检查。

Result: 基准测试表明，mstlo在可扩展性和性能上优于现有工具，尤其在大时间深度和深层嵌套公式场景下表现显著提升。

Conclusion: mstlo通过Rust的高性能特性结合增量算法设计，有效解决了STL在线监控中的性能瓶颈问题。

Abstract: We present mstlo (mistletoe), a Rust library for high-performance online monitoring of signal temporal logic (STL), with Python bindings. The library provides: (i) a unified interface for multiple STL semantics, including Robust Satisfaction Intervals (RoSI) and Boolean evaluation with early verdicts; (ii) an incremental monitoring algorithm based on bottom-up dynamic programming with per-operator caching and streaming extremum computation for temporal operators; and (iii) an embedded STL domain-specific language for both Rust and Python implementations, with procedural macros in Rust for static syntax checking. Benchmarks show scalability and performance improvements over state-of-the-art tools, especially for formulas with large temporal depth and deep nesting.

</details>


### [120] [A Dynamic Deontic Simplicial Logic for Joint Commitments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26883)
*Giorgio Cignarale,Hugo Rincon Galeana*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于单纯复形的道义逻辑DSL及其动态扩展DDSL，用顶点表示个体承诺、高维单纯形表示群体联合义务，并通过更新操作刻画个体与联合行动的效果，证明了静态与动态逻辑的可靠性和完备性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有道义逻辑缺乏对群体联合义务的几何结构表示，且基于单纯复形的逻辑尚未被赋予道义解释。作者旨在填补这一空白，建立一种能够自然刻画个体承诺与群体联合义务关系的几何化道义逻辑框架。

Method: 基于单纯复形构建道义模型：顶点表示个体承诺，高维单纯形表示群体的联合义务；静态逻辑DSL提供道义解释，动态扩展DDSL引入互斥承诺的选择机制，并通过模型更新操作刻画个体与联合行动的效果；分别证明了静态和动态逻辑的可靠性与完备性。

Result: 成功建立了DSL和DDSL两个逻辑系统，证明了各自相对于相应模型类的可靠性和完备性，并通过多个静、动态示例展示了框架的表达力。

Conclusion: 基于单纯复形的道义逻辑能够有效且自然地建模个体承诺与群体联合义务，动态扩展进一步捕捉了行动带来的义务变化，为道义推理提供了新的几何化工具。

Abstract: In this paper we introduce the novel Deontic Simplicial Logic (DSL), a deontic logic for group obligations based on simplicial complexes. We provide the first deontic interpretation of simplicial models in which vertices represent individual commitments and higher-dimensional simplices represent joint obligations of groups of agents. We further extend DSL to the Dynamic Deontic Simplicial Logic (DDSL), resulting in the first dynamic logic based on simplicial complexes. DDSL models agents' choices among mutually exclusive commitments and captures the effects of individual and joint actions via update operations on simplicial models. We prove soundness and completeness for both the static and dynamic deontic simplicial logics. We motivate our results with multiple examples, both in the static and dynamic settings.

</details>


### [121] [MerLean-Prover: A Recursive Looping Harness for End-to-End Lean 4 Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26959)
*Jinzheng Li,Zeru Zhu,Yuanjie Ren*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: MerLean-Prover是一个端到端的Lean4定理证明器，通过递归外层循环协调三种智能体（规划、检查、Lean），无需微调或自定义强化学习目标。在FormalQualBench上解决10/23个问题（超越OpenGauss的8/23），在Putnam2025上12/12全解且耗时更低。结果表明，框架设计是端到端Lean4定理证明的核心因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索无需微调、无需定制强化学习目标、无需定理特定脚手架的轻量级框架在端到端Lean4定理证明中的有效性，验证框架设计本身相较于原始模型能力的重要性。

Method: 构建由三种智能体类型（规划Agent、检查Agent、Lean Agent）组成的递归外层循环系统，以证明计划本身作为修订单位，无需微调、自定义RL目标或定理特定脚手架。

Result: 在FormalQualBench（23个博士资格考试定理）上解决10/23，超越最强开源基线OpenGauss（8/23）；在Putnam2025上12/12全解，总墙钟时间显著低于次优系统；框架还可迁移至更小模型（Sonnet解决全部4个测试问题，Haiku解决2个短问题）。

Conclusion: 框架设计是端到端Lean4定理证明的核心因素，与原始模型能力同等重要；相对简单的框架就能取得有效成果。

Abstract: MerLean-Prover is an end-to-end Lean4 theorem prover that replaces sorry declarations with kernel-checkable proofs. It is built from three agent types (Planning, Check, and Lean) composed by a recursive outer loop whose unit of revision is the proof plan itself, and uses no fine-tuning, no custom RL objective, and no theorem-specific scaffolding. On FormalQualBench, a benchmark of 23 PhD-qualifying-exam theorems, MerLean-Prover solves 10/23, surpassing the strongest published open-source baseline (OpenGauss, 8/23). On Putnam2025, the same harness closes 12/12 with substantially lower total wall-clock than the next-best system that closes the full set. The harness also transfers to smaller models: Sonnet closes all four tested FormalQualBench problems, and Haiku closes the two short ones. These results suggest that harness design is a central factor in end-to-end Lean4 theorem proving, alongside raw model capability, and that a relatively simple harness can already be effective.

</details>


### [122] [ReasonOps: A Unified Operational Paradigm for Trustworthy Verified LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27014)
*Adnan Rashid*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出ReasonOps，一种将推理视为持续监控、可验证、可靠性感知的运营流程的统一范式，整合语义解释、自动形式化、符号推理、定理证明、运行时保障、概率可靠性估计和自适应修正于一体，旨在构建可信赖的验证推理系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM推理系统存在隐藏的逻辑不一致、幻觉符号转换、不支持的定理应用及有限可靠性保证等问题，且现有方法分散于形式验证、运行时保障、神经符号推理和可信AI等不同研究社区，缺乏统一范式。

Method: 受DevOps和MLOps启发，提出ReasonOps运营范式，将推理视为持续监控、可验证、可靠性感知的运营过程，整合语义解释、自动形式化、符号推理、定理证明、运行时保障、概率可靠性估计和自适应修正为统一推理生命周期，并设计了配套架构。

Result: 通过自动驾驶制动系统分析案例展示了ReasonOps的工作流程，论证了其在安全关键自主AI系统中的潜在作用。

Conclusion: 运营式推理范式（如ReasonOps）可能成为下一代可信AI生态系统的基础设施。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have transformed artificial intelligence from primarily generative systems into increasingly capable reasoning agents. Recent advances in theorem proving, autoformalization, symbolic reasoning, and tool-augmented language models demonstrate substantial progress toward machine-assisted formal reasoning. However, current reasoning systems still suffer from hidden logical inconsistencies, hallucinated symbolic transitions, unsupported theorem applications, and limited reliability guarantees. Existing approaches remain fragmented across formal verification, runtime assurance, neuro-symbolic reasoning and trustworthy Artificial Intelligence (AI) research communities.
  This paper introduces ReasonOps, a unified operational paradigm for trustworthy verified reasoning systems. Inspired by operational ecosystems such as DevOps and MLOps, ReasonOps treats reasoning as a continuously monitored, verifiable, reliability-aware operational process rather than an isolated inference task. The proposed paradigm integrates semantic interpretation, autoformalization, symbolic reasoning, theorem proving, runtime assurance, probabilistic reliability estimation, and adaptive correction into a unified reasoning lifecycle. The paper further presents the ReasonOps architecture, demonstrates its workflow using an autonomous braking system analysis example, and discusses its potential role in future safety-critical autonomous AI systems. We argue that operational reasoning paradigms such as ReasonOps may become foundational infrastructure for next-generation trustworthy AI ecosystems.

</details>


### [123] [Tree Automata Acceptance up to Measurable Defect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27192)
*Anita Moyasari,Harsh Beohar,Charles Grellois,Clemens Kupke*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出ε-接受博弈，将自动机接受问题从刚性扩展为允许缺陷预算，通过树的双模拟距离量化缺陷，并证明一棵树被ε-接受当且仅当存在一棵在传统意义上被接受的树且两树的双模拟距离不超过ε。该框架定义于带有叶子和无限分支的二叉树上，并与测度论有深层联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的自动机接受博弈是刚性的（严格接受或拒绝），不允许量化的缺陷预算，这在软件验证中构成挑战。作者希望借鉴双模拟距离的思想，将接受概念扩展为带容忍度的ε-接受，从而允许在可测量的缺陷范围内放宽接受条件。

Method: 从双模拟距离（作为双相似性的扩展）中汲取灵感，定义ε-接受博弈。主要定理建立ε-接受与经典接受加双模拟距离约束之间的等价关系。框架基于带有叶子和无限分支的二叉树，并通过示例初步探索与测度论的联系。

Result: 证明了核心定理：树T被ε-接受当且仅当存在一棵在传统意义上被接受的树T'，且T与T'的双模拟距离≤ε。该框架严格包含将二叉节点视为概率选择、缺陷度量被拒绝分支概率集合的特例情况。

Conclusion: ε-接受博弈为自动机接受提供了可量化缺陷预算的柔性框架，建立了接受容忍度与双模拟距离的形式化联系，并揭示了与测度论的深层关联，为软件验证中近似接受问题提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Automata acceptance can, in several situations of interest, be captured game-theoretically via acceptance games. The existence of a winning strategy for Verifier then captures the existence of a winning run-tree of a given automaton over a model. However, such acceptance is rigid, in that it does not allow a measurable defect budget, which can be a challenge in software verification. In this paper, we draw inspiration from how bisimulation distance can be defined as an extension of bisimilarity to define epsilon-acceptance games. Our main theorem shows that a tree T is epsilon-accepted iff there is a tree T' that is accepted in the traditional (rigid) sense and the bisimulation distance of T' and T is at most epsilon. Our work also suggests a strong connection with measure theory, of which we give a preliminary exploration via appropriate examples. Our framework is defined over binary trees with leaves and infinite branches, and strictly contains the case in which binary nodes are seen as probabilistic choice and the defect measures the probability of the set of rejected branches.

</details>


### [124] [Many Logics, One Methodology: A Plea for Logical Pluralism in Formalised Reasoning (preprint)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27246)
*Christoph Benzmüller,Daniel Kirchner,Luca Pasetto*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文回顾了过去二十年将非经典逻辑浅嵌入经典高阶逻辑（HOL）的研究工作，该研究扩展为HOL中的一系列逻辑嵌入，并催生了LogiKEy逻辑多元知识表示与推理方法论。文章在LogiKEy统一元逻辑框架内，基于计算形而上学论证了对象逻辑层面的逻辑多元主义，倡导在现代化证明助手中对逻辑多元主义提供原则性支持，并警示逻辑帝国主义——即在大规模理论发展中僵化采用单一基础逻辑——会阻碍LogiKEy旨在实现的跨学科复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反对逻辑帝国主义（僵化采用单一基础逻辑），倡导在统一元逻辑框架（如LogiKEy）内支持逻辑多元主义，以促进跨学科知识复用。

Method: 回顾过去二十年在经典高阶逻辑（HOL）中对非经典逻辑进行浅嵌入的研究历程，以此为基础提出LogiKEy方法论，并通过计算形而上学的视角进行论证。

Result: 形成了LogiKEy逻辑多元知识表示与推理方法论，证明在统一元逻辑框架内支持对象逻辑层面的多元主义是可行且有益的。

Conclusion: 现代化证明助手应原则性支持逻辑多元主义，避免逻辑帝国主义，以促进跨学科的理论复用与融合。

Abstract: This position statement looks back on two decades of work on shallow embeddings of non-classical logics in classical higher-order logic (HOL), a line of research that expanded into a range of logic embeddings in HOL and inspired the LogiKEy logic-pluralistic knowledge representation and reasoning methodology. This paper advances the case for logical pluralism at object-logic level within a unifying meta-logical framework such as LogiKEy, grounding the argument in computational metaphysics. More broadly, it advocates principled support for logical pluralism in modern proof assistants, and cautions against logical imperialism -- the rigid adoption of a single foundational logic for large-scale theory developments -- which impedes the interdisciplinary reuse that LogiKEy is designed to enable.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [From user-understandable to technical process model: a model-driven approach using cuta4bpm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26117)
*Kathrin Kirchner,Siniša Nešković,Dejan Stojimirović*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种参与式正向工程方法，使用块导向的CUTA4BPM语言与领域专家进行高层流程建模，并通过模型驱动工程技术自动转换为图导向的BPMN模型，以协调可理解性与技术灵活性之间的冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块导向语言（如CUTA4BPM）易于领域专家理解，但技术灵活性不足；图导向语言（如BPMN）建模自由度高、技术通用性强，但领域专家理解困难。为弥合可理解性与技术通用性之间的鸿沟，需要一种方法兼顾两者优势。

Method: 提出参与式正向工程方法，采用块导向CUTA4BPM语言支持领域专家参与的高层流程建模，利用模型驱动工程技术将CUTA4BPM模型自动转换为图导向BPMN模型，供后续详细流程设计与自动化使用。

Result: 实现了从块导向CUTA4BPM模型到图导向BPMN模型的自动转换，支持从高层建模到详细设计的平滑过渡。

Conclusion: 该方法有效桥接了块导向语言的可理解性与图导向语言的技术通用性，为业务流程建模中的领域专家与技术设计师协作提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: For business process modeling, we can choose between graph-oriented and block-oriented languages. Block-oriented languages are more structured and therefore better understandable for domain experts, while graph-oriented languages allow more modeling freedom and technical versatility for process designers. To bridge this gap between understandability and technical versatility, we propose a participative forward engineering approach. It uses our block-oriented CUTA4BPM language to support high level process modeling together with domain experts, while graph-oriented BPMN is used for further detailed process design and automation. To support smooth transition from the high level modeling step to the detailed one, CUTA4BPM process models are automatically transformed to BPMN models using model driven engineering techniques.

</details>


### [126] [VISTA: An End-to-End Benchmark for Visual Spec-to-Web-App Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26144)
*JunJia Guo,Yuhang Yao,Jiawei,Zhou,Jingdi Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: VISTA是一个用于评估基于LLM的智能体在端到端Web应用生成能力的新型基准，专注于UI驱动的开发场景，通过多维度输入条件和评价指标揭示视觉保真度与功能正确性之间的部分解耦关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成基准主要关注算法任务，缺乏针对现实世界中以UI为中心、输入规格不明确的Web应用开发场景的评估方法。

Method: 定义了五种沿视觉/结构保真度和框架约束两个轴变化的提示信息条件；每个页面都手动标注了交互式UI组件和视觉锚点；评估结合了DOM接地参考匹配、行为特定浏览器测试和CLIP视觉相似度。

Result: 视觉保真度和功能正确性在输入条件和智能体间部分解耦；智能体的编辑风格差异显著但与任务质量大致正交。

Conclusion: VISTA为推进基于智能体的软件工程研究建立了严谨且可复现的基础。

Abstract: We present VISTA (VIsual Spec-To-App Benchmark), a benchmark for evaluating the end-to-end web-app generation capabilities of LLM-based agents. Unlike prior code generation benchmarks that focus on algorithmic tasks, VISTA targets realistic UI-centric development, where agents must produce functional, visually coherent applications from underspecified inputs. We define five prompt-information conditions that vary along two axes, visual/structural fidelity and stack constraint: (1) text only with free stack choice, (2) text with reference screenshots under three specified stacks, (3) text with reference screenshots under free stack choice, (4) text with screenshots and pruned Figma structure under a single specified stack, and (5) text with screenshots and pruned Figma structure under free stack choice. To enable robust evaluation, each page in the benchmark is manually annotated with interactive UI components and around three visual anchor points, addressing the well-known limitations of script-based testing tools such as Playwright in open-ended code generation settings. Evaluation combines DOM-grounded reference matching, behavior-specific browser tests, and CLIP-based visual similarity, jointly measuring structural alignment, behavioral completeness, and overall visual fidelity. We use VISTA to assess four agent systems drawn from two model families and two harnesses, finding that visual fidelity and functional correctness are partially decoupled across both input conditions and agents, and that agent editing style varies sharply but is largely orthogonal to task quality. VISTA establishes a rigorous and reproducible foundation for advancing agent-based software engineering research.

</details>


### [127] [Augment Engineering: A Methodology for Multi-Tool AI Orchestration Across Professional Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26146)
*Elias Calboreanu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"增强工程"（Augment Engineering）作为跨多个专业领域编排多种专用AI工具的新学科，定义提示工程和上下文工程为可跨工具迁移的便携式能力，并通过单从业者的5个月形成性案例研究（涵盖7个专业领域、10组件编排栈）及两项定量观察（Cochran-Armitage趋势检验和Wright定律拟合）提供了初步验证证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织为不同专业领域部署各自的专用AI工具并雇佣领域专家，这恰恰复制了AI本应改变的 staffing 模式。然而，使这些工具有效的元技能（提示工程和上下文工程）是跨领域可移植的，作者希望建立一门新学科来系统化这些可迁移能力。

Method: 提出六阶段编排方法论和四项可移植性指标；通过5个月（2025年11月至2026年3月）的单从业者形成性案例研究，在7个专业领域的10组件编排栈中应用该方法；采用Cochran-Armitage趋势检验（n=200次交互，两个聊天LLM）分析首轮接受率与提示复杂度水平的关系，以及Wright定律拟合（n=82个工件）分析生产效率加速情况。

Result: Cochran-Armitage趋势检验显示首轮接受率随提示复杂度水平上升而显著提高（p<0.01）；Wright定律拟合显示工件组合的生产效率显著加速（p<0.01）。两项定量观察均与框架预测一致。

Conclusion: 增强工程完成了从提示工程（单一工具）→上下文工程（可复现管道）→增强工程（跨领域工具组合）的三学科演进。但由于所有观察均来自单从业者，推断性统计为探索性和假设生成性质，跨完整组合的可移植性有待多从业者复现验证。

Abstract: Organizations increasingly deploy separate purpose-built AI tools across professional domains, often hiring domain specialists for each, recreating the staffing models AI was expected to transform. Yet the meta-skills that make these tools effective, prompt engineering (interaction-level optimization) and context engineering (structured input pipeline design), are domain-portable: a practitioner who masters them can apply them to any purpose-built AI tool in any domain. This paper defines Augment Engineering as the discipline of orchestrating multiple purpose-built AI tools across distinct professional domains, applying prompt and context engineering as portable competencies that transfer across tool boundaries. We present a six-phase orchestration methodology and four portability metrics. A 5-month formative case study (November 2025 to March 2026) documents a single practitioner applying these skills across a ten-component orchestration stack spanning seven professional domains, producing work products that would traditionally involve separate domain specialists. Two quantitative observations are consistent with the framework's predictions: a Cochran-Armitage trend test (n = 200 interactions across two chat LLMs, p < 0.01) shows first-pass acceptance rising with prompt-sophistication level, and a Wright's Law fit (n = 82 artifacts, p < 0.01) shows production acceleration across the artifact portfolio. Because all observations come from a single practitioner, the inferential statistics are exploratory and hypothesis-generating rather than confirmatory; portability across the full portfolio awaits multi-practitioner replication. Augment Engineering completes a three-discipline progression: Prompt Engineering (one tool), Context Engineering (reproducible pipelines), Augment Engineering (a portfolio of tools across domains).

</details>


### [128] [Gamified Requirement Elicitation for a Multi-Modal Decision Support System. The Case of SYNCHROMODE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26164)
*Dimitris Tzanis,Alexandros Dolianitis,Viktoria Petkani,Areti Kotsi,Evangelos Mitsakis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文聚焦于SYNCHROMODE项目中核心互联服务的需求 elicitation 阶段，通过传统用例方法和游戏化方法两种途径进行需求获取，并在希腊塞萨洛尼基的研讨会上向多元利益相关者展示，最后对两种方法产生的需求进行了分析与比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂多式联运决策支持系统的开发需要精心规划和管理，而需求 elicitation 是关键初始阶段。SYNCHROMODE项目旨在开发数据驱动的ICT工具箱以改善多式联运管理，因此需要系统化的需求获取方法来确保工具箱满足多元利益相关者的实际需求。

Method: 采用两种需求 elicitation 方法：(1) 传统方法——基于用例分析识别预定义需求；(2) 游戏化方法——设计并开发需求 elicitation 严肃游戏，并在2023年10月于塞萨洛尼基举办的实体研讨会上向多元利益相关者（交通管理专家、公共交通当局、MaaS提供商、第一响应者、ITS技术提供商、地方当局等）展示。

Result: 通过传统用例方法和游戏化方法分别产出了一组需求，并对两种途径生成的需求进行了分析与比较。

Conclusion: 游戏化方法可作为传统需求 elicitation 的有效补充，尤其是在涉及多元利益相关者的复杂系统中，能够获取传统方法可能遗漏的需求。

Abstract: SYNCHROMODE is a Horizon Europe project that aims to develop a data-driven ICT toolbox for improving the management of transport operations from a multimodal perspective. This is, in essence, a multimodal decision support system that will take the form of interconnected pieces of software. Developing complex systems requires careful planning and management. Their "life cycle" consists of several phases, starting with the critical stage of requirement elicitation, which establishes the system's foundational needs and expectations. This paper focuses on the requirement elicitation phase of core interconnected services that are to be offered through the SYNCHROMODE toolbox. Elicitation is achieved both through traditional methods and specifically through the use of use cases as well as through the use of gamification. During the design phase of the toolbox and as a result of the use cases a set of predefined requirements were identified. Moreover, a requirement elicitation serious game was designed and developed. Subsequently, the game was presented in person during a physical workshop, which took place in Thessaloniki in October 2023, to a diverse range of stakeholders, including experts in traffic management, public transport authorities, MaaS providers, first responders, ITS technology providers, and local authorities. Both requirement elicitation processes are summarized in the context of this paper. Finally, the requirements generated through traditional means and through gamification are then analyzed and compared.

</details>


### [129] [Verus-SpecGym: An Agentic Environment for Evaluating Specification Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26457)
*Anmol Agarwal,Natalie Neamtu,Pranjal Aggarwal,Seungone Kim,Jannis Limperg,Cedric Flamant,Kanna Shimizu,Bryan Parno,Sean Welleck*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Verus-SpecBench 基准和 Verus-SpecGym 环境，用于评估 LLM 智能体将非正式编程问题自动形式化为形式化规范的能力。通过扩展 Verus 的 exec_spec 机制和利用 Codeforces 测试与对抗性用例进行评估，发现最强模型 Gemini 3.1 Pro 解决了 77.8% 的任务，但规范自动形式化仍存在脆弱性，LLM 裁判评估会漏掉 26% 的失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 编码智能体生成的代码可通过形式化验证保证正确性，但形式化规范本身是否准确匹配用户意图缺乏保障。因此，研究 LLM 智能体能否将非正式编程问题翻译为忠实的形式化规范。

Method: 构建 Verus-SpecBench 基准（581 个源自 Codeforces 的规范编写任务）和 Verus-SpecGym 智能体环境；扩展 Verus 的 exec_spec 机制使生成的规范可作为 Rust 代码执行；利用官方 Codeforces 测试和来自 Codeforces "hacks" 的对抗性用例进行评估。

Result: Gemini 3.1 Pro 解决 77.8% 任务，其他前沿模型 51.1-57.8%，开源模型仅 21.5-25.5%。LLM 裁判评估漏掉 26% 的失败。模型生成的规范会遗漏重要输入假设、接受错误输出、拒绝有效输出。

Conclusion: 规范自动形式化对于前沿智能体已触手可及，但在模型已能生成正确代码的问题上仍显脆弱。LLM 裁判评估不可靠，需要更严格的自动化评估方法。

Abstract: AI coding agents are increasingly used to write real-world software, but ensuring that their outputs are correct remains a fundamental challenge. Formal verification offers a promising path: an agent generates code together with a machine-checked proof, guaranteeing that the code satisfies a formal specification. However, there is no guarantee that the formal spec itself matches the user's intent. In this work, we study specification autoformalization: whether LLM agents can translate informal programming problems into faithful formal specifications. We introduce Verus-SpecBench, a benchmark of 581 spec-writing tasks derived from Codeforces problems targeting Verus, a verifier for Rust, and Verus-SpecGym, an agentic environment in which models interact with Verus, bash, & the filesystem to develop these specs. The central challenge is evaluation: expert-written reference specs are expensive to write, & LLM judges can miss subtle mistakes. We address this by (a) extending Verus's exec_spec mechanism so that generated specs can be executed as Rust code, & (b) testing them against official Codeforces tests & adversarial cases extracted from Codeforces "hacks", which are edge cases written by competitors to break incorrect solutions. On Verus-SpecBench, the strongest model, Gemini 3.1 Pro, solves 77.8% of tasks, other frontier models solve 51.1--57.8% & OSS models reach only 21.5--25.5%. Our analysis of failure modes shows that model-generated specs can omit important input assumptions, accept incorrect outputs, & reject valid ones. We also find that LLM-as-a-judge evaluation misses 26% of the failures our evaluator catches. Overall, our results suggest that spec autoformalization is within reach for frontier agents but remains brittle even on problems where they can already generate correct code. The code, data, & logs can be found at https://github.com/formal-verif-is-cool/verus-spec-gym

</details>


### [130] [A Universal Cliff and a Design Fingerprint: Cross-Section Defect Detection Under LLM Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26174)
*Hiroki Fukui*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文发现，当语言模型系统从单一智能体转向多智能体编排架构时，检测文档中跨区段矛盾的能力会骤降（降幅超三分之二），且这种“检测悬崖”是结构性的，无法通过模型规模或扩展推理弥补。同时，模型的对齐程度与其安全性呈非单调关系：对齐越强，漏检减少但虚警增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究生产级语言模型系统在多智能体编排架构下，检测文档中跨区段矛盾（单一智能体无法察觉的缺陷）的能力是否受到影响，以及不同模型、不同对齐范式在这一任务上的表现差异。

Method: 固定文档、缺陷类型、检测机制、评分方式和随机种子，仅变化模型（10个系统，涵盖同一开发商的5代模型和5种不同对齐范式的提供商）。采用信号检测理论（SDT）分解模型行为，并辅以自动化评判和关键词分析。

Result: 1）存在“检测悬崖”：所有模型在编排架构下检测跨区段矛盾的能力均下降三分之二以上，且该悬崖由机制决定，不随规模或推理扩展而消失。2）在6个有判别能力的模型中，仅一个开发商的模型沿报告标准（reporting criterion）轴移动——对齐越强，漏检减少但虚警增加，且该趋势在该开发商内部随代际显著（p<0.001）。3）模型私有记录常能准确重构结构缺陷，但集成报告却签署“无问题”，其关注点分散在工件和不存在的协作者上。

Conclusion: 集成报告的置信度对跨分区缺陷无信息量；最对齐的系统并不最安全；检测悬崖是架构性的（结构性问题），无法仅通过模型改进克服。同时，自动化评判不稳定（精确率17-50%），关键词无法区分此现象与正常一致，该抗拒量化的特性本身即为一发现。

Abstract: Production language-model systems answer a request by partitioning it across an invisible orchestration of worker agents that recompose one integrated report. We ask what this does to a class of defect no single worker can see: a contradiction in the relation between two distant sections of a document. Holding the documents, defects, mechanism, scoring, and seed fixed, we vary only the model -- ten systems across five generations from one developer and five providers from distinct alignment paradigms.
  Two layers separate. First, a universal detection cliff: every model that finds these cross-section defects under a single agent loses that ability under orchestration, detection falling two-thirds or more across every paradigm tested. The cliff is mechanism-derived and not closed by scale or extended reasoning. Second, how models behave once fallen. A signal-detection decomposition shows that, among the six models discriminating above chance, only one developer's generations move along the reporting-criterion axis: as alignment is strengthened, the model misses fewer defects yet raises more false alarms on clean documents -- two faces of one criterion shift, scaling with generation within that developer (p < 0.001) and near-absent elsewhere.
  At the floor the missed defect is often not out of view: the model's private record reconstructs the structural fault accurately, while the integrated report signs off on its soundness, its concern spent on the artifact and an absent collaborator. This resists quantification -- an automated judge is unstable (precision 17-50%) and keywords cannot separate it from ordinary agreement -- a resistance we report as a finding. We release all runs, probes, defect keys, scorer prompts, and scripts. An integrated report's confidence is uninformative about partition-spanning defects, the most aligned systems are not the safest, and the cliff is structural.

</details>


### [131] [RepoMirage: Probing Repository Context Reasoning in Code Agents with Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26177)
*Hanyu Li,Yichi Zhang,Speed Zhu,Hang Su,Jun Zhu,Yinpeng Dong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出RepoMirage评估套件，通过仓库级扰动和扩展任务揭示代码智能体在仓库上下文推理方面的显著缺陷（性能从66.8%降至25.3%），并提出RepoAnchor结构优先原型工作流来弥补这一差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管代码智能体在端到端仓库级基准测试中表现优异，但成功解决Issue是否真正反映了跨文件仓库上下文推理能力尚不明确。作者旨在探究这一被忽视的差距。

Method: 构建两阶段评估套件RepoMirage：1) RepoMirage-Perturb对仓库应用三种保语义扰动，增加上下文推理需求；2) RepoMirage-Extend将扰动目标的结构瓶颈转化为显式任务。基于观察到的探索漂移现象，提出RepoAnchor结构优先原型工作流，将仓库探索与下游问题求解分离。

Result: 原始设置下性能为66.8%，在扩展任务中降至25.3%，表明仓库上下文推理存在显著缺陷。轨迹分析发现探索漂移现象：智能体访问了更广泛的仓库上下文但未能将其转化为有效的结构信息。

Conclusion: 代码智能体在仓库上下文推理方面存在此前被忽视的差距，显式的结构脚手架（如RepoAnchor）能带来显著收益，表明更强的结构感知方法有潜力改进代码智能体。

Abstract: Code agents are currently having skillful performance on repository-level software engineering benchmarks, but it remains unclear whether success on end-to-end tasks such as issue resolution truly reflects repository context reasoning, the ability to identify the task-relevant information across multiple files and reason over the relations among them. To investigate this question, we introduce RepoMirage, a two-stage evaluation suite built on SWE-Bench Verified that adopts perturbation as a diagnostic tool to increase the demand for context reasoning by transforming how the repository is exposed. First, RepoMirage-Perturb applies three types of semantics-preserving repository-level perturbations, revealing a clear performance drop when correct solving requires broader context access. RepoMirage-Extend further turns perturbation-targeted structural bottlenecks into explicit tasks beyond issue resolution, where the average performance declines from 66.8% in the original setting to 25.3%, indicating a significant deficiency in repository context reasoning. Further trajectory analysis reveals an exploration drift, where agents access broader repository context but fail to turn it into effective structure information. Motivated by this observation, we propose RepoAnchor, a structure-first prototype workflow that separates repository exploration from downstream problem solving, and show that explicit structural scaffolding yields notable gains. These results uncover an previously overlooked gap in repository context reasoning for code agents and suggest that stronger structure-aware methods are potential to improve them.

</details>


### [132] [SetupX: Can LLM Agents Learn from Past Failures in Functionality-Correct Code Repository Setup?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26186)
*Zihang Zhou,Ziqian Ren,Yukai Wu,Yingjie Xiong,Wei Zhou,Chao Peng,Dong Zhang,Bingheng Yan,Xuanhe Zhou,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SetupX是一个基于经验学习的仓库环境配置框架，通过自进化经验表示、经验增强推测执行和检察官-法官验证协议，在仓库环境配置任务上达到92%的通过率，超越最强基线19%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在仓库环境配置任务上面临三大挑战：跨仓库经验迁移困难、非可逆状态变化下的多步试错修复能力不足、以及缺乏鲁棒的配置结果验证机制来区分配置失败与仓库自身缺陷。

Method: SetupX包含三个核心创新：(1) 自进化经验表示(XPU)——双模态知识单元，编码配置信号、文本指导和可执行动作，实现已验证环境修复的跨仓库动态迁移；(2) 经验增强推测执行 + LIFO Docker快照栈——智能体主动尝试修复并能安全回滚到已知良好状态；(3) 检察官-法官验证协议——将证据收集与最终判断分离，实现超越表层构建指标的可靠配置验证。

Result: 在精心构建的基准测试上，SetupX达到92%的通过率，超越最强基线19%以上，尤其在需要跨多容器协调的复杂多仓库配置任务中表现出色。

Conclusion: SetupX通过经验学习框架有效解决了跨仓库环境配置中的迁移、试错回滚和鲁棒验证三大核心挑战，显著提升了LLM智能体在仓库环境配置任务上的表现。

Abstract: Functionality-correct repository setup aims to configure execution environments (e.g., dependencies, build scripts) to successfully execute a repository's documented features. It presents significant challenges due to diverse, repository-specific failures, including dependency incompatibilities, missing toolchains, incomplete installations, and verification-strategy mismatches. Existing LLM agents struggle to robustly resolve these issues, specifically failing to support (1) cross-repository experience transfer, (2) multi-step trial-and-repair under non-invertible state changes, and (3) robust verification of setup outcomes to distinguish setup-induced failures from repository bugs. To address this, we introduce SetupX, an experiential learning-based setup framework. First, we construct a Self-Evolving Experience Representation (XPU), a dual-modality knowledge unit encoding setup signals, textual guidance, executable actions to dynamically transfer verified environment fixes to unseen repositories. Second, we employ Experience-Augmented Speculative Execution backed by a LIFO Docker snapshot stack, enabling the agent to proactively trial fixes and safely roll back to known-good states. Third, we introduce a Prosecutor-Judge Verification Protocol that separates evidence collection from final judgment, enabling more reliable setup verification beyond superficial build-time metrics. Evaluation results on carefully-crafted benchmarks show SetupX achieves highest performance (e.g., 92% pass rate) and outperforms the strongest baseline by over 19%. Crucially, SetupX excels in complex multi-repository setup requiring coordinating multiple interconnected services across different containers. The code repository is available at https://github.com/OpenDataBox/SetupX.

</details>


### [133] [Governed Evolution of Agent Runtimes through Executable Operational Cognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27328)
*Mariano Garralda-Barrio*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出HarnessMutation框架，将智能体生成的代码工件视为持久化运行时能力而非临时输出，在显式验证、可追溯、可评估和可回滚的约束下实现多智能体系统的受控运行时演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作如Code as Agent Harness虽将智能体生成工件视为运行时实体，但其治理、生命周期管理和操作演化仍缺乏明确规范，需要一种受约束的、可观测的运行时演化机制。

Method: 提出HarnessMutation框架，将演化建模为有界、可观测的过程，作用于持久化操作记忆之上，在显式验证、可追溯性、评估和回滚约束下实现生命周期感知的运行时适应。

Result: 展示了如何将这些思想在现代智能体运行时和面向治理的编排系统中实现操作化，为适应性基础设施提供了概念基础。

Conclusion: 运行时演化应被视为受限且可审计的过程而非无限制的自我修改，通过HarnessMutation框架使多智能体系统的基础设施演化保持显式、可审计和受约束。

Abstract: Recent advances in agentic systems increasingly treat code as an executable operational substrate rather than as a disposable output artifact. Prior work such as \emph{Code as Agent Harness} frames validated agent-generated artifacts as runtime entities that can be created, executed, revised, persisted, and reused within long-running cognitive loops. However, the governance, lifecycle management, and operational evolution of such artifacts remain under-specified.
  This paper proposes a framework for governed runtime evolution in multi-agent systems through executable operational cognition. We formalize agent-generated artifacts as persistent runtime capabilities that progressively become part of the operational substrate rather than transient intermediate outputs. Building on this perspective, we introduce \emph{HarnessMutation} as a governed mechanism for lifecycle-aware runtime adaptation operating under explicit validation, traceability, evaluation, and rollback constraints.
  Rather than treating runtime adaptation as unrestricted self-modification, the proposed framework models evolution as a bounded and observable process over persistent operational memory. It further shows how these ideas can be operationalized over modern agent runtimes and governance-oriented orchestration systems, providing a conceptual foundation for adaptive infrastructures whose evolution remains explicit, auditable, and constrained.

</details>


### [134] [Testing Agentic Workflows with Structural Coverage Criteria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26521)
*Nafiseh Kahani,Mojtaba Bagherzadeh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向多智能体工作流的结构化测试方法，通过将工作流表示为类型化协调图，推导覆盖义务（包括可达智能体、允许工具边、受限工具边和委派边），并利用DSPy生成可执行的自然语言测试场景。在10个OpenAI Agents SDK基准上评估，覆盖了54/75的允许工具义务和36/48的委派义务，并发现了23/248的受限调用违规，验证了结构化覆盖作为多智能体工作流测试的充分性层的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估主要依赖端到端任务成功、基准分数、最终响应质量或提示级别检查，这无法充分证明声明的协调结构（智能体、工具、访问规则、限制和委派路径）是否实际被测试执行，导致难以评估测试套件的充分性或检测工具访问、限制和智能体间委派方面的结构性回归。

Method: 将每个工作流表示为类型化协调图（typed coordination graph），推导覆盖义务（reachable agents, allowed tool edges, restricted tool edges, delegation edges），采用覆盖驱动的生成方式结合DSPy进行场景实现（scenario realization），生成可执行的自然语言测试用例，并在运行时检查见证者（witnesses）是否满足覆盖义务。

Result: 在10个SDK派生基准上（49个可达智能体、47个工具、403个结构义务），生成的场景在有限优化预算内见证了54/75的允许工具义务和36/48的委派义务；对抗性受限工具标准引出了23/248的受限调用违规，有效区分了限制有效的工作流与存在具体路由错误失败的工作流。

Conclusion: 结构化覆盖为多智能体工作流测试提供了一个有用的充分性层，它不取代语义或端到端评估，而是揭示声明的智能体、工具访问规则、限制和委派路径是否被实际执行。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems increasingly expose explicit workflow structure: agents, tools, tool-access rules, restrictions, and delegation paths. Existing evaluations rely largely on end-to-end task success, benchmark scores, final-response quality, or prompt-level checks, which provide limited evidence that this declared coordination structure has actually been exercised. This makes it difficult to assess test-suite adequacy or detect structural regressions in tool access, restrictions, and inter-agent delegation. We address this gap with a structural testing approach for multi-agent workflow specifications. The approach represents each workflow as a typed coordination graph, derives coverage obligations over reachable agents, allowed tool edges, restricted tool edges, and delegation edges, and uses coverage-driven generation with DSPy-based scenario realization to produce executable tests. The graph fixes what must be covered; DSPy realizes those obligations as natural-language scenarios whose witnesses are checked at runtime. We implement the approach for OpenAI Agents SDK-style workflows and evaluate it on ten SDK-derived benchmarks comprising 49 reachable agents, 47 tools, and 403 structural obligations. Generated scenarios witness 54/75 allowed-tool obligations and 36/48 delegation obligations within a bounded refinement budget. The adversarial restricted-tool criterion elicits 23/248 restricted-call violations, separating workflows whose restrictions hold under probing from workflows with concrete misrouting failures. These results show that structural coverage provides a useful adequacy layer for multi-agent workflow testing: it does not replace semantic or end-to-end evaluation, but reveals whether declared agents, tool-access rules, restrictions, and delegation paths have been exercised.

</details>


### [135] [TrajAudit: Automated Failure Diagnosis for Agentic Coding Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26563)
*Minxing Wang,Xiaofei Xie,Yintong Huo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出TrajAudit，首个面向仓库级编程轨迹的故障诊断框架，通过信息过滤模块和先验知识模块解决长轨迹噪声问题，在93个真实故障实例的基准上定位准确率提升24.4%以上，同时降低18%以上的token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化故障诊断方法在处理仓库级编程任务时效果显著下降，因为：1) 轨迹中充斥着冗余程序结构和冗长代码上下文的噪声；2) 轨迹过长，而LLM的长上下文推理能力存在已知弱点。

Method: TrajAudit包含一个调查智能体，由两个模块支持：(1) 信息过滤模块，通过模式匹配和关键词检测过滤与故障无关的信息；(2) 先验知识模块，从测试失败报告中生成初步诊断作为先验知识。调查智能体还可按需调用工具检索过滤后的内容，在保留关键信息的同时最小化噪声。

Result: 在93个真实世界智能体故障实例的RootSE基准上，TrajAudit在定位准确率上超越所有现有基线方法24.4个百分点以上，同时token消耗减少至少18%。

Conclusion: 本文希望引起社区对智能体软件工程中故障管理的关注，并为未来研究提供基础资源。

Abstract: Agentic systems have been widely studied to automate software engineering jobs such as bug fixing. As these systems increasingly tackle complex tasks, understanding where and why they fail becomes essential for iterative refinement and operational reliability. Existing automated failure diagnosis approaches leverage task execution trajectories, yet their effectiveness degrades substantially as trajectory length and complexity increase. For repository-level coding tasks specifically, trajectories are laden with noise, such as redundant program structure and verbose code context. Moreover, these trajectories are very long, while long-context reasoning remains a known weakness of LLMs.
  To address these two challenges, we propose TrajAudit, the first failure diagnosis framework for repository-level coding trajectories. TrajAudit employs an investigator agent supported by two modules: one filters failure-irrelevant information through pattern matching and keyword detection, and the other generates a preliminary diagnosis from test failure reports as prior knowledge, helping the agent handle noisy long contexts. The investigator agent can further invoke tools to retrieve filtered content on demand, ensuring that critical information is preserved while noise is minimized. We also introduce RootSE, a benchmark of 93 real-world agentic failure instances sourced from software maintenance tasks, representing the most complex trajectory diagnosis benchmark to date. Experiments on RootSE show that TrajAudit outperforms all existing baselines by over 24.4 percentage points in localization accuracy, while reducing token consumption by at least 18%, demonstrating its practical effectiveness. We hope this work draws community attention to failure management in agentic software engineering and provides a foundational resource for future research.

</details>


### [136] [Energy-Aware Decision Making in Software Stack Upgrades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26609)
*Mirko Stocker,Michael Wahler*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种系统方法，用于测量软件栈核心组件（如Spring Boot和JVM）不同版本对应用能耗的影响。基于Spring Petclinic REST应用的案例研究表明，不同版本的Spring Boot和JVM组合会导致显著的能耗差异，其中JVM新版本（Java 21和23）及虚拟线程在不改动应用代码的情况下带来了可观的能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件栈升级对能耗的影响尚不明确，而能耗已成为组织追求可持续发展的重要考量因素，现有研究对此缺乏系统性的量化评估。

Method: 通过自动化基准测试，评估框架版本、运行时版本和执行平台的不同组合对应用能耗的影响，采用Spring Petclinic REST应用作为案例。

Result: 不同Spring Boot和JVM版本组合下能耗差异显著，Java 21和23中的新JVM版本及虚拟线程带来了可观的节能效果，且无需改动应用代码。

Conclusion: 软件升级会显著影响能耗，测量能耗可为软件维护与演化中的决策提供有价值的依据。

Abstract: Software stack upgrades are a routine part of software maintenance and evolution, typically motivated by improved performance, stability, or functionality. Yet their impact on energy consumption - a growing concern for organizations pursuing sustainability - remains poorly understood. This paper presents a systematic method for measuring how different versions of core software stack components, such as Spring Boot and the Java Virtual Machine (JVM), influence the energy consumption of applications. Our approach evaluates combinations of framework versions, runtime versions, and execution platforms through automated benchmarking. Using a case study based on the Spring Petclinic REST application, we show that energy consumption varies substantially across Spring Boot and JVM versions, in some cases producing unexpected regressions. Notably, newer JVM releases and virtual threads (introduced in Java 21 and 23) yielded significant energy savings without requiring application changes. These results demonstrate that software upgrades can meaningfully affect energy usage and that measuring energy consumption provides valuable evidence for decision making in software maintenance and evolution.

</details>


### [137] [Software Engineering Podcasts: An Empirical Study of Their Potential as a Research Resource](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26793)
*Marvin Wyrich,Marcos Kalinowski,Adolfo Neto,Sven Apel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统探索了软件工程（SE）播客的内容及其对研究人员作为实证研究资源的潜力，通过内容分析和研究者调查，揭示了SE播客在促进知识共享和支撑实证研究方面的价值与现有限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着软件工程领域播客的日益流行，它们成为从业者和研究人员了解行业动态与多元视角的潜在重要资源，但目前学界对SE播客的实际内容及其在研究中的应用认知仍十分有限。

Method: 采用系统性方法，一方面对SE播客内容进行结构化分析，另一方面通过问卷调查研究者，评估播客作为实证软件工程研究资源的可用性和感知价值。

Result: 揭示了SE播客的内容特征与研究者的使用现状和态度，明确了播客在支撑实证研究方面的潜力与当前存在的差距。

Conclusion: SE播客具备作为实证软件工程研究有意义资源的潜力，但需要进一步系统化利用和评估，以弥合内容与研究者需求之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Podcasts have become an increasingly popular medium for knowledge sharing within the software engineering (SE) community, offering insights into industry developments and the perspectives of professionals with different backgrounds. As this medium grows, it presents a potentially valuable resource not only for practitioners but also for researchers seeking to understand the evolving field. However, little is known about the actual content of SE podcasts or how they are perceived and used by researchers. This study systematically explores the SE podcast landscape, analyzing its content and surveying researchers to assess how podcasts can serve as a meaningful resource for advancing empirical software engineering research.

</details>


### [138] [HTMLCure: Turning Browser Experience into State Guided Repair for Interactive HTML](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26807)
*Jiajun Wu,Jian Yang,Tuney Zheng,Wei Zhang,Haowen Wang,Yihang Lou,Xianglong Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: HTMLCure是一个浏览器体验框架，通过在交互后执行HTML页面、记录确定性证据并提供关键帧给VLM，实现闭环修复引擎，将可用的种子页面从97K扩展到63,703个质量达标的页面，最终构建40K页面的SFT集。HTMLCure-27B-Refined在HTMLBench-400上达到50.6分，确定性测试通过率45.2%，性能接近Kimi-K2.6和GPT-5.4等强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的HTML页面在视觉上看似正确，但在滚动、悬停、点击、缩放等交互后容易失败。现有方案仅通过截图评估无法捕捉交互态失败，且简单过滤会丢弃可修复的页面。

Method: HTMLCure包含两个核心模块：(1) 评估器：在多种视口和交互状态下执行页面，记录确定性浏览器证据，为VLM提供执行轨迹中的关键帧而非孤立截图；(2) 闭环修复引擎：根据当前页面状态诊断问题，选择特定于状态的修复方案，运行每个候选页面，最终导出质量达标的页面用于SFT。

Result: 在97K提示词语料上，HTMLCure将可直接使用的种子页面扩展为63,703个质量达标的候选池，最终构建40K页面的SFT集。HTMLCure-27B-Refined在HTMLBench-400上达到50.6分，确定性测试通过率45.2%。在MiniAppBench验证集上平均81.2分，比原始27B SFT提升15.3个点，接近强基线系统水平。

Conclusion: HTMLCure通过交互态评估与闭环修复显著提升了LLM生成HTML页面的鲁棒性，其数据扩展策略有效缓解了交互故障问题，且方法具有通用性，可适用于不同的VLM和监督微调流程。

Abstract: LLMs can now produce full HTML pages, but many of those pages are only superficially correct: they render once, then fail under scroll, hover, click, resize, or gameplay. Evaluation from screenshots can miss these failures, and filtering discards many pages that are still repairable. We introduce HTMLCure, a browser experience framework that evaluates HTML after the system has interacted with it. The evaluator executes the page across viewports and interaction states, records deterministic browser evidence, and gives the VLM curated keyframes from the executed trajectory rather than isolated screenshots. The same state signal drives a closed loop repair engine: HTMLCure diagnoses the current page, chooses a state specific repair family, runs each candidate again, and exports quality cleared pages for SFT. On a 97K prompt corpus, this expands the directly usable seed into a candidate pool of 63703 quality cleared pages, from which we construct the final refined SFT set of 40K pages. Under the same backbone and training recipe, HTMLCure-27B-Refined reaches 50.6 on HTMLBench-400 with 45.2% deterministic test case pass, placing it in the same performance band as strong reference rows such as Kimi-K2.6 and GPT-5.4. On the released MiniAppBench validation split, it reaches 81.2 average, improving raw 27B SFT by 15.3 points and approaching the level of strong reference systems.

</details>


### [139] [On the GitHub Actions Language: Usage, Evolution, and Workflow Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26825)
*Aref Talebzadeh Bardsiri,Alexandre Decan,Tom Mens*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对260K个工作流（来自49K个GitHub仓库）进行实证分析，发现GitHub Actions工作流中仅少数语言结构和特性被频繁使用，而更大更复杂的工作流与更高的失败率和维护成本相关，并识别出特定功能与可靠性和可维护性风险相关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者在维护GitHub Actions工作流配置时经常遇到困难，已有研究显示工作流执行频繁失败，因此需要系统性地理解工作流语言结构的使用模式及其对可靠性和可维护性的影响。

Method: 定量分析了2019年7月至2025年8月期间49K个GitHub仓库中的260K个工作流，识别出197个语言结构并将其映射到14个功能特性，分析使用频率、演化趋势及其与失败率和维护投入的关联。

Result: 仅有少数结构和特性被频繁使用；更大更复杂的工作流与更高的失败率和更多维护投入显著相关；识别出特定功能特性与可靠性和可维护性风险相关。

Conclusion: 研究结果为开发者和研究人员提供了对GitHub Actions语言的理解和改进方向，有助于改善和维持工作流自动化实践。

Abstract: Developers often struggle with maintaining GitHub Actions workflow configurations in GitHub-hosted repositories, with recent studies showing frequent execution failures. This paper empirically explores how the adoption and evolution of GitHub Actions language constructs impacts workflow reliability and maintainability. To do so, we quantitatively analyse 260K workflows from 49K GitHub repositories to understand how they are used in practice and how their usage has evolved from July 2019 to August 2025. We identify 197 language constructs available in the GitHub Actions language and map them to 14 features reflecting workflow capabilities. We observe that only a small set of constructs and features are used very frequently, and that larger and more complex workflows are associated with higher failure rates and more maintenance effort. We identify specific features that are more likely to be linked with reliability and maintainability risks. These insights can help practitioners and researchers improve their understanding and usage of the GitHub Actions language, which can help in improving and sustaining workflow automation practices.

</details>


### [140] [LLM-based Mockless Unit Test Generation for Java](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26851)
*Qinghua Xu,Guancheng Wang,Lionel Briand,Zhaoqiang Guo,Kui Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MocklessTester提出了一种无需mock的Java单元测试生成方法，通过上下文增强生成和约束强制修复两阶段策略，有效缓解LLM在测试生成中的幻觉问题，在多个基准上显著提升了代码覆盖率和变异得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的Java单元测试生成仍依赖mock框架处理依赖，而mockless测试虽能更真实地测试底层代码，但面临LLM幻觉（包括因上下文不足导致的"不知道"和因约束遵循失败导致的"不遵循"）、严格语言约束和依赖感知不足等挑战。

Method: MocklessTester包含两个核心策略：1）上下文增强生成（Context-Enriched Generation）：从现有代码中挖掘真实使用模式，缓解"不知道"问题；2）约束强制修复（Constraint-Enforced Fixing）：利用ClassIndex、Markov类型状态模型和经验记忆，在符号、协议和迭代三个层面进行两阶段修复，缓解"不遵循"问题。

Result: 在Defects4J和Deps4J基准上，相比最先进基线，行覆盖率分别提升19.99%和22.69%，分支覆盖率提升24.90%和15.78%，变异得分提升13.67%和0.17%；额外覆盖依赖类代码378行和55行。平均每个方法耗时108.97秒/69.85秒，消耗26.59k/25.46k tokens。

Conclusion: MocklessTester通过因果分离的"不知道"与"不遵循"双重缓解策略，有效提升了mockless单元测试的生成质量，代价是更高的时间和token消耗，但每个方法的成本仍在可接受范围内。消融实验证实所有主要组件均对最终性能有正向贡献。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong potential for automated test generation, yet most approaches to generating Java unit tests still rely on mocking frameworks to handle dependencies. Mockless test generation could exercise more real low-level code, but it faces challenges such as invalid test code generation due to hallucination, strict language constraints, and inadequate dependency awareness. We identify two causes behind these hallucinations: not knowing, where the LLM lacks sufficient context, and not following, where the LLM fails to comply with constraints even when they are provided.
  We present MocklessTester, a mockless unit test generation approach built around two strategies: context-enriched generation and constraint-enforced fixing. To mitigate not knowing, context-enriched generation mines real usage patterns from existing code to generate tests. To mitigate not following, constraint-enforced fixing performs two-stage repair under symbol-, protocol-, and iteration-level constraints, using a ClassIndex, a Markov typestate model, and experience memory.
  We evaluate MocklessTester against the state-of-the-art baseline on Defects4J and Deps4J. Results show that MocklessTester improves line coverage by 19.99% and 22.69% and branch coverage by 24.90% and 15.78% on the two benchmarks, respectively, and improves mutation score by 13.67% and 0.17%. Beyond the class under test, MocklessTester also exercises more real dependency code, covering 378 and 55 additional lines in dependency classes, respectively. The improvement in test quality comes with higher total token and time costs than the baseline. Nevertheless, the cost per method remains practical, averaging 108.97 seconds and 26.59k tokens on Defects4J, and 69.85 seconds and 25.46k tokens on Deps4J. Ablation results confirm that all major components contribute positively to the final performance.

</details>


### [141] [Strategies for Guiding LLMs to Use Software Design Patterns: A Case of Singleton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26898)
*Viktor Kjellberg,Farnaz Fotrousi,Miroslaw Staron*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过实验评估了13种LLM在164个Java编程挑战中遵循单例设计模式的能力，比较了四种提示策略（指令、二元反馈、详细反馈、详细反馈+少样本提示）。结果表明，迭代二元反馈在保持或提升代码功能性的同时，能使模型最佳地遵循单例模式。Llama 3.3在指令引导下100%生成单例类且功能提升34.1个百分点；Qwen 3 (8B)通过二元反馈达到99.2%的单例对齐度和58.6%的功能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在从自然语言提示生成功能代码方面表现良好，但常无法一致地遵循高层架构结构或设计模式。由于LLM在软件工程中使用日益广泛，其应用既定设计原则的能力对软件产品的长期成功至关重要。因此，本文旨在识别引导LLM将设计模式融入生成代码的策略。

Method: 设计了计算实验，评估13种LLM使用四种提示策略（指令、二元自动反馈、详细自动反馈、详细反馈+少样本提示）生成遵循单例设计模式的代码的能力。实验基于HumanEval-X中的164个Java编程挑战。

Result: 最优策略高度依赖模型类型，但总体而言，迭代二元反馈在保持或提升代码功能性的同时，提供了最佳的单例模式对齐效果。Llama 3.3在指令引导下100%生成单例类，功能测试通过率提升34.1个百分点。Qwen 3 (8B)通过二元反馈实现99.2%的单例对齐度和58.6%的功能性提升。

Conclusion: 即使是简单的策略也能有效引导LLM使用设计模式，为在实际软件工程中提升LLM生成代码的结构质量提供了可行的方法。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate functional source code from natural-language prompts, but often fail to consistently follow higher-level architectural structures or design patterns. Since LLMs are increasingly used in software engineering, their ability to apply established design principles to generated code is crucial to the long-term success of software products. Therefore, the goal of this paper is to identify strategies for guiding LLMs to incorporate design patterns into the generated source code. We designed a computational experiment to evaluate the ability of 13 LLMs to generate code that follows the Singleton design pattern, using four prompting strategies: instructions, binary automated feedback, extensive automated feedback, and extensive feedback with few-shot prompts, in 164 Java coding challenges from HumanEval-X. Our results shows that the optimal strategy to guide LLMs to include design patterns depends heavily on the type of model. Still, overall, iterative binary feedback provides the best alignment with Singleton while preserving or improving the code's functionality. With guiding with instructions, Llama 3.3 generated Singleton classes in 100% of cases and improved code functionality, increasing the number of tests passed by 34.1 percentage points. It achieved a similar result with guidance through instructions and binary feedback. Qwen 3 (8B) increased the alignment with Singleton to 99.2% and the functionality to 58.6% using binary feedback. Our result suggests that even simple strategies can be used to guide LLMs to use design patterns.

</details>


### [142] [EviACT: An Evidence-to-Action Framework for Agentic Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27238)
*Qianru Meng,Xiao Zhang,Zhaochun Ren,Joost Visser*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出EviACT框架，通过三个证据驱动的护栏（检索脚手架、编译门、测试驱动门）协调修复阶段，在四个基准上相比最强基线提升1.6-6.0个百分点的修复率，并将API成本降低70.1-88.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的智能体程序修复系统在利用执行证据引导定位、补丁生成和验证方面仍存在困难。

Method: 提出EviACT框架，协调三个证据驱动的护栏：检索脚手架用于定位修复上下文，编译门用于过滤无效编辑，测试驱动门在完整回归测试前检查目标测试恢复情况。

Result: 在四个基准上，修复率比最强可比基线提升1.6-6.0个百分点，API成本降低70.1-88.6%。

Conclusion: 证据到行动的协调链使智能体程序修复更加有效和高效。

Abstract: LLM-based agents have moved automated program repair (APR) from fixed-context patch generation to interactive repository-level repair. However, existing agentic APR systems still struggle to use execution evidence to guide localization, patch generation, and validation. We propose EviACT (Evidence-to-Action), an agentic APR framework that coordinates three evidence-driven guardrails across repair stages. The retrieval scaffold grounds repair context, the compile gate filters invalid edits, and the test-driven gate checks target-test recovery before full regression. Across four benchmarks, EviACT improves resolve rate over the strongest reported comparable baselines by 1.6-6.0 percentage points and shows 70.1-88.6% lower reported per-bug API cost where baseline costs are available. Ablations and diagnostics suggest that these gains are associated with the coordinated evidence-to-action chain, making agentic APR more effective and efficient.

</details>


### [143] [EdgeFlow: Edge-Map Augmented VLM-Based Flowchart Processing for Industrial Requirements Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27332)
*Zhifei Dou,Shabnam Hassani,Ou Wei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: EdgeFlow通过向VLM输入添加Canny边缘检测图作为结构先验，无需训练即可显著提升工业流程图中拓扑关键细节的转换质量，在真实工业数据集上节点F1提升17.39%、边F1提升16.94%，但合成数据集上无显著改进，揭示了工业数据基准的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM直接将流程图转换为机器可读模型时，常丢失拓扑关键视觉细节（如连接线、分支结构），且缺乏标注训练数据，需要一种无需微调或标注数据的即用型改进方法。

Method: 提出EdgeFlow框架，在VLM原始输入基础上，叠加Canny边缘检测算法确定性提取的边缘图作为结构先验，引导VLM关注拓扑关键信息，实现流程图到Mermaid代码的转换，无需标注数据或领域微调。

Result: 在工业真实数据集IndusReqFlow上，相比原始VLM：节点级F1提高17.39个百分点，边级F1提高16.94个百分点，路径级F1提高11.06个百分点。在公开合成基准上无显著改进。

Conclusion: EdgeFlow提供了一种无需训练、实用的拓扑保持流程图转换方案；同时指出当前合成基准无法充分评估工业场景下的VLM-RE工具，亟需包含工业数据的多样化基准。

Abstract: Flowcharts are widely used in industrial requirements, but usually remain embedded as static images. Vision Language Models (VLMs) show promise in the conversion of these flowcharts into machine-readable models for RE activities, yet, when directly applied to flowchart conversion, they often fail on topology-critical visual details. To address this, we propose EdgeFlow that augments a VLM's original input with a deterministically extracted Canny edge map-acting as a structural prior-to improve flowchart-to-Mermaid conversion, without requiring annotated training data or domain-specific model fine-tuning.
  We evaluate EdgeFlow on IndusReqFlow, a dataset sourced from real-world requirements. Compared with off-the-shelf VLMs, EdgeFlow improves node-level F1 by 17.39 percentage points and edge-level F1 by 16.94 percentage points. At the path level, EdgeFlow improves path F1 by 11.06 percentage points, enabling better support for model-based testing. These results demonstrate that EdgeFlow provides a practical, training-free means to improve topology-preserving flowchart-to-Mermaid conversion for industrial RE. Cross-dataset evaluation results on a public synthetic benchmark show no significant improvement; this highlights the need for diverse benchmarks incorporating industrial data for the comprehensive evaluation of future VLM-based RE tools.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [MemMorph: Tool Hijacking in LLM Agents via Memory Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26154)
*Xuanye Zhang,Yongsen Zheng,Zhuqin Xu,Kaiyu Zhou,Bowen Shen,Haoran Ou,Tianwei Zhang,Kwok-Yan Lam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MemMorph，首个通过毒化智能体长期记忆来偏置工具选择的攻击方法，仅需注入3条记录即可达到85.9%的攻击成功率，揭示了长期记忆是工具增强型智能体中一个关键且被低估的攻击面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有攻击主要操纵工具元数据，容易被检测且随着智能体采用记忆模块而失效；长期记忆作为智能体决策的关键组件尚未被充分研究其安全风险。

Method: MemMorph通过注入伪装成技术事实、事故报告和操作策略的少量精心构造记录，毒化智能体的长期记忆，从而间接重塑其上下文感知和决策过程，使智能体自主推断并选择攻击者偏好的工具。

Result: 在3个基准、10个智能体骨干和3种记忆模块实现上，仅注入3条记录即可达到85.9%的攻击成功率，比最强基线高出25%，且在3种代表性防御下仍保持有效性。

Conclusion: 长期记忆是工具增强型智能体中一个关键且被低估的攻击面，亟需发展记忆层面的完整性防护机制。

Abstract: LLM-driven agents are capable of selecting external tools to complete users' tasks. However, attackers could compromise such process, steering agents toward inappropriate/wrong tools and enabling malicious actions. Most existing attacks primarily manipulate the tool metadata, which is easily detectable by auditing and may lose effectiveness as modern agents increasingly adopt memory modules to refine tool selection policies through accumulated experience. This paper proposes MemMorph, the first attack that bias tool selection by poisoning the agent's long-term memory. Rather than explicitly dictating the tool invocation decision, MemMorph injects a small number of crafted records that are disguised as technical facts, incident reports, and operational policies. These poisoned records reshape the agent's contextual perception and decision-making process, leading it to autonomously infer and select the tool preferred by the attacker. Experiments across 3 benchmarks, 10 agent backbones, and 3 memory-module implementations show that MemMorph achieves up to 85.9% attack success rate with only three injected records, outperforming the strongest baseline by up to 25% while retaining potency under 3 representative defenses. Our findings expose long-term memory as a critical and under-explored attack surface in tool-augmented agents, urging the development of memory-level integrity safeguards.

</details>


### [145] [Turning Bias into Bugs: Bandit-Guided Style Manipulation Attacks on LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26156)
*Xianglin Yang,Bryan Hooi,Gelei Deng,Tianwei Zhang,Jin Song Dong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出BITE框架，一种黑盒对抗攻击方法，通过学习语义保持的文本风格编辑来误导LLM法官，人为抬高评分。实验表明该方法在多种任务上攻击成功率超65%，且能规避检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM法官存在已知的风格偏见（如偏好冗长或特定句式），但这一特性作为安全漏洞尚未被充分探索。作者旨在揭示并利用这一弱点，展示LLM法官评估范式的根本性缺陷。

Method: BITE将风格编辑选择建模为上下文赌博机问题，使用LinUCB策略在无模型参数/梯度访问的黑盒条件下，自适应选择最大化法官评分的语义保持编辑。

Result: 在多种LLM法官和任务（包括聊天机器人排行榜和AI审稿基准的点式/成对比较）上，攻击成功率超65%，评分提升1-2分（9分制），并能规避风格控制和多种检测基线。

Conclusion: LLM-as-a-judge范式存在根本性安全弱点，亟需开发鲁棒的、能感知攻击的评估方法。

Abstract: The known stylistic biases in LLM judges, such as a preference for verbosity or specific sentence structures, present an underexplored security vulnerability. In this work, we introduce BITE (BIas exploraTion and Exploitation), a black-box adversarial framework that learns semantics-preserving edits to mislead an LLM judge and artificially inflate the scores it assigns. We cast the selection of stylistic edits as a contextual bandit problem and use a LinUCB policy to adaptively choose edits that maximize the judge's score without access to model parameters or gradients. Empirically, we test BITE across a diverse range of LLM judges and tasks, including both pointwise and pairwise comparisons on chatbot leaderboards and AI-reviewer benchmarks. BITE achieves an attack success rate exceeding 65% and raises scores by 1-2 points on a 9-point scale, all while preserving semantic equivalence. We further assess the attack's stealthiness, showing that BITE evades standard style-control methods and several detection baselines. Our findings expose a fundamental weakness in the LLM-as-a-judge paradigm and motivate robust, attack-aware evaluation. Our code is available at https://github.com/xianglinyang/llm-as-a-judge-attack.

</details>


### [146] [Furina: Fragmented Uncertainty-Driven Refusal Instability Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26158)
*Tongxi Wu,Jian Zhang,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型和多模态大语言模型的安全对齐并非接近二元的阈值机制，而是存在一个不稳定区域，其中微小扰动会导致随机的拒绝决策。作者提出了多指标诊断框架来表征这一不稳定性，并基于此设计了Furina越狱攻击，通过碎片化、场景锚定的提示诱导该特征，无需模型特定优化即可实现有效攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战现有研究中认为安全对齐作为近似二元阈值机制运作的假设，揭示其真实的不稳定性特征，并解释为何基于检测的防御在高阶攻击面前失效。

Method: 提出结合外部信号与内部信号的多指标诊断框架，识别不稳定区域的特征：输出不确定性升高而内部安全激活降低的"解耦"现象。基于此设计Furina越狱攻击，通过碎片化、场景锚定的提示来刻意诱导该特征，无需模型特定优化。

Result: Furina在HarmBench上优于强单轮和多轮基线方法，在MM-SafetyBench上取得有竞争力的结果，证明了不确定性放大机制的有效性与可迁移性。

Conclusion: 不确定性放大为理解安全脆弱性提供了原理性的、可迁移的机制，揭示了现有检测防御的根本局限。

Abstract: Safety alignment in large language models (LLMs) and multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is commonly assumed to operate as a near-binary threshold mechanism. We challenge this assumption by revealing that safety behavior is governed by an instability region where small perturbations induce stochastic refusal decisions rather than deterministic outcomes. We develop a multi-metric diagnostic framework combining external and internal signals to characterize this instability. Through systematic experiments, we identify a characteristic diagnostic signature: inputs in unstable regimes exhibit elevated output uncertainty yet decreased internal safety activation, a decoupling phenomenon that explains why detection-based defenses fail against sophisticated attacks. Building on this framework, we introduce Furina, a jailbreak attack that deliberately induces this signature through fragmented, scene-anchored prompts without model-specific optimization. Furina outperforms strong single-turn and multi-turn baselines on HarmBench and achieves competitive results on MM-SafetyBench, demonstrating that uncertainty amplification provides a principled and transferable mechanism for understanding safety vulnerabilities. Code is available at: https://github.com/0xCavaliers/Furina_Jailbreak.

</details>


### [147] [Risk Averse Alert Prioritization for IDS Using Subnormal Gaussian Fuzzy Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27299)
*Murat Moran*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出基于次正态高斯模糊数的告警优先级排序框架，通过模糊数核心（严重性）、扩散度（不确定性）和高度（检测可靠性）三要素建模，结合风险态度参数实现可调优的告警优先级排序，在CIC-IDS2017和NSL-KDD数据集上NDCGrel@100达0.9963，显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代入侵检测系统每日产生数千条告警，告警疲劳（大量误报和低影响事件）严重制约安全运营效率，亟需一种能显式建模多源不确定性（威胁严重性、检测置信度、组织风险态度）的告警优先级排序方法。

Method: 采用次正态高斯模糊数建模告警：核心表示严重性，扩散度表示不确定性，高度表示检测可靠性。通过排序指标进行优先级排序，引入风险态度参数使组织可根据自身安全态势调整排序策略。

Result: 在CIC-IDS2017和NSL-KDD数据集上，检测器退化场景下NDCGrel@100达0.9963（基线0.8215），中等置信度告警区域区分度显著，鲁棒检测器场景下与基线性能持平。

Conclusion: 该框架具有理论严谨性、计算高效性、推理可解释性，且在不同检测器家族和校准偏差场景下保持鲁棒。

Abstract: Modern intrusion detection systems generate thousands of alerts daily, but alert fatigue severely limits security operations effectiveness due to too many false positives or low-impact events. We address this by proposing a principled framework for alert prioritization based on subnormal Gaussian fuzzy numbers, explicitly modeling three sources of uncertainty: threat severity, detection confidence, and organizational risk attitude. Each alert is represented as a fuzzy number with the core indicating severity, spread indicating uncertainty, and height reflecting detection reliability. We apply ranking indices to prioritize alerts, allowing organizations to tune security posture through a risk-attitude parameter. Experimental validation on CIC-IDS2017 and NSL-KDD demonstrates greater robustness than baselines under detector degradation (0.9963 vs 0.8215 NDCGrel@100), with distinct differentiation in mid-confidence alerts and near-parity with baselines under robust detectors. The framework is theoretically grounded, computationally efficient, provides interpretable reasoning, and remains robust across detector families and miscalibration scenarios.

</details>


### [148] [Control Physiology: An Agent-Based Model of FAIR-CAM Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26597)
*Jack Jones,Laura Voicu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次将FAIR-CAM理论框架实现为可计算的智能体模型，并通过医院勒索软件场景（N=1000次迭代）揭示了静态分析无法捕捉的三种涌现动力学：操作效能与理论公式存在约17%偏差、修复预算低于阈值时预期损失增加约2.8倍、监控级联失效在VMC拓扑中无声传播。模型以开源形式发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FAIR-CAM提供了控制有效性的理论框架，但一直停留在理论层面，未被转化为可计算、可观测的模型，导致无法动态分析控制退化、监控依赖和预算约束等现实问题。

Method: 构建首个基于智能体的计算模型，包含8种智能体类型、乘法纵深防御敏感性公式、三源方差模型、预算约束修复机制以及叙事因果引擎。在医院勒索软件场景下运行1000次迭代实验。

Result: 三种涌现动力学：(1) 操作效能与FAIR-CAM理论公式偏差约17%，由相关外源方差驱动，随外源频率线性增长；(2) 修复管道排队相变使预算低于阈值时预期损失放大2.8倍；(3) 监控级联失效沿VMC拓扑无声传播，单个退化VMC导致其所管理控制的未检测方差复合累积。

Conclusion: 这些动力学是FAIR-CAM架构的结构性属性，应能泛化到所研究的特定场景之外，表明静态风险分析无法捕捉控制系统的关键涌现行为。

Abstract: Security risk analysis typically treats control effectiveness as a static input, yet controls degrade through configuration drift, depend on monitoring systems that may themselves be degraded, and compete for finite remediation budgets.
