The FAIR Controls Analytics Model (FAIR-CAM) provides the theoretical framework for these dynamics but has so far remained theoretical. We present the first agent-based model to operationalize the core FAIR-CAM dynamics, making control physiology computationally observable, and release the implementation as open source. The simulation implements eight agent types, a multiplicative defense-in-depth susceptibility formula, a three-source variance model, budget-constrained remediation, and a narrative causation engine that produces a complete causal trace for every loss event. In a hospital ransomware scenario (N=1,000 iterations), three organizational dynamics emerge that static analysis cannot represent. First, emergent operational efficacy diverges from the analytical FAIR-CAM formula by approximately 17 percent, driven by correlated extrinsic variance; the divergence grows linearly with extrinsic frequency and vanishes under purely intrinsic drift. Second, a sharp queueing regime transition in the remediation pipeline approximately 2.8x expected loss when budget falls below a scenario-specific threshold (5-10 engineer-hours/month). Third, cascading monitoring failures propagate through the VMC topology: a single degraded VMC silently compounds undetected variance across the controls it manages. These dynamics are structural properties of the FAIR-CAM architecture and should generalize beyond the specific scenario studied.

</details>


### [149] [Context-Aware Metric Differential Privacy for Vehicle Trajectory Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26351)
*Gaoyi Chen,Yan Huang,Chenxi Qiu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出C-mDP框架，通过将上下文信息（如历史移动轨迹）纳入隐私保护和效用模型，并利用条件独立性结构简化线性规划求解，在车辆位置隐私场景中相较于传统mDP在相同隐私预算下获得了更高的效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有度量差分隐私（mDP）机制将每个位置记录孤立处理，未能建模上下文信息（如近期移动历史）对发布数据效用的影响，这一问题在车辆移动轨迹场景中尤为突出，因为服务质量通常依赖于时间上相关的位置序列。

Method: 提出上下文感知的mDP（C-mDP）框架，将受保护的秘密视为上下文增强记录并在该增强域上施加度量不可区分性；将最优C-mDP机制设计形式化为线性规划（LP），最小化期望效用损失；利用当前位置与上下文变量之间的条件独立性结构导出精简的优化公式，大幅减少决策变量和约束数量。

Result: 在真实车辆移动数据集上的实验表明，C-mDP在相同隐私预算下始终比标准mDP基线取得更高的效用，同时满足所需的度量隐私保证。

Conclusion: C-mDP通过将上下文依赖融入隐私定义和效用模型，有效解决了时序相关位置数据的隐私-效用权衡问题，为车辆位置隐私保护提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Metric Differential Privacy (mDP) generalizes differential privacy by allowing privacy guarantees to be expressed with respect to an arbitrary distance metric over secrets. While mDP has been adopted in geo-location protection, most existing mechanisms perturb each location record in isolation and do not model how contextual information (e.g., recent mobility history) affects the utility of the released data. This mismatch is particularly pronounced for vehicle mobility traces, where service quality often depends on temporally correlated locations.
  In this paper, we propose Context-aware mDP (C-mDP), a framework for vehicle location privacy that incorporates contextual dependencies into both the utility model and the privacy notion. C-mDP treats the protected secret as a context-augmented record and enforces metric indistinguishability over this augmented domain. We formulate optimal C-mDP mechanism design as a linear program (LP) that minimizes expected utility loss subject to C-mDP constraints. To improve scalability, we exploit conditional-independence structure between the current location and contextual variables to derive a reduced formulation with substantially fewer decision variables and constraints. We evaluate C-mDP on real-world vehicle mobility datasets and compare it with standard mDP baselines. The results show that C-mDP consistently achieves higher utility under the same privacy budget while satisfying the required metric privacy guarantees.

</details>


### [150] [Jailbreak susceptibility prediction and mitigation via the behavioral geometry of models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26409)
*Hayden Helm,Xiaodong Liu,Weiwei Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文形式化定义了模型群体的行为几何（Behavioral Geometry），利用已评估和防御过的模型，实现对群体内新模型的高效越狱敏感性预测和防御迁移。在79个模型（24个提供商）和100个系统配置上，该方法以约98%更少的探测次数达到0.94的AUPRC，防御迁移比同提供商分配更优（+2%，p=0.03），仅需3个模型即可覆盖整个群体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式系统面临越狱攻击威胁，需要对大量模型配置进行安全评估与防御优化。由于可部署系统数量庞大，对每个配置进行完整的评估和优化在实践中不可行，亟需一种高效的方法来预测模型脆弱性并在模型间迁移防御策略。

Method: 形式化定义模型群体的行为几何（Behavioral Geometry），利用已评估和防御过的模型构建几何空间，支持高效越狱敏感性预测和防御迁移。

Result: 在79个模型（24个提供商）和100个单基础模型的系统配置上，行为几何方法以约98%更少的探测次数达到0.94的AUPRC；利用行为几何选择防御迁移来源比同提供商分配更优（+2%，p=0.03），无需额外探测成本，仅3个模型即可覆盖整个群体；结果对超参数选择和评测者选择具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 行为几何框架能够显著降低评估成本，同时提升防御迁移效果，为大规模模型群体安全部署提供了一种高效、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Evaluating and mitigating a generative system's susceptibility to jailbreak attacks is critical to its safe deployment. Given the number of deployable systems, full per-configuration evaluation and optimization is impractical. In this paper, we formalize the behavioral geometry of a population of models that, by leveraging previously evaluated and defended models, supports both efficient susceptibility prediction and effective defense transfer across a population. We apply the framework to 79 models spanning 24 providers and to 100 system configurations of a single base model. Simple methods that use the behavioral geometry reach an AUPRC of $0.94$ for susceptibility detection with $\approx98\%$ fewer probes relative to a full evaluation. Using the behavioral geometry to select which model to transfer an optimized defense from outperforms same-provider assignment ($+2\%$, $p = 0.03$) at no additional probe cost, with a set of three models sufficient to cover the population. Results are robust to hyperparameter selection and judge.

</details>


### [151] [Beyond Epsilon: A Principled QIF Framework for Local Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26465)
*Ramon G. Gonze,Natasha Fernandes,Heber H. Arcolezi,Catuscia Palamidessi,Nataliia Bielova*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于量化信息流（QIF）和 Blackwell 序的框架，用于系统地比较本地差分隐私（LDP）频率估计协议，揭示了以往认为“最优”的协议在某些情况下实际上可以被其他协议严格支配或不可比较，为 LDP 协议提供了更原则性的、考虑攻击者模型的分类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 LDP 协议的比较缺乏系统性和原则性：仅靠隐私参数 ε（最坏情况区分度）或基于效用的分析（给定 ε 下数据效用排名）都无法反映协议在不同攻击者模型下的安全性差异，亟需一种更全面的比较框架。

Method: 将 LDP 机制建模为概率信道，利用量化信息流（QIF）和 Blackwell 序（精炼序）概念建立协议比较框架。通过对七种主流 LDP 协议（GRR、BLH、OLH、SUE、OUE、THE 等）进行 QIF 分析，使用 Blackwell 序判断协议间的内在优劣关系。

Result: 发现一些先前被认为“最优”的协议实际上与其它协议不可比较（incomparable）或被严格支配（strictly dominated）；Blackwell 序能够区分哪些协议对所有可能的攻击者都更优越，揭示了仅靠 ε 或效用分析无法捕捉的协议间关系。

Conclusion: 该框架弥合了 LDP 与形式化方法社区之间的鸿沟，实现了对本地隐私系统的、考虑攻击者的原则性推理，为未来 LDP 协议设计和评估提供了更严谨的理论基础。

Abstract: Local Differential Privacy (LDP) has become the de facto standard for privacy-preserving data collection in large-scale systems, in particular for the purpose of estimating frequencies. However, the current research landscape lacks a systematic and principled way to compare LDP protocols. The parameter $\varepsilon$ of LDP is considered the measure of privacy, but it only bounds worst-case distinguishability. Other comparisons rely on utility-driven analyses, where mechanisms are ranked based on their ability to preserve data utility for a given privacy budget $\varepsilon$. Both such kinds of comparisons fail to account for the strength of protocols against diverse attacker models. In this paper, we propose a framework for analyzing LDP frequency estimation protocols through the lens of Quantitative Information Flow (QIF). By modeling LDP mechanisms as probabilistic channels, we leverage the concept of refinement (Blackwell ordering) to establish more principled classifications. This approach allows us to determine when one protocol is intrinsically superior to another for all possible adversaries, and to discuss the implications for utility. In particular, our analysis uncovers cases where protocols previously deemed "optimal" are, in fact, incomparable with, or strictly dominated by, other protocols. We provide a formal QIF-based treatment of seven state-of-the-art protocols, including Generalized Randomized Response (GRR), local hashing variants (BLH, OLH), unary encoding schemes (SUE, OUE), and Thresholding with Histogram Encoding (THE). This perspective bridges the gap between the LDP and formal methods communities and enables principled, adversary-aware reasoning about locally private systems.

</details>


### [152] [SEC-bench Pro: Can Language Models Solve Long-Horizon Software Security Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26548)
*Hwiwon Lee,Jiawei Liu,Dongjun Kim,Ziqi Zhang,Chunqiu Steven Xia,Lingming Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出SEC-bench Pro基准，用于评估LLM智能体在真实世界高复杂度软件系统（V8和SpiderMonkey）中的漏洞狩猎能力，包含183个已验证漏洞。评估显示最强前沿模型成功率不足40%，双智能体组合可达48.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准依赖模糊测试工具、目标特定描述或漏洞复现任务，无法真实评估LLM在实际漏洞狩猎场景中的表现，缺乏对高复杂度、关键软件系统的评估。

Method: 通过三阶段流水线（漏洞收集、环境重建、基于oracle的验证）构建基准，包含183个V8和SpiderMonkey的已验证漏洞，覆盖内存安全、沙箱、JIT和竞态条件等类型，并在浏览器级和运行时级条件下评估。

Result: 前沿模型成功率低于40%；Kimi-K2.6在V8上达11.7%；最强配置在V8上达32.0%，在SpiderMonkey上达38.8%；ClaudeCode和Codex双智能体联合在V8上达37.9%，在SpiderMonkey上达48.8%。

Conclusion: SEC-bench Pro为评估基于LLM的安全智能体提供了鲁棒环境，揭示了现有模型在长程漏洞狩猎任务中的局限性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) now support automated software security tasks, including vulnerability discovery and proof-of-concept (PoC) generation. Existing benchmarks do not faithfully evaluate LLMs in real-world bug hunting scenarios because they rely on fuzzing harnesses, target-specific descriptions, or vulnerability-reproduction tasks. We present SEC-bench Pro, a benchmark for measuring agent bug hunting on critical, high-complexity software systems. This work discloses reports with concrete PoC inputs and links fixes into reproducible tasks through a three-phase pipeline for vulnerability collection, environment reconstruction, and oracle-based validation. We instantiate SEC-bench Pro with 183 validated vulnerabilities across V8 and SpiderMonkey, including a V8 subset with more than $1.5 million in cumulative Google Vulnerability Reward Program awards. These instances span memory-safety, sandbox, JIT, and race-condition bugs under browser-grade and runtime-grade execution conditions. Our evaluation shows that coding agents with frontier models remain below 40% success on both evaluated engines. The open-weight Kimi-K2.6 baseline reaches 11.7% on V8, while the strongest frontier configuration reaches 32.0% on V8 and 38.8% on SpiderMonkey. ClaudeCode and Codex solve complementary instance sets, and their two-agent union reaches 37.9% on V8 and 48.8% on SpiderMonkey. SEC-bench Pro provides robust environments for assessing LLM-based security agents and exposes limitations in long-horizon bug hunting tasks.

</details>


### [153] [GradSentry: Gradient Spectral Entropy for Backdoor Sample Filtering in Large Language Model Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26574)
*Haodong Zhao,Tianyi Xu,Tianhang Zhao,Zhuosheng Zhang,Gongshen Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GradSentry是一种基于梯度谱熵的后门样本过滤方法，通过监测每个样本梯度的谱熵来检测投毒样本，无需聚类和配对样本比较，适用于各种微调方式，在各种投毒比例下均有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用不可信数据微调大语言模型时存在后门攻击风险，现有基于聚类的样本过滤方法需要足够数据且在极端投毒比例下容易失效，因此需要一种更鲁棒的后门样本检测方法。

Method: GradSentry利用每个样本梯度的谱熵作为检测指标：投毒样本产生的梯度具有比干净样本更高的谱熵。方法通过计算每个样本梯度的谱特征来捕捉改变输出的后门信号，避免了配对样本比较和聚类步骤。该方法与训练方式无关，支持LoRA和全参数微调。

Result: 在四个QA数据集和四种攻击类型上的评估验证了谱熵对后门检测的有效性。GradSentry在所有投毒比例（1%-90%）下均有效，计算开销低（7B模型上每个样本仅需20-50毫秒）。

Conclusion: GradSentry是一种高效、鲁棒且训练无关的后门样本过滤方法，通过梯度谱熵有效检测后门样本，无需聚类，适用于多种微调范式，为LLM安全微调提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Fine-tuning Large Language Models with untrusted data exposes models to backdoor attacks, where poisoned samples cause targeted misbehavior. Existing sample-filtering defenses rely on clustering, which requires sufficient data and can fail at extreme poison ratios. We propose GradSentry ({Grad}ient {Sentry}), a backdoor sample filtering method based on the spectral entropy of per-sample gradients. Our key finding is that poisoned samples produce gradients with higher spectral entropy compared to clean samples. GradSentry captures output-altering backdoor signatures using per-sample gradient spectra, avoiding pairwise sample comparisons and clustering during feature construction. Importantly, our method is training-agnostic: it works for both parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods like LoRA and full-parameter tuning, as the gradient analysis operates independently of which parameters are being updated during training. GradSentry requires no clustering, operates effectively across all poison ratios (1%--90%), and introduces minimal computational overhead (20-50ms per sample for 7B model). Evaluation on four QA datasets and four attack types demonstrates the effectiveness of spectral entropy for backdoor detection. Code is available at https://github.com/dongdongzhaoUP/GradSentry.

</details>


### [154] [Cordyceps: Covert Control Attacks on LLMs via Data Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26595)
*Zedian Shao,Charles Fleming,Teodora Baluta*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的数据投毒方法——隐蔽控制攻击（Covert Control Attacks），通过语义关联在LLM中诱导信息隐藏方案，使模型能对任意恶意指令进行编码和解码，从而绕过现有防御机制。实验表明，该方法在5个LLM、3种后门防御和4种提示注入防御下，攻击成功率高达93%-98%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对LLM的投毒攻击依赖固定触发短语，容易被异常检测、干净数据正则化或在线监控等防御手段识别和中和，缺乏隐蔽且鲁棒的攻击方法。

Method: 通过语义关联（如事实或概念与攻击者选择的短语之间的关联）教导LLM一种可靠且隐蔽的信息隐藏方案，实现对任意恶意指令的编码与解码，从而实施隐蔽控制攻击。

Result: 在5个LLM上，少量投毒即可使攻击成功率比启发式提示注入攻击相对提高约40%；后门防御下攻击成功率高达93%，提示注入防御下达98%。

Conclusion: 隐蔽控制攻击揭示了LLM微调中一种新的、难以防御的投毒诱导漏洞，现有检测和微调防御手段难以有效抵御。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are often fine-tuned on uncurated text datasets that adversaries can poison. Existing poisoning attacks primarily rely on fixed trigger phrases that defenses such as outlier detection, clean-data regularization, or online monitoring can neutralize. In this paper, we propose a data poisoning method that teaches an LLM an information hiding scheme reliably and stealthily through semantic associations between shared knowledge such as facts or concepts and attacker-chosen phrases. The induced hiding scheme can encode and decode arbitrary malicious instructions, thus revealing a new and subtle poisoning-induced vulnerability: covert control attacks.
  We precisely characterize covert control attacks and evaluate them across $5$ LLMs, $3$ backdoor defenses, and $4$ prompt injection defenses. With a small poisoned fraction, covert control attacks outperform heuristic-based prompt injection attacks in average attack success rate by about $40\%$ relative to clean fine-tuned models. They also circumvent defenses based on detection and fine-tuning, maintaining up to $93\%$ attack success rate after backdoor defenses and up to $98\%$ after prompt injection defenses.

</details>


### [155] [Batch Me If You Can: Coverage-guided RPKI Fuzzing at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26651)
*Haya Schulmann,Niklas Vogel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CAT的新型模糊测试工具，通过连续采样、函数侧信道代码覆盖率归因和结构/语义变异等技术，解决了RPKI软件因多对象加密仓库复杂性而难以被现有模糊测试器有效测试的问题，实现了66倍吞吐量提升，发现了21个先前未知的漏洞并已分配8个CVE（CVSS 7.5-9.8）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RPKI对域间路由安全至关重要，但其软件缺乏充分测试；现有模糊测试器（如AFL++、libFuzzer）假设单输入执行模型，无法处理RPKI仓库中数百个相互依赖、加密关联的对象，缺乏多对象仓库的精确覆盖率归因能力，导致反馈驱动探索失效，遗漏严重漏洞。

Method: 提出连续采样技术、利用函数作为侧信道实现每对象覆盖率归因，将输入解析为带标签树以支持结构和语义变异同时保持加密有效性，实现非顺序模糊测试与模板无关的ASN.1变异引擎。

Result: CAT相比顺序模糊测试实现66倍吞吐量提升，比libFuzzer和已有工作多探索24-47%的独特代码路径；在RPKI验证器中发现21个未知漏洞，8个已分配CVE（CVSS 7.5-9.8），包括缓冲区溢出、拒绝服务和可利用的仓库投毒逻辑缺陷。

Conclusion: CAT有效解决了复杂加密协议的多对象模糊测试难题，并已开源以支持可重复性、进一步研究及将方法适配至DNSSEC、TLS等其他协议。

Abstract: The Resource Public Key Infrastructure (RPKI) has become essential to secure inter-domain routing. Despite its critical role, RPKI software remains largely untested beyond shallow parsing. Existing fuzzers, like AFL++ or libFuzzer, do not work well for RPKI as they assume a single, self-contained input per execution, while RPKI repositories contain hundreds of interdependent cryptographically linked objects. Existing fuzzers fail to handle this complexity and lack the ability for precise coverage attribution in multi-object repositories, breaking feedback-based exploration and thereby missing most severe vulnerabilities in RPKI validation. In this paper, we overcome these limitations through novel fuzzing techniques, including continuous sampling and using functions as side-channels for per-object coverage attribution in large input repositories. We further show how parsing inputs to a labeled tree allows structural and semantic mutations while preserving cryptographic validity in mutated repositories. We implement our new techniques into a powerful fuzzing tool called CAT, combining non-sequential fuzzing with our template-agnostic ASN.1 mutation engine to achieve 66x throughput improvement over sequential fuzzing and exploring 24 - 47% more unique code paths compared to libFuzzer and previous work. Evaluating CAT on RPKI validators uncovered 21 previously unknown vulnerabilities with 8 CVEs already assigned (CVSS 7.5 - 9.8). These include a buffer overflow, Denial-of-Service (DoS), and exploitable repository-poisoning logic flaws. We open-source CAT to enable reproducibility, further research, and adaptation of our methods to other complex cryptography-based protocols such as DNSSEC and TLS.

</details>


### [156] [Resolving the Correct Library: A Loader-Level Defense Solution Against Shared Object Hijacking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26665)
*Can Ozkan,Dave Singelee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于加载器的验证框架，通过结合路径绑定和Build-ID的身份模型与密码学哈希，确保动态链接器解析的共享库是预期且可信的，从而有效防御Linux环境（包括嵌入式Linux）中的共享库劫持攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御措施（如加固/替换加载器、控制流完整性、文件完整性机制）均未验证加载器实际解析的共享对象是否为预期且可信的库，存在关键空白。

Method: 设计基于加载器的验证框架，支持路径绑定和基于Build-ID（位置无关）的身份模型，结合密码学哈希验证，在glibc上实现，并在通用Linux（Ubuntu）和嵌入式Linux（Buildroot）环境下评估。

Result: 该机制在通用Linux和嵌入式Linux环境下均能有效阻止共享库劫持攻击。

Conclusion: 共享库劫持本质上是加载器解析的真实性问题，所提框架通过强制加载器解析过程的真实性保证，填补了现有防御的空白。

Abstract: Shared library hijacking attacks in the Linux ecosystem, including embedded Linux, are a significant concern. It fundamentally exploits the dynamic linker's library-resolution semantics rather than modifying trusted libraries directly. Prior research has extensively analyzed attack vectors exploiting environment variables, embedded search paths, and dynamic loader internals, demonstrating that hijacking is rooted in fundamental loader behavior rather than isolated misconfigurations. Existing defenses either harden or replace the loader, enforce control-flow integrity after libraries are loaded, or apply file-centric integrity mechanisms such as signatures and measurement frameworks. However, these approaches fail to address a critical gap: none verify whether the shared object actually resolved by the loader is the intended and trusted one.
  In this paper, we argue that shared library hijacking is fundamentally a loader-resolution authenticity problem and present a loader-centric verification framework that enforces authenticity guarantees for the dynamic linker's resolution process. Our design supports both path-bound and location-independent (i.e., Build-ID-based) identity models combined with cryptographic hashing. We implement our approach on GNU libc (glibc) systems and evaluate it on both general-purpose Linux (e.g., Ubuntu) and embedded Linux (e.g., Buildroot) environments under emulation. Our results demonstrate that our proposed mechanism indeed prevents shared library hijacking attacks.

</details>


### [157] [Anonymous YARA Rules Are Not Anonymous](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26791)
*Usman Rabiu Isah,Laurent Bobelin,Pascal Berthomé*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统性地证明，即便移除元数据，YARA规则文本本身仍可被用于识别作者身份（76%准确率）、来源仓库（99%准确率）及目标恶意软件家族（95%准确率），揭示规则共享行为存在可量化的OPSEC攻击面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑并验证"移除元数据即可保护组织身份"这一隐含假设的可靠性，评估YARA规则共享实践中的信息泄露风险。

Method: 构建包含23,305条规则的三类公共仓库语料库，采用三种互补方法（词汇n-gram的Burrows' Delta、句法AST特征的Caliskan-Islam、微调CodeBERT），沿四个文体指纹维度（作者、来源仓库、恶意软件家族、时序漂移）训练独立分类器。

Result: 仓库归属识别准确率高达99%，作者重识别达76%，恶意软件家族分类达95%。全历史与时间受限子集在仓库归属任务上存在9-18%准确率差距，表明存在时序漂移。在控制恶意软件家族相同的条件下，7个家族中有5个仍可重识别作者（平均74.6%）。

Conclusion: 首次系统证明YARA规则共享构成可量化的OPSEC攻击面，仅移除元数据不足以缓解该风险。

Abstract: YARA rules are widely shared across threat intelligence communities to enable collective defence against malware. This practice implicitly assumes that removing metadata (e.g., author fields) sufficiently protects the identity of contributing organisations. To assess the validity of this assumption, we systematically evaluate how much can be inferred from YARA rule text alone. Specifically, using a corpus of 23,305 rules from three major public repositories, we train independent classifiers along four stylometric fingerprint dimensions: individual author, source repository, malware family, and temporal drift, using three complementary methods: lexical n-grams (Burrows' Delta), syntactic AST features (Caliskan-Islam), and fine-tuned CodeBERT. Our results demonstrate that repository origin is almost perfectly recoverable (up to 99% accuracy), individual authors can be re-identified well above chance (76%), and malware family classification reaches 95%. Comparing the same repository attribution task across full-history and time-restricted subsets reveals a 9-18% accuracy gap, providing preliminary evidence of temporal drift in repository fingerprints.To further disentangle content from style, we conduct per-malware family author attribution experiments. Even when the malware family is the same for all samples considered, authors can still be re-identified for five of seven tested families (mean accuracy 74.6%).
  These findings constitute the first systematic demonstration that YARA rule sharing is a measurable OPSEC attack surface, and that metadata removal alone does not mitigate it.

</details>


### [158] [Privacy-Preserving Screening for Record Linkage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26882)
*Chenyu Huang,Fan Zhang,Huangxun Chen,Yongjun Zhao,Huaming Rao,Peng Chen,Danqing Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Screening-then-Linkage 框架，通过轻量级筛选阶段（PPRS）解决两方隐私保护记录链接（PPRL）的可扩展性问题。基于电路-PSI 的系统 Appraisal 实现了安全高效的 PPRS，设计了 oblivious attribute/feature alignment 协议，相比传统方法通信成本降低 14 倍，比现有最佳 PPRS 系统多处理 850 倍数据，比最佳 PPRL 快 165 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大数据与机器学习时代数据协作至关重要，但须遵守法规约束。两方隐私保护记录链接（PPRL）可评估协作价值并保证数据隐私安全，然而其巨大的计算和通信开销阻碍了在拥有大量潜在协作者的数据市场中的实际应用。

Method: 提出 Screening-then-Linkage 框架，在资源密集的 PPRL 阶段之前引入轻量级筛选阶段（PPRS）。设计基于电路-PSI 的 Appraisal 系统，并提出 oblivious attribute/feature alignment 协议，该协议支持更广泛的比较函数，且比传统协议通信效率高 14 倍。

Result: Appraisal 在相同约束下可处理比现有最佳 PPRS 系统 SFour 多 850 倍的数据记录，比最佳 PPRL 快 165 倍。Oblivious attribute/feature alignment 协议将通信成本降低 14 倍。

Conclusion: Screening-then-Linkage 框架显著减少了从大量候选者中识别最有价值协作者所需的计算时间。Appraisal 在安全性、有效性和效率方面均表现优异。

Abstract: In an era dominated by big data and machine learning, establishing valuable data collaboration has never been more critical. However, such collaborations must operate under regulatory and legal constraints. Two-party Privacy-Preserving Record Linkage (PPRL) emerges to assess the potential collaboration value and also ensure the privacy and security of the involved data. Nevertheless, the substantial computational and communication overheads associated with PPRL hinder its practical adoption in data markets with numerous potential collaborators. Therefore, we present the Screening-then-Linkage framework, which incorporates a lightweight Screening phase prior to the resource-intensive PPRL phase, i.e., PPRS, to mitigate the scalability issue of PPRL. We propose a circuit-PSI-based system, named Appraisal to realize a secure, effective, and efficient PPRS. To reconcile the approximate matching and/or schema-aware setting required in PPRS with the limitations of the circuit-PSI supporting only symmetric functions, we propose a more communication-efficient secure permutation, i.e., Oblivious Attribute/Feature Alignment protocol tailored for PPRS. This protocol supports a broader range of comparison functions and significantly improves efficiency, i.e., reducing communication costs by a factor of 14 compared to the conventional protocol. Our rigorous analysis and comprehensive empirical evaluations demonstrate the security, effectiveness, and efficiency of Appraisal. Appraisal can accommodate up to $850\times$ more records than the SOTA PPRS system, SFour, within the same constraints. Moreover, it is $165 \times$ faster than SOTA PPRL, indicating the Screening-then-Linkage framework substantially decreases the computation time required to identify the most valuable collaborators from a large pool of candidates.

</details>


### [159] [Practical Anonymous Two-Party Gradient Boosting Decision Tree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26903)
*Huang Chenyu,Zhang Fan,Du Minxin,Chow Sherman SM,Chen Huangxun,Rao Huaming,Huang Danqing,Qian Bo,Chen Peng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种匿名梯度提升决策树（GBDT）训练协议，通过双电路PSI和混淆可编程伪随机函数（OPPRF）在保护记录标识符（ID）隐私的前提下，实现垂直分割数据上的高效安全训练，同时将密文打包开销减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于PSI的安全GBDT训练存在ID泄露风险，而通用电路PSI成本过高。需要在不暴露共享ID的前提下实现高效的垂直分割数据训练。

Method: 提出双电路PSI设计，使双方交替作为接收者执行pick-then-sum操作；利用混淆可编程伪随机函数传播电路PSI输出作为共享状态；通过避免全局对齐解决ID隐藏成本随域大小扩展的问题；将密文打包成本减半以优化同态加密效率。

Result: 实验表明，该协议在效率上与存在ID泄露的已有方法具有竞争力；实现了ID隐藏聚合功能，可扩展到其他垂直分割分析场景。

Conclusion: 首次实现了匿名GBDT训练，在不泄露记录标识符的前提下达到了实用效率，为垂直分割数据上的隐私保护机器学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: Structured data is well handled by gradient-boosted decision trees (GBDT), which are usually trained on vertically partitioned features across mutually distrustful parties. High speed and interpretability make GBDTs popular in finance and healthcare, where neural networks may fall short. Enabling secure computation for GBDTs poses unique challenges, requiring secure record alignment for comparison. Relying on private set intersection (PSI) is a de facto approach. Mistaking PSI for a safety measure actually exposes which record identifiers (IDs) are shared between the datasets. Although circuit-PSI could help, it is costly for generic uses. New ideas are needed to efficiently train in a "dark forest". Aiming to hide the IDs, we initiate the study of anonymous GBDT training on split data held by two parties. Dual circuit-PSI in our design lets the parties alternate as receiver to run pick-then-sum over local features. Via oblivious programmable pseudorandom functions, we propagate circuit-PSI outputs as shared state across runs. Avoiding universal alignment, we resolve the neglected dilemma that ID hiding incurs a cost that scales with domain size. Next, we halve the cost of ciphertext packing used to convert single-instruction multiple-data homomorphic encryption from (ring) learning with errors in prior secure GBDT (Usenix Security' 23) and related secure machine-learning computations. Comparative experiments show our protocol remains competitive with leaky approaches in efficiency. Enabling ID-hiding aggregation, our techniques can extend to other vertically partitioned analytics.

</details>


### [160] [The Fault in Our Drafts: Vulnerabilities in RPKI Specification and Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26986)
*Oliver Jacobsen,Tobias Kirsch,Haya Schulmann,Niklas Vogel,Michael Waidner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次全面分析了RPKI RFC规范缺陷与其实现及部署漏洞之间的因果关系，通过差异化模糊测试、互联网范围爬取和验证日志分析，发现61个验证行为不一致问题，其中23个直接源于RFC缺陷，并识别出两个被分配CVE的新漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RPKI作为保障互联网路由安全的关键基础设施，其规范分布在数十个RFC中，但规范本身的模糊、冲突或不完整要求如何影响实际实现和部署的安全性尚未被系统研究。

Method: 结合差异化模糊测试（对主要RPKI实现进行）、互联网范围爬取和验证日志分析，将实际漏洞追溯至有缺陷的RFC需求。

Result: 发现61个验证行为不一致问题，23个直接源于RFC缺陷，识别出2个被分配CVE的新漏洞。

Conclusion: RPKI安全问题本质上是规范编写、解释和实现中的系统性缺陷，而非孤立的编码错误。提出具体改进建议并引入新型告警服务来实时监控RPKI部署中的不一致性。

Abstract: The Resource Public Key Infrastructure (RPKI) secures the Internet's routing system by defining a complex trust and validation framework for certificates, Route Origin Authorizations (ROAs), manifests, and Certificate Revocation Lists (CRLs). These mechanisms are specified across dozens of RFCs. This paper presents the first comprehensive analysis of the causal link between flaws in RPKI Requests for Comments (RFCs) and vulnerabilities in implementations and real-world deployments. We reveal how vague, conflicting, or underspecified requirements in 50 RPKI RFCs propagate into inconsistent implementation behavior and operational failures. We conduct the first large-scale, impact-driven evaluation of RPKI specifications. Our methodology combines differential fuzzing of major RPKI implementations with Internet-wide crawling and validation log analysis, enabling us to trace practical vulnerabilities back to flawed RFC requirements. We uncover 61 previously undocumented inconsistencies in validation behavior, trace 23 directly to RFC flaws, and identify two novel vulnerabilities that were assigned CVEs. Our findings reveal that these are not isolated coding errors but rather systemic issues inherent in how RPKI standards are written, interpreted, and implemented. To mitigate these threats, we propose concrete recommendations and introduce a novel alerting service that monitors and reports live inconsistencies in RPKI deployments. Our open-source datasets, code, and tools support reproducibility and further research.

</details>


### [161] [Lessons from Penetration Tests on Large-Scale Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27042)
*Kevin Eykholt,Dhilung Kirat,Xiaokui Shu,Jiyong Jang,Frederico Araujo,Ian Molloy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过对2025年两款专有智能体产品的渗透测试，评估了AI智能体的安全态势是否有所改善，发现尽管采用了更严格的开发标准，专有智能体系统仍存在与开源系统类似的安全脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统自主性和执行能力增强，安全漏洞数量持续上升，但许多漏洞并非全新，而是反映了以往计算系统中长期存在的弱点类别。现有研究主要关注开源智能体和框架的漏洞，尚不清楚专有智能体系统是否表现出类似的安全弱点。

Method: 对2025年两款专有智能体产品进行渗透测试，评估其安全态势相较于测试前是否有改善。

Result: 专有智能体系统尽管在更严格的编码标准和正式审查流程下开发，仍存在与开源系统类似的安全脆弱性。

Conclusion: 专有智能体系统的安全性并未因开发标准更严格而显著优于开源系统，AI智能体的安全态势仍有待改善。

Abstract: As AI systems gain increasing autonomy and execution capability, the number of discovered security vulnerabilities continues to rise. However, many of these vulnerabilities are not fundamentally novel, but instead reflect recurring classes of weaknesses long observed in prior computing systems. Execution-capable AI agents are effectively unbounded, self-modifying programs that interact extensively with multiple layers of the computing stack. This broad interaction surface imposes a significant security burden on developers, who must reason about and secure complex cross-layer behaviors. Prior research has primarily focused on vulnerabilities in open-source agents and agent frameworks. In contrast, it remains unclear whether proprietary agent systems -- developed under stricter coding standards and formal review processes -- exhibit similar security weaknesses. In this paper, we present findings from two penetration tests conducted in 2025 against proprietary agent products and evaluate whether the security posture of AI agents has improved since these assessments.

</details>


### [162] [BAIT: Boundary-Guided Disclosure Escalation via Self-Conditioned Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27110)
*Xuan Luo,Yue Wang,Geng Tu,Jing Li,Ruifeng Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出BAIT三步越狱框架，通过让模型自我识别保护边界、细化边界、提供详细示例，利用模型自身推理和一致性倾向绕开安全限制，在多个基准上对顶级LLM实现高攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越狱攻击多依赖外部扰动或对抗性提示，尚未充分利用模型自身推理过程中暴露内部信息来绕过安全限制的机制。

Method: 三步框架：(1)要求模型识别保护边界；(2)要求模型细化该边界；(3)要求提供详细示例。每一步基于前一步的回应扩展，利用模型的自洽性和推理连贯性逐步突破安全防线。

Result: 在AdvBench、JailbreakBench、AIR-Bench、SORRY-Bench四个基准上对顶级LLM持续取得强攻击成功率，显著超过传统越狱基线。

Conclusion: 预防性框架显著优于直接知识请求；细化步骤对披露升级至关重要；前两步有一定概率引发有害内容且几乎不触发过滤机制。

Abstract: In this work, we propose BAIT (Boundary-Aware Iterative Trap), a three-step jailbreak framework that approaches malicious goals through internal disclosure. BAIT first asks the model to identify the protection boundary, then requires it to refine that boundary, and finally requests a detailed example. By expanding each step upon the model's previous responses, BAIT turns the model's own reasoning and consistency tendency into a disclosure pathway. Experiments on AdvBench, JailbreakBench, AIR-Bench, and SORRY-Bench demonstrate that BAIT consistently achieves strong attack success rates across top-tier large language models, significantly advancing conventional jailbreak baselines. Further analysis reveals that: 1) prevention-oriented framing significantly outperforms direct knowledge request; 2) the refinement step plays a critical role in disclosure escalation; and 3) the first two steps have a certain chance of eliciting harmful content while triggering little filtering.

</details>


### [163] [Landseer: Exploring the Machine Learning Defense Landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27148)
*Ayushi Sharma,Rosemary Agbozo,Santiago Torres-Arias,Zahra Ghodsi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Landseer是一个模块化框架，用于集成和评估机器学习防御机制。该框架将防御封装为容器化模块，支持自动化实验评估。通过对35种最新防御方法的分析，发现防御家族之间存在可复现性差距，揭示了组合多种防御的挑战与机遇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的ML部署需要同时满足鲁棒性、隐私性和公平性等多重保障，但现有防御方法通常只解决单一风险。防御组合的复杂性及其对性能和安全的影响尚不明确，缺乏系统化评估框架。

Method: 提出Landseer模块化框架：将ML防御封装为容器化模块，支持即插即用；内置评估引擎自动化多指标实验，支持单独和组合评估；对35种最新防御方法进行筛选和可复现性分析。

Result: 识别出35种最新ML防御方法，经可复现性过滤后进行了统一评估；揭示了防御家族间存在可复现性差距，证明了Landseer框架对集成和评估多重防御的有效性。

Conclusion: Landseer为系统化研究和组合ML防御提供了基础框架，揭示了防御组合的挑战与机遇，有助于提高机器学习系统的可靠性。

Abstract: Machine learning systems face diverse threats that undermine robustness, privacy, and fairness. Although many defenses have been proposed, each typically addresses a single risk in isolation. Real-world deployments, however, require these defenses to be composed to meet multiple guarantees simultaneously. The process of composing defenses is complex and not well understood, and its impact on performance and security remains unclear.
  We present Landseer, a modular framework for integrating machine learning (ML) defenses into the ML lifecycle and systematically evaluating their composition. Landseer encapsulates defenses as containerized modules, allowing existing and new techniques to be plugged in with minimal effort. Its evaluation engine automates experiments across multiple metrics, supporting the study of defenses both individually and in combination. In a preliminary study, we identified 35 state-of-the-art machine learning defenses. After filtering for reproducibility, we analyzed their performance using Landseer's unified evaluation process.
  Our findings reveal gaps in replicability across defense families and provide insights into the challenges and opportunities in integrating multiple defenses, establishing a foundation for improving the reliability of machine learning systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [PhyPush: One Push is All You Need for Sensorless Physical Property Estimation with Physics-Guided Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26284)
*Koyo Fujii,Luis Figueredo,Praminda Caleb-Solly,Ivan Boschi,Edoardo Ida',Marco Carricato,Aly Magassouba*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PhyPush框架，仅利用单次推送中机械臂末端执行器的运动学速度数据，结合牛顿第二定律和库伦摩擦模型的物理约束，即可估计物体的质量和摩擦系数，在仿真和真实实验中均优于依赖力传感器的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物体质量与摩擦估计方法大多依赖力/力矩传感器、触觉阵列或多相机运动捕捉等专用硬件，限制了可扩展性和实际部署。本文旨在利用标准机械臂可获取的运动学数据，通过单次推实现低成本、传感器高效的物理属性估计。

Method: 提出PhyPush框架，采用Transformer架构，输入仅包含单次推送中机械臂末端执行器的运动学速度信息；通过物理引导损失函数将牛顿第二定律和库伦摩擦模型作为约束引入模型训练，增强物理一致性和泛化能力。

Result: 在仿真中，相较于可获取完整力信息的基线方法，误差降低超过10%；在真实实验中，优于纯数据驱动损失的方法；在多种域外条件下均实现更准确的质量与摩擦估计。

Conclusion: 物理引导学习使得仅依赖单次推送和运动学数据即可实现低成本、传感器高效的质量与摩擦估计，无需专用力感测硬件，具有良好的可部署性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Accurately estimating object mass and friction is fundamental to achieving reliable and adaptive robotic manipulation. Although interactive perception provides a powerful mechanism for inferring such properties, most existing approaches depend on specialized hardware such as force/torque sensors, tactile arrays, or multi-camera motion-capture systems, limiting scalability and deployment. This paper presents PhyPush, a physics-guided Transformer framework that estimates an object's mass and friction coefficient using only kinematically derived end-effector velocity from a single push. This typically requires data available on standard robotic arms. The model incorporates constraints from Newton's second law and the Coulomb friction model through a physics-guided loss, improving physical consistency and generalization to unseen objects and surfaces. Across diverse simulation and real-world setups, PhyPush consistently achieves more accurate mass and friction estimation in challenging out-of-domain conditions. In simulation, it reduces error by over 10% compared with a baseline that has privileged access to full force information, while in real-world experiments, it outperforms a data-driven loss approach. Overall, the results demonstrate that physics-guided learning can enable low-cost, sensor-efficient estimation of physical properties, relying solely on a single push and readily available kinematic data.

</details>


### [165] [Collaborative Navigation and Exploration with $β$-Sparse Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26304)
*Evangelos Psomiadis,Dipankar Maity,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于β-稀疏高斯过程的框架，使异构机器人（领航机器人和移动传感器机器人）在未知环境中协同导航，传感器可联合选择传输的地图点和导航动作，同时预测未探索区域，在火星和地球地图上实现路径成本降低18%、传输信息减少76%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决异构机器人在未知环境中协同导航时面临的感知、通信和计算限制问题，特别是带宽约束下如何有效传输信息以辅助导航。

Method: 提出β-稀疏高斯过程用于任务感知的诱导点选择；开发了平衡任务相关性与探索的动作选择策略；传感器在线联合选择传输的地图点和导航动作，同时预测未探索区域。

Result: 在火星和地球地图上的仿真结果显示，相比无通信基线，路径成本降低18%；相比原始数据传输基线，传输信息减少76%。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效在带宽约束下实现异构机器人协同导航，大幅降低通信需求的同时提升导航效率。

Abstract: Collaborative navigation of heterogeneous robots in unknown environments poses significant challenges due to sensing, communication, and computational limitations. In this work, a lead robot navigates toward a target while a mobile sensor robot (e.g., a drone) assists by transmitting information about its locally observed environment under bandwidth constraints. We propose a framework that enables the sensor to jointly select its transmitted map points and navigation actions online, while also predicting unexplored regions of the environment. To this end, we present $β$-Sparse Gaussian Processes, a novel and robust variational sparse Gaussian Process model for task-aware inducing point selection. Furthermore, we develop an action-selection strategy that balances task relevance with exploration. Simulations on Mars and Earth maps show that the framework can reduce path cost by 18% relative to no communication and decrease transmitted information by 76% compared to raw-data transmission baselines.

</details>


### [166] [Robust Koopman Control Barrier Filters for Safe Actor-Critic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26452)
*Dhruv S. Kushwaha,Zoleikha A. Biron*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种鲁棒Koopman-CBF SAC框架，通过学习数据驱动的Koopman预测器构造仿射CBF约束，并利用二次规划安全层和投影残差裕度来保证安全，在多种安全控制任务上实现了零约束违反或显著降低违规次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制屏障函数（CBF）在模型无关的强化学习中使用受限，主要因为需要精确的动力学模型和手工设计的屏障证书。现有方法难以在保证安全的同时有效结合无模型RL的灵活性和CBF的形式化安全保障。

Method: 1）从数据中学习有限维Koopman预测器；2）在提升空间（lifted space）中构造仿射CBF约束；3）通过二次规划（QP）安全层施加约束；4）利用投影残差裕度（projected residual margin）收紧CBF条件以补偿有限维近似误差；5）Critic在执行的Safe Action上训练，Actor被正则化朝向Koopman-CBF可行集。

Result: 在CartPole稳定和跟踪任务上实现零约束违反，且回报等于或超过无约束SAC；在Safety Gymnasium高维 locomotion任务中部分设置下降低了违规次数，但也暴露了一阶速度屏障和线性EDMD模型的局限性。

Conclusion: 鲁棒Koopman-CBF滤波器是无模型RL与可证明安全之间的有前景桥梁，但其有效性依赖于特定的结构性条件，未来需要高阶和多步Koopman-CBF扩展。

Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning (RL) for robotic systems requires policies that improve task performance while satisfying state and input constraints during both training and deployment. Control barrier functions (CBFs) provide a principled mechanism for enforcing forward invariance through minimally invasive safety filters, but their use in model-free RL is limited by the need for accurate dynamics and hand-designed barrier certificates. We propose Robust Koopman-CBF SAC, a safety-filtered actor--critic framework that learns a finite-dimensional Koopman predictor from data, constructs affine CBF constraints in the lifted space, and enforces them through a quadratic-program safety layer. To account for finite-dimensional Koopman approximation error, the CBF condition is tightened using a projected residual margin estimated from held-out rollout data. The critic is trained on the executed safe action, while the actor is regularized toward the Koopman-CBF feasible set, reducing dependence on the filter over training. Across safe-control benchmarks, the method achieves zero constraint violations on CartPole stabilization and tracking while matching or exceeding unconstrained SAC returns. On high-dimensional Safety Gymnasium locomotion tasks, the method reduces violations in some settings but also exposes important limitations of first-order velocity barriers and linear EDMD models, motivating high-order and multi-step Koopman-CBF extensions. These results suggest that robust Koopman-CBF filters are a promising bridge between model-free RL and certifiable safety, while clarifying the structural conditions under which such filters remain effective. All code is available at \href{https://github.com/DhruvKushwaha/Koopman-CBF-Soft-Actor-Critic}{Github Repository}.

</details>


### [167] [RCSP: Risk-Sensitive Conjectural Scenario Planning for Safe Dynamic Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26348)
*Zhengye Han,Quanyan Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出风险敏感推测性场景规划（RCSP）方法，通过维护轻量级信念、采样未来交互并惩罚高风险尾部，在动态瓶颈场景中预测性地规避近失碰撞风险，实验表明RCSP能提升安全性和路径质量，但严格基准成功率仍弱于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人在动态障碍物环境中，当前安全的线速度和角速度可能导致机器人在未来被迫进入即将被障碍物封闭的通道，即预测性近失承诺问题，现有方法未充分解决。

Method: RCSP规划层：维护关于局部运动推测的轻量级信念，采样短时域未来交互场景，对高风险尾部进行惩罚，并通过局部安全检查执行候选指令。

Result: MuJoCo瓶颈任务中RCSP达到无碰撞目标，安全性和路径质量点估计优于非自适应预测器但延迟更高；ROS2/Gazebo中增加局部安全层减少动态近失失败；DynaBARN/Jackal迁移中DWA和TEB在严格基准成功率上更强。

Conclusion: RCSP可作为预测性风险模块补充现有导航栈在动态瓶颈场景中的能力，但严格基准成功率边界仍需突破。

Abstract: Mobile robots can fail before they collide: a velocity that is safe now may commit the robot to a passage that moving obstacles will soon close. We study this predictive near-miss commitment problem and propose Risk-Sensitive Conjectural Scenario Planning (RCSP), a planning layer that evaluates candidate commands against plausible short-horizon obstacle futures. RCSP maintains a lightweight belief over local motion conjectures, samples future interactions, penalizes high-risk tails, and executes through a local safety check. In controlled MuJoCo bottleneck tasks, the RCSP planner reaches the goal without collisions and yields higher secondary safety and path-quality point estimates than a non-adaptive predictor, with additional latency. In ROS2/Gazebo, adding the local safety layer to a standard Nav2 stack reduces dynamic near-miss failures. On official DynaBARN/Jackal transfer, tuned DWA and TEB remain stronger on strict benchmark success, revealing the boundary of the approach. These simulation results position RCSP as a predictive-risk module that complements existing navigation stacks in dynamic bottleneck regimes.

</details>


### [168] [Efficient On-policy Visual-RL via Stochastic Decoupled Policy Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26478)
*Haoxiang You,Yilang Liu,Davis Zong,Qian Wang,Teeratham Vitchutripop,Qi Wang,Daniel Rakita,Ian Abraham*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出随机解耦策略梯度（SDPG），一种轻量级视觉强化学习方法，通过轨迹滚动的随机扰动估计策略梯度，在单张RTX 4080 GPU上数小时内端到端训练多样化视觉运动控制策略，显著减少计算和内存开销，在视觉MuJoCo基准上优于基线方法，并引入真实视觉机器人基准套件及有效的sim-to-real迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉强化学习方法通常需要大量批量渲染环境和计算资源（多GPU、多天训练），计算和内存开销巨大，难以在单GPU上高效训练端到端的视觉运动控制策略。

Method: 提出SDPG方法，通过轨迹滚动的随机扰动来估计策略梯度，无需大量批量渲染环境，从而大幅降低计算和内存开销，实现轻量级训练。

Result: 在视觉MuJoCo基准上，SDPG在训练时间、内存使用和奖励方面均一致优于基线方法；同时引入一套真实视觉机器人基准，涵盖灵巧操作和具有挑战性的运动任务，并在物理硬件上展示了有效的sim-to-real迁移。

Conclusion: SDPG是一种高效、轻量的视觉RL方法，能够在单GPU上快速训练多样化视觉运动控制策略，并支持从仿真到真实机器人的有效迁移，为未来研究提供了实用的基准平台。

Abstract: We present the stochastic decoupled policy gradient (SDPG), a lightweight visual reinforcement learning (RL) method that trains diverse visuomotor control policies end-to-end within a few hours on a single NVIDIA RTX 4080 GPU. SDPG estimates policy gradients via random perturbations of trajectory rollouts, requiring orders of magnitude fewer batch-rendered environments and substantially reducing compute and memory overhead. On visual MuJoCo benchmarks, SDPG consistently outperforms baseline methods in training time, memory usage, and rewards. Finally, to support future research, we introduce a suite of realistic visual robotics benchmarks spanning dexterous manipulation, challenging locomotion, and demonstrate effective sim-to-real transfer on physical hardware.

</details>


### [169] [Closing the Loop in Teleoperation: Episode-Level Data Quality Assessment and Feedback for High-Quality Demonstration Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26349)
*Gokul Narayanan,Yash Shahapurkar,Melih Erdogan,Brian Zhu,Eugen Solowjow*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据质量评估与反馈（DQAF）框架，通过在遥操作后立即提供基于语义任务进展和机器人遥测的结构化质量评估与可操作的自然语言反馈，帮助新手操作员提升演示数据质量，实验表明该框架能显著加速操作员的学习曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业自动化正从刚性系统向灵活自适应系统转型，物理AI驱动下对大规模真实世界机器人演示数据的需求日益增长，遥操作成为重要数据采集手段。然而新手操作员产生的演示数据虽然任务成功但往往因运动效率低、重复修正、接近关节极限等原因而次优，亟需一种能提供超越二元成功/失败判断的精细化质量反馈机制。

Method: 提取子任务进展、运动平滑度、停顿、运动学极限等质量相关信号，将其转化为结构化质量评估和可操作的自然语言反馈，在每次遥操作后立即提供给操作员。

Result: 在诊断验证研究中，系统与人类审查员在数据集筛选中的拒绝原因和可操作反馈方面表现一致；在包含三名新手操作员和两项操作任务的初步用户研究中，接收到系统即时自动反馈的操作员比其他操作员提升更快，更早产生高质量演示数据。

Conclusion: DQAF框架通过提供超越简单成功/失败的细粒度、可操作的即时反馈，能有效帮助新手操作员快速提升遥操作演示数据质量，对大规模高质量机器人数据采集具有重要实用价值。

Abstract: Industrial automation is at a pivotal moment, as Physical AI is driving a transition from rigid, hand-engineered automation systems toward more flexible and adaptive systems. This shift has created a growing demand for large-scale, real-world robot demonstration data, making teleoperation an increasingly important mechanism for data collection. However, high-quality teleoperated demonstrations remain difficult to obtain in practice, as novice operators often produce episodes that are task-successful but suboptimal for downstream use due to inefficient motion, repeated corrections, or operation near robot joint limits. We present a Data Quality Assessment and Feedback (DQAF) framework that closes the loop in teleoperation by providing immediate post-episode feedback grounded in semantic task progress and robot telemetry. The framework extracts quality relevant signals such as sub-task progress, motion smoothness, stalls, kinematic limits and converts them into structured quality assessments and actionable natural-language feedback. Unlike binary success or failure feedback, the proposed system explains why an episode is suboptimal and highlights specific behaviors to correct in the next trial. We evaluate the framework through a diagnostic validation study and a pilot user study. In the validation study, the system is compared with a human reviewer during dataset curation, producing rejection reasons and actionable feedback for improvement. In the pilot study with three novice operators across two manipulation tasks, the operator who received the systems immediate, automated post-episode feedback improved faster than those who did not, producing higher-quality demonstrations sooner.

</details>


### [170] [Provably Safe Motion Planning Under Unknown Disturbances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26625)
*Ibon Gracia,Qi Heng Ho,Luca Laurenti,Morteza Lahijanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向受未知分布随机扰动的机器人系统的可证明安全的基于采样的运动规划算法。该算法利用系统轨迹数据学习Wasserstein模糊管（一系列模糊集），以高置信度包含系统状态分布轨迹，并在此基础上构建概率完备的采样运动规划树，同时满足机会约束。通过学习多个低维模糊管替代单一高维模糊管，有效降低保守性并提升可扩展性；设计的基于Bandit的高效有效性检查器在不牺牲概率完备性的前提下显著提升经验性能。实验表明该方法在严格安全阈值下能在杂乱环境中找到有效规划，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有采样运动规划方法在处理未知分布随机扰动下的安全约束（机会约束）时存在不足，需要一种既能保证安全性（可证明安全）又能保持概率完备性的算法。

Method: 1) 利用系统轨迹数据学习Wasserstein模糊管，以高置信度包含状态分布轨迹；2) 基于此模糊管构建概率完备的采样运动规划树；3) 通过学习多个低维模糊管替代高维模糊管降低保守性；4) 设计基于Bandit的有效性检查器提升计算效率。

Result: 在杂乱环境中，该方法能在严格安全阈值下找到有效规划，性能优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 提出的算法在未知分布随机扰动下实现了可证明安全的运动规划，通过低维模糊管分解和Bandit检查器有效平衡了保守性、可扩展性和计算效率。

Abstract: We present a provably safe sampling-based motion planning algorithm for robotic systems affected by random disturbances of unknown distribution. We consider systems with linear or linearizable dynamics evolving in workspace with arbitrary-shaped obstacles subject to state and control constraints. Safety requirements are formulated as chance-constraints. Our approach leverages data from trajectories of the system to learn a Wasserstein ambiguity tube, i.e., a sequence of ambiguity sets, which contains the trajectory of the system's state distribution with high confidence. This ambiguity tube is then used in a probabilistically complete algorithm to grow a sampling-based motion planning tree that respects the constraints of the problem. We show that learning several lower-dimensional ambiguity tubes instead of a single high-dimensional one effectively reduces the conservatism and boosts scalability. Additionally, we design an efficient bandit-based validity checker that remarkably increases the empirical performance of our approach without sacrificing probabilistic completeness. Case studies show our algorithm finds valid plans in cluttered environments under strict safety thresholds, outperforming state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [171] [Multi-Robot Box Transport over Different Surfaces with Decentralized Role-based Proportional Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26430)
*Aditya Bhatt,Himavarshini Yarragangu,Urvish Shah,Venkata Sai Yaswanth Mohan Thota,Souma Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为R2P2（Roles with Rules and Proportional-control Primitive）的异步去中心化任务与运动规划方法，用于多机器人协作推箱，能适应不同倾斜度和摩擦系数的地面（平地、上坡、下坡），通过分配角色（推、支撑、阻止）和规则/比例控制实现，在六机器人仿真和四台TurtleBot实物实验中验证了优于经典虚拟领航-跟随方法的成功率和泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人协作推物体在建筑、仓储、灾后清理等场景有广泛应用，但在地面倾斜度和摩擦特性变化的情况下实现协作搬运面临独特挑战，现有方法在通信、同步和共识方面存在瓶颈，且存在单点故障问题。

Method: 提出R2P2方法：采用去中心化异步架构，首先根据操作模式（旋转vs平移）和规则为机器人分配角色（推、支撑、阻止），然后基于角色执行规则控制或比例速度控制；每个机器人仅需观测自身和箱体的位置与朝向。

Result: 在NVIDIA IsaacSim仿真中通过六机器人团队验证，R2P2在不同地面摩擦/倾斜和箱体质量场景下均表现出泛化性，成功率优于经典虚拟领航-跟随方法；并在四台TurtleBot搬运1.2kg箱体的实物实验中成功验证。

Conclusion: R2P2作为一种去中心化、低通信依赖的协作推箱方法，能有效应对不同地形和负载变化，具有良好的泛化性和鲁棒性，适合多机器人协作搬运任务。

Abstract: Collaborative transport of objects via pushing by multiple robots has many applications, ranging from construction and warehouse environments to post disaster debris clean-up. Achieving collaborative transport over surfaces with different inclination and friction properties however poses unique challenges. To address these challenges, this paper presents an asynchronous decentralized task and motion planning approach for transporting rectangular boxes of varying mass over flat, uphill and downhill terrain. Such a decentralized approach alleviates communication, synchronization and consensus needs and mitigates single point of failure issues. Our approach, called R2P2 or Roles with Rules and Proportional-control Primitive, assigns roles (e.g., push, support and prevent) to robots based on rules cognizant of the mode of manipulation needed (box rotation vs translation); this is followed by either rule-based control or proportional control of robot velocity based on the roles. Each robot is assumed to observe the location and heading of self and the box in executing the role and controls. R2P2 is evaluated with a six-robot team deployed in a simulator built using NVIDIA IsaacSim -- demonstrating generalizability across different surface friction/inclination and box mass scenarios, and better success rate compared to a standard virtual-leader-follower method. R2P2 is also successfully validated with a physical experiment, where it is executed onboard four turtlebots tasked with moving a 1.2 kg box.

</details>


### [172] [Riding the Shifting Potential: When Reactive Control Suffices for Multi-Goal Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27314)
*Vito Mengers,Oliver Brock*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将零空间投影扩展至基于图的世界模型，通过根据当前状态动态确定优先级来解决多目标控制中的冲突，无需重新训练即可处理导航、物体推搡及真实机器人任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反驳"反应式控制对多目标任务不足"的观点，指出问题源于静态编码无法反映目标间的动态交互关系，而非反应式控制本身。

Method: 将零空间投影整合到基于图的交互结构世界模型中：在冲突产生处通过将低优先级梯度投影到高优先级梯度的零空间来解决冲突，优先级根据当前状态连续确定。

Result: 在非凸障碍导航（静态势场完全失效）和非凸物体推搡任务中，该方法在100个配置上达到100%成功率，而最速下降基线为0%、扩散策略约55%。该公式可直接迁移至真实机器人，通过相同机制处理额外的感知和运动学约束。

Conclusion: 反应式控制通过动态优先级和零空间投影能够有效解决多目标冲突，为复杂机器人控制提供了一种无需演示或重新训练的通用框架。

Abstract: Reactive control is often considered insufficient for multi-objective tasks because conflicting objectives give rise to local minima. We argue this limitation is not inherent but arises from static encodings that fail to reflect how objectives currently interact. We exploit the interaction structure encoded in a graph-based world model by extending it with nullspace projections: conflicts are resolved where they arise by projecting lower-priority gradients into the nullspace of higher-priority ones, with priorities determined continuously from the current state. We demonstrate this in two domains where conflicts between objectives are central: navigation around non-convex obstacles, where static potential fields fundamentally fail, and planar pushing of non-convex objects, where our method achieves $100\%$ success across one-hundred configurations versus $0\%$ for the steepest-descent baseline and ${\sim}55\%$ for diffusion policy, without demonstrations or retraining. The same formulation transfers directly to a real robot with additional perceptual and kinematic constraints, accommodating them through the same mechanism.

</details>


### [173] [Manipulating Tangible Virtual Object Dynamics to Promote Learning of Precision Force Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26782)
*Alberto Garzás-Villar,Alba Riera-Cardona,Alexis Derumigny,J. Micah Prendergast,Jane Murray Cramm,Laura Marchal-Crespo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过操控虚拟物体的物理动态（线性和非线性弹簧模型）来训练精细力控制能力，实验发现非线性AS-Gaussian弹簧组在训练中力控制精度更高，但长期保持效果无显著差异，且参与者主要依赖目标伸长量而非目标力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人触觉设备结合虚拟现实为训练精细力生成提供了新机会，这对于中风后康复至关重要但常被忽视。本文提出通过操控虚拟物体的渲染动力学来训练精确力控制，同时激活体感系统。

Method: 50名健康参与者完成冰壶启发任务（拉伸虚拟弹簧产生目标释放力推动冰壶），训练中弹簧力-伸长关系采用线性、高斯或反对称高斯（AS-Gaussian）函数建模，其中AS-Gaussian在目标释放力处导数为零。分析训练精度、人格特质影响及迁移任务表现。

Result: AS-Gaussian组训练中力控制精度始终高于线性组；高斯组仅在训练后期优于线性组。高Free Spirit人格特质在高斯动力下表现更差且探索减少，高Transform-of-Challenge则增加探索。长期保持无显著差异。迁移任务显示参与者主要依赖目标伸长量而非目标力。

Conclusion: 该方法对体感神经康复具有潜力，但需改进以减少对本体感觉线索的依赖，然后才能对神经疾病患者进行测试。

Abstract: Robotic haptic devices combined with virtual reality offer novel opportunities to train fine force generation, an essential yet overlooked component of post-stroke rehabilitation. This study proposes that manipulating the rendered dynamics of tangible virtual objects can be leveraged to train precise force control while engaging the somatosensory system. We conducted an experiment with fifty healthy participants who performed a curling-inspired task in which they had to stretch a virtual spring to generate a target release force to propel the stone to a predefined location on the ice sheet. During training, the spring's force-elongation relationship was modeled as either a linear or non-linear function, i.e., a Gaussian or antisymmetric Gaussian (AS-Gaussian) function with zero derivative at the release target force. Results indicate that the AS-Gaussian group consistently achieved higher force accuracy during training than the linear group, while the Gaussian group only outperformed the linear group toward the end of training. Analysis of personality traits revealed that higher Free Spirit scores were associated with poorer performance and reduced task exploration under Gaussian dynamics, whereas higher Transform-of-Challenge scores correlated with increased exploration. Despite these training effects, no significant differences in long-term retention were found across spring types or personality traits. Participants primarily relied on learned target elongation rather than target force, as evidenced by performance in a transfer task with a different stiffness but the same target force. While promising for somatosensory neurorehabilitation, these methods require refinement to reduce reliance on proprioceptive cues before testing with neurological patients.

</details>


### [174] [Enabling Extensible Embodied Capabilities with Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26637)
*Xueyang Zhou,Zijia Wang,Qianjiang Li,Yibo Hu,Guiyao Tie,Li Wan,Yidan Liu,Pan Zhou,Lichao Sun,Yongchao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出能力外化方法，通过标准化协议ETP将具身智能中的异构能力解耦为独立工具，实验证明该方法在认知和感知任务上平均提升31%-36%，但在执行类能力上提升有限，且模型在工具调用时机、选择和组合上仍面临挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能方法将感知、推理、规划和控制统一在单一参数化策略中，但这些能力本质上是层次化和异质的，难以在单一模型中可靠学习和模块化。

Method: 提出能力外化方法，设计标准化具身工具协议(ETP)用于工具注册、发现、调用和执行，构建100+验证工具的EmbodiedToolBench基准，在仿真和真实平台上评估工具增强效果。

Result: 能力外化在EB-ALFRED上平均提升31%，在EB-Navigation上平均提升36%；认知和感知类能力提升显著，执行类能力提升有限；所有模型在工具调用时机、选择和组合上仍存在持续挑战。

Conclusion: 能力外化是提升具身智能性能的有效范式，但揭示了工具使用能力（包括何时、选择何种及如何调用工具）是未来研究的关键方向。

Abstract: Most existing embodied intelligence methods formulate perception, reasoning, planning, and control within a unified parameterized policy. Yet these capabilities are inherently hierarchical and heterogeneous, making them difficult to reliably learn and modularize within a single model. We propose a capability externalization approach that decouples heterogeneous capabilities into independently optimized tools, dynamically invoked at inference time. To this end, we introduce Embodied Tool Protocol (ETP), a standardized protocol for embodied tool registration, discovery, invocation, and execution, and curate 100+ validated tools spanning perception, cognition, reasoning, and execution as the tool base. Building on this, we construct EmbodiedToolBench to evaluate both whether tool augmentation improves embodied performance and how well current models use tools across tool-necessity recognition, tool selection, tool execution, and tool-chain composition. Experiments across simulation and real-world platforms confirm that capability externalization consistently improves embodied performance (avg. gain 31% on EB-ALFRED and 36% on EB-Navigation), yet reveal a clear boundary: gains are substantial for cognition and perception but are limited for execution-type capabilities. Moreover, our analysis reveals that knowing when, which, and how to invoke tools remains a persistent challenge across all models, thereby highlighting embodied tool competence as a critical direction for future research.

</details>


### [175] [HyperSim: A Holistic Sim-To-Real Framework For Robust Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26638)
*Junyi Dong,Haotian Luo,Ziwei Xu,Shengwei Bian,Heng Zhang,Sitong Mao,Jingyi Guo,Yang Xu,Wenhao Chen,Qiuyu Feng,Yao Mu,Ping Luo,Shunbo Zhou,Xiaodong Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HyperSim提出了一个从合成数据生成到策略训练再到真实世界部署的全流程框架，通过高保真环境合成、对抗轨迹生成和虚实联合训练三个核心模块弥合仿真到现实的领域差距。在400次真实任务执行中，使用ACT和π₀模型分别达到了80%和95%的仿真到现实成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展数据规模与多样性对于泛化具身智能至关重要，但物理数据采集成本高昂，而合成数据生成面临仿真到现实的领域差距挑战，亟需系统性的解决方案。

Method: HyperSim包含三大核心支柱：(1) 高保真环境合成——增强视觉真实性；(2) 对抗轨迹生成——扩展数据覆盖范围；(3) 仿真与真实联合训练——强制学习域不变表示。三者协同工作以弥合领域差异。

Result: 在400次真实任务执行中，与ACT和π₀模型结合分别达到80%和95%的仿真到现实成功率。采用对抗轨迹训练的策略在物理扰动下完成率提升35%。

Conclusion: HyperSim通过系统性地整合高保真合成、对抗数据生成和联合训练，有效解决了机器人操作策略从仿真迁移到现实的关键挑战，显著提升了策略在真实环境中的鲁棒性和成功率。

Abstract: Scaling data volume and diversity is critical for generalizing embodied intelligence. While synthetic data generation offers a scalable alternative to expensive physical data acquisition, transferring robotic manipulation policies from simulation to the real world (sim-to-real) remains a formidable challenge due to the domain gap. This paper presents HyperSim, a holistic framework spanning from synthetic data generation to policy training and seamless real-world deployment. To systematically bridge the sim-to-real gap, HyperSim is realized through three core pillars: high-fidelity environment synthesis, adversarial trajectory generation, and sim-and-real co-training. Collectively, these modules address domain discrepancies by enhancing visual fidelity, expanding data coverage, and enforcing domain-invariant representations. We rigorously validate HyperSim through a large-scale empirical study involving 400 real-world task executions across two representative manipulation models. Assessed across three fine-grained metrics, our complete pipeline achieves remarkable sim-to-real success rates of 80% and 95% with ACT and π_{0}, respectively. Furthermore, policies trained on our adversarial trajectories exhibit significantly enhanced robustness against dynamic uncertainties, achieving a 35% higher completion rate under physical perturbations.

</details>


### [176] [L-Learning : A Lyapunov-Based Approach Leveraging Lagrangian Mechanics for Efficient and Stable Robot Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26648)
*Quan Quan,Hao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出L-Learning，一种将李雅普诺夫稳定性理论与拉格朗日力学相结合的数据驱动控制框架，通过从数据中学习系统能量函数，在保证闭环稳定性的同时优化轨迹跟踪性能，具有高控制精度、理论稳定性保证和高样本效率等优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统控制方法在动态不确定环境中性能下降，而纯数据驱动方法虽然适应性更强，但样本复杂度高且缺乏严格的稳定性保证。L-Learning旨在弥合这两类方法的鸿沟。

Method: 将李雅普诺夫稳定性理论与拉格朗日力学融合到数据驱动框架中，通过从数据中显式学习系统的能量函数，在优化控制性能的同时内在保证闭环稳定性。

Result: 该方法具有优越的控制精度、理论上的稳定性保证以及高样本效率，在机器人轨迹跟踪任务中表现优异。

Conclusion: L-Learning为解决实际机器人应用中的控制问题提供了一种有前景的方案，兼顾了数据驱动方法的灵活性和传统控制方法的稳定性保障。

Abstract: This paper presents L-Learning, a novel data-driven control framework for robotics that integrates Lyapunov stability theory with Lagrangian mechanics to enhance trajectory tracking performance. While traditional control methods often suffer from performance degradation in dynamic and uncertain environments, data-driven approaches, while more adaptable, are frequently limited by high sample complexity and a lack of rigorous stability guarantees. L-Learning mitigates these challenges by explicitly learning the system's energy function from data, thereby optimizing performance while ensuring closed-loop stability intrinsically. Characterized by superior control accuracy, theoretical stability guarantees, and high sample efficiency, L-Learning represents a promising solution for practical robotic applications.

</details>


### [177] [On the Generalization Capabilities, Design Choices and Limitations of Keypoint Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26649)
*Thomas Lips,Marco Moletta,Michael C. Welle,Danica Kragic,Francis wyffels*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于关键点的模仿学习（KIL）在机器人操作中的泛化能力，通过2000+真实世界实验验证，KIL在五个任务上平均成功率达75%，显著优于RGB基线（47%），但与S2-diffusion（73%）表现相当。研究还探讨了基础模型的局限性，并将KIL扩展到多物体实例任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于RGB的模仿学习需要大量演示才能泛化到未见过的物体或场景，因此需要研究中间表示方法来提高机器人操作的泛化能力。视觉基础模型能够通过一次学习提取关键点作为中间表示，但如何最优地将其集成到模仿学习中以及何时优于其他表示方法仍不明确。

Method: 结合先前关键点模仿学习（KIL）的方法，研究多种设计选择以提供实践指导；使用超过2000次真实世界 rollout 评估KIL对未见物体和场景变化的泛化能力；将KIL扩展到多物体实例任务。

Result: KIL在五个任务上总体成功率达75%，显著优于RGB基线（47%），与S2-diffusion（73%）表现相当。KIL被证实是一种数据高效的机器人学习方法。

Conclusion: KIL是一种数据高效的模仿学习方法，但在性能上并未超越其他表示方法，且继承了用于关键点提取的基础模型的局限性。

Abstract: RGB-based imitation learning requires many demonstrations to generalize to unseen objects or scenes, motivating research into intermediate representations to improve generalization for robotic manipulation. Visual foundation models enable one-shot extraction of keypoints to provide such representation. However, it remains unclear how to integrate them into imitation learning optimally and when they outperform alternative representations. We combine approaches from previous works on keypoint imitation learning (KIL) and investigate several design choices to provide practical guidelines. Using over 2000 real-world rollouts, we also assess the generalization capabilities of KIL to unseen objects and scene variations. KIL achieves a 75% overall success rate across five tasks, significantly outperforming the RGB baseline (47%) and performing on par with S2-diffusion (73%). Finally, we explore the limitations of the foundation models used for keypoint extraction and extend KIL to tasks with multiple object instances. Our results confirm KIL as a data-efficient approach for robot learning, though it does not outperform alternative representations and inherits limitations of the foundation models used for keypoint extraction. All rollout videos, demonstrations, and results are available at https://kil-manipulation.github.io/.

</details>


### [178] [SteelDS: A High-Resolution Video Dataset of E40 Steel Scrap for Object Detection and Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26682)
*Melanie Neubauer,Christian Rauch,Gerald Koinig,Alexia Tischberger-Aldrian,Roland Pomberger,Elmar Rueckert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该数据集包含24,297帧高分辨率标注视频，记录了传送带上E40级钢和铜废料的图像，用于机器学习模型训练，以实现材料分类、目标检测和实例分割，特别是帮助识别铜杂质以替代人工分拣。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业后磁选阶段仍需人工干预去除铜杂质，缺乏高质量的标注数据集来开发自动化分拣算法。

Method: 在受控实验室环境采集传送带上钢和铜废料的高分辨率视频，提供像素级分割掩码和材料类别标注，包含5个子集、396个钢目标和101个铜目标，并模拟了不同间距和密度变化。

Result: 成功构建了包含24,297帧标注数据的多子集数据集，覆盖不同尺寸的钢和铜目标，支持分类、检测和分割任务。

Conclusion: 该数据集可作为自动化分拣算法评估基准，有助于在复杂钢废料流中识别铜杂质，减少人工干预。

Abstract: This dataset provides high-resolution, annotated video sequences of shredded E40-grade steel and copper scrap on a conveyor belt. Captured in a controlled laboratory environment, the data reflects the industrial post-magnetic sorting stage, where manual intervention is typically required to remove copper contaminants. The dataset comprises 24,297 labeled frames across five subsets, featuring 396 steel and 101 copper objects categorized by size. It supports the development of machine learning models for material classification, object detection, and instance segmentation. Variations in object spacing and density are included to simulate realistic industrial sorting conditions. Ground truth annotations include pixel-wise segmentation masks and material classes. This dataset serves as a benchmark for evaluating automated sorting algorithms aiming to identify copper impurities within complex, heterogeneous steel scrap streams.

</details>


### [179] [Look Further: Socially-Compliant Navigation System in Residential Buildings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26710)
*Akira Shiba,Marina Obata,Nathan Kau,Zoltan Beck,Rishi Shah,Michael Sudano,Sabrina Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出主动变道导航模式，通过将移动机器人的反应距离延长至8米以上（远超传统社交导航的几米范围），显著提升了用户在直走廊场景中对机器人安全性、流畅性和礼貌性的感知，但在交叉口盲角场景中不同运动模式无显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统社交导航方法仅在近距离（几米内）处理人机交互冲突，而本文探索通过大幅延长反应距离（超过8米）能否改善人类对机器人运动的感知。

Method: 提出Proactive Lane-Changing (PLC)运动模式：机器人在走廊中距迎面行人8米时从中心横向移动到侧边；并在42人参与的用户研究中，在直走廊正面对抗和交叉口盲角两种场景下，将PLC与减速、停止、近距离反应式避障等传统模式进行对比评估。

Result: 直走廊场景下，PLC在安全性、流畅性和礼貌性三个维度上均显著优于传统模式；交叉口盲角场景下，各模式间无显著差异，用户偏好多样。

Conclusion: 延长反应距离至8米以上的主动变道策略能有效改善用户在直走廊中对机器人运动的主观感受，但在视线受限的盲角场景中需进一步研究。

Abstract: The distance at which a mobile robot reacts to a person strongly impacts various qualities of the human-robot interaction. In this paper, we focus on the navigation of a mobile delivery robot platform in a residential indoor hallway environment. Social navigation methods typically focus on avoiding uncomfortable human-robot interactions, such as when a robot encroaches on someone's personal space. Since personal space has been shown to be in the range of just a few meters, social navigation methods typically focus on deconflicting and resolving these short-range interactions. In this work, however, we demonstrate that by extending the reaction distance to over eight meters, far beyond the typical interaction distance, we can improve the human's perception of the robot's motion. We introduce the Proactive Lane-Changing (PLC) motion pattern and a navigation system that leverages it to react to people at an increased distance. This pattern consists of changing the robot's lateral position as it navigates down the hallway from the center to the side at an eight-meter distance from an oncoming person.
  We conducted a user study with 42 participants to assess their impressions of the delivery robot based on three service objectives: safety, smoothness, and politeness. In the straight hallway scenario (Frontal Approach), results showed significant improvement in each of these three objectives compared to typical motion patterns found in the literature: slowing down, stopping, and reactive collision avoidance in the proximity of a person. In contrast, in the intersection (Blind Corner) scenarios, none of the approaches performed significantly better than any other, with participants having a diverse range of preferences among robot motion patterns.

</details>


### [180] [Can VLA Models Learn from Real-World Data Continually without Forgetting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26820)
*Jiarun Zhu,Yijun Hong,Xiaoquan Sun,Zetian Xu,Mingqi Yuan,Zhiyong Wang,Wenjun Zeng,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次在真实世界场景下研究了VLA模型的持续学习问题，构建了包含四个连续操作任务的数据集，发现VLA模型存在严重的灾难性遗忘，并系统评估了经验回放方法，为部署长期机器人策略提供了实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型持续学习研究仅限于狭窄的模拟环境，缺乏在真实世界条件下的探索，而实际部署需要模型能够持续获取新技能同时保留已学行为。

Method: 构建了一个包含四个顺序操作任务（刚性物体抓取放置、接触式按压、可变形物体折叠）的真实世界持续学习数据集，进行综合实验，并系统评估经验回放策略。

Result: VLA模型在持续学习异构真实世界演示时表现出显著的灾难性遗忘；经验回放方法可缓解该问题，其成功取决于关键实现因素。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了真实世界持续VLA学习的首个实证研究，为部署长期机器人策略提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models provide a promising foundation for general-purpose robotics. However, their successful deployment in real-world scenarios requires the ability to continually acquire new skills while retaining previously learned behaviors. While pioneering research has studied the continual learning of VLA models in narrowly simulated environments, this challenge remains largely unexplored under realistic conditions. To address this limitation, we construct a real-world continual learning dataset comprising four sequential manipulation tasks, spanning rigid-object pick-and-place, contact-rich pressing, and deformable-object folding. Using this dataset, we conduct comprehensive experiments and find that VLA models suffer significant catastrophic forgetting when continually learning from heterogeneous real-world demonstrations. We then systematically evaluate experience replay and uncover key implementation factors that govern its success. In summary, this work provides the first empirical study of real-world continual VLA learning and offers practical guidance for deploying long-lived robot policies.

</details>


### [181] [Learning Compositional Symbolic Task Rules from Demonstrations with Inductive Logic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26828)
*Oleh Borys,Karla Stepanova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用归纳逻辑编程（ILP）通过将复杂任务分解为不同抽象层级上的简单学习目标，从演示中学习可解释、可重用且强泛化的符号化任务结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LfD方法通常只关注如何执行任务，而忽略了任务的高层结构；随着机器人自主性提升，需要可检查、可重用、人类可解释的任务表示。

Method: 使用归纳逻辑编程（ILP）将复杂任务分解为不同本体抽象层级上的多个简单学习目标，从演示和先验知识中推理符号规则，并在学习高层任务结构时重用已学规则。

Result: 在合成积木组装场景中，该方法学习的抽象表示具有可解释性，并在未见物体的更难任务上展现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 分解式ILP是实现任务级LfD的可行方法。

Abstract: Learning from Demonstration~(LfD) should capture not only how a task is executed, but also its high-level task structure that explains the demonstrated behavior. As robots become more autonomous, such task representations must be inspectable, reusable, and human-interpretable. To address this, we study how to represent and learn robotic tasks with inductive logic programming~(ILP) by decomposing a complex task into a series of simpler learning objectives at different abstraction (ontological) levels. The system infers symbolic rules from demonstrations and prior (domain) knowledge, and reuses learned rules when learning higher-level task structure. We evaluate the approach in a synthetic block-assembly scenario and show that the learned abstractions are interpretable and support strong generalization to harder, held-out tasks with unseen objects. These results provide preliminary evidence that decomposed ILP is a feasible approach to task-level LfD.

</details>


### [182] [A Bioinspired Underwater Robot with a Latch-Mediated Soft Bistable Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26936)
*Chongze Bi,Wenjie Wu,Zonghao Zuo,Li Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仿生软体双稳态执行器，集成闩锁机制，利用单个电机实现非对称能量输入与释放，结合鳍结构实现小型水下机器人的高效推进与机动控制，实验达到最大推力0.528N、冲量0.147Ns、垂直位移30mm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统水下机器人小型化受限于低能量密度的动力源；自然界生物（如螳螂虾、跳蚤）的闩锁-弹簧驱动系统（LaMSA）能实现快速运动，但如何在简单紧凑结构中复现这种快速非对称驱动仍具挑战。

Method: 设计了一种仿生软体双稳态执行器，集成闩锁机制，使用单个电机实现非对称能量输入与释放；结合鳍结构实现水下推进与机动。

Result: 实现了稳定的周期性拍动、精确转向；最大推力0.528N，冲量0.147Ns，垂直位移30mm；通过调节鳍角实现垂直上升、对角线前进和横向平移等多种运动模式。

Conclusion: 本研究提出了一种新颖的、节能的小型水下机器人运动控制方法，为探索、环境监测和检测等应用领域的先进仿生设计奠定了基础。

Abstract: Underwater robotics has advanced significantly over recent decades. however, the development of miniaturized underwater robots remains limited by low energy densities of traditional power sources. Nature offers compelling solutions-organisms like mantis shrimps and fleas utilize latch-mediated spring actuation (LaMSA) systems that achieve rapid movements through a decoupled energy storage and release mechanism. Despite extensive studies of LaMSA, replicating such rapid, asymmetric actuation within simple, compact structures remains challenging. In this work, we introduce a bioinspired, soft bistable actuator with an integrated latch mechanism that enables asymmetric energy input and release using a single motor. Coupled with fin structures, this design facilitates efficient underwater propulsion and maneuverability. Experimental results demonstrate stable periodic flapping, precise steering, and a maximum thrust of 0.528 N, impulse of 0.147 Ns, and vertical displacement of 30 mm. By modulating fin angles, the robot achieves versatile motions, including vertical ascent, diagonal forward movement, and lateral translation. This study presents a novel, energy-efficient approach for controlling motion in compact underwater robots, paving the way for advanced biomimetic designs with potential applications in exploration, environmental monitoring, and inspection.

</details>


### [183] [Object Pose and Shape Estimation for Grasping: Does it Work?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26944)
*Pavan Karke,Kushal Shah,Gaurav Singh,Md Faizal Karim,K Madhava Krishna,Rajat Talak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统性地研究了基于单视图RGB(-D)的物体姿态与形状估计方法结合对极抓取采样是否优于端到端抓取合成方法。实验表明，模块化方法（先估计姿态形状再采样抓取）在所有实验中均优于端到端方法，尤其对小物体表现更佳，但其有效性依赖于姿态形状估计的准确性，在杂乱场景中会部分退化。此外，模块化方法可结合视觉语言模型实现语言条件抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究当前物体姿态与形状估计方法是否已足够成熟，以至于结合对极抓取采样后能够超越端到端抓取合成方法。

Method: 将一种最先进的端到端抓取合成方法与三种模块化方法（先对场景中所有物体进行姿态和形状估计，然后使用对极采样生成抓取）进行比较。模块化方法采用两种不同的姿态形状估计方式：一种基于编码器-解码器模型，另一种基于扩散模型。实验限定于平行爪夹持器、7-DoF抓取和单视图RGB(-D)图像输入。

Result: 模块化方法在所有实验中均优于端到端方法，能够为小物体合成大量抓取（端到端方法在此场景下失败）。模块化方法的有效性依赖于姿态形状估计的精度，在杂乱场景中会部分退化。三种模块化方法在失败模式和运行时间上各有特点。结合视觉语言模型后，单视图姿态形状估计方法可实现语言条件抓取，性能与LERF-TOGO基线相当。

Conclusion: 模块化方法（先估计姿态形状再采样抓取）在当前阶段优于端到端抓取合成方法，但受限于姿态形状估计在杂乱场景中的精度。未来改进姿态形状估计可进一步提升抓取性能。

Abstract: The problem of object pose and shape estimation has seen key advancements lately. Encoder-decoder (e.g., SAM3D, LRM, CRISP) and diffusion-based models (e.g., InstantMesh, Zero123, SceneComplete) have shown category-agnostic shape encoding capacity and open-set generalizability. In this work, we ask the question: Are the object pose and shape estimation methods mature enough, such that when used with antipodal grasp sampling, can outperform the end-to-end grasp synthesis methods? We explore this question in detail by scoping our study to parallel jaw grippers, 7-DoF grasps, and single-view RGB(-D) image as input. We implement and compare a state-of-the-art, end-to-end grasp synthesis method and three modular methods, which first estimate the object pose and shape for all objects in the scene, and generate grasps using antipodal sampling. We observe that the modular methods outperform the end-to-end method in all our experiments. The modular methods are able to synthesize plenty of grasps, even for small objects, where the end-to-end methods fail. The effectiveness of the modular methods is contingent on the accuracy of the pose and shape estimation, and suffers partial degradation in cluttered scenes - a limitation of the existing pose and shape estimation methods. We also analyze the failure modes and run-times for the three modular methods, which use two different ways of object pose and shape estimation: one based on an encoder-decoder model, while another a diffusion model. Finally, we demonstrate that the single-view object pose and shape estimation methods can be augmented with vision-language models to yield language-conditioned grasps from just single-view RGB-D image as input. We notice comparable performance to the state-of-the-art LERF-TOGO baseline.

</details>


### [184] [Trust, Geometry, and Rules: A Credibility-Aware Reinforcement Learning Framework for Safe USV Navigation under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26974)
*Yuhang Zhang,Shuqi Chai,Yukang Zhang,Liusha Yang,Mingchuan Zhang,Wei Wang,Qingjiang Shi,Quanbo Ge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种集成可信感知学习、几何安全屏蔽和连续规则感知嵌入的框架，解决USV在动态海洋环境中存在感知不确定性时的自主导航与COLREGs合规问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于RL的方法在感知系统存在未校准不确定性时表现不佳，因为状态估计误差导致不可靠的信念状态误导价值函数，而离散交通规则引入学习目标的不连续性。

Method: 1) 可信加权价值学习(CW-VL)：利用滤波估计协方差与经验误差统计之间的差异构建动态信任因子，调节评论家的异方差损失；2) 协方差膨胀速度障碍(CI-VO)：将位置估计不确定性映射为集合角度余量，形成保守几何屏蔽；3) 风险感知COLREGs职责嵌入：将二元相遇职责松弛为连续规则感知信号。

Result: 仿真遭遇研究表明，该方法在感知不一致性下具有更优的训练鲁棒性，以及在碰撞避免和COLREGs合规方面优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效解决了感知不确定性下USV自主导航的挑战，在鲁棒性和合规性上均取得显著提升。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation of Unmanned Surface Vehicles (USVs) that is safe and compliant with the International Regulations for Preventing Collisions at Sea (COLREGs) remains a formidable challenge in dynamic maritime environments, particularly when perception systems exhibit miscalibrated uncertainty. Existing Reinforcement Learning (RL)-based methods often falter because state-estimation errors induce unreliable belief states that mislead the value function, while discrete traffic rules introduce discontinuity in the learning objective. To address these challenges, we propose a framework integrating credibility-aware learning, geometric safety shielding, and continuous rule-aware embedding. First, Credibility-Weighted Value Learning (CW-VL) introduces a dynamic trust factor derived from the discrepancy between filter-estimated covariance and empirical error statistics to modulate the critic's heteroscedastic loss, preventing policy overfitting to noisy samples. Second, the Covariance-Inflated Velocity Obstacle (CI-VO) maps position-estimation uncertainty into set-wise angular margins, forming a conservative geometric shield that overrides hazardous exploratory actions. Third, Risk-Aware COLREGs Duty Embedding relaxes binary encounter duties into continuous rule-aware signals, providing smooth sector-transition information and suppressing oscillation from sparse rule rewards. Simulated encounter studies demonstrate improved training robustness against perceptual inconsistency and superior collision avoidance and COLREGs compliance over baselines.

</details>


### [185] [Towards Shared Embodied Intelligence in Humanoid Robots through Optimization Development and Testing of the Human Aware ergoCub Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26991)
*Carlotta Sartore,Mohamed Elobaid,Lorenzo Rapetti,Giulio Romualdi,Stefano Dafarra,Nicola A. Piga,Ines Sorrentino,Paolo Maria Vicecone,Silvio Traversaro,Ugo Pattacini,Luca Fiorio,Francesco Draicchio,Giovanna Tranfo,Lorenzo Natale,Marco Maggiali,Daniele Pucci*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种融合共享智能与具身认知的架构，通过将人体模型嵌入机器人硬件与物理智能优化中，实现人机物理协作，并实例化为人形机器人ergoCub，其形态与控制均针对人类工效学指标进行了优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类协作依赖于共享智能（对他人内部表征进行协调）和具身认知（身体与认知能力对环境优化的响应），而设计能够安全有效与人协作的人形机器人需要统一这两个原理。
