Method: 提出一种架构，将人体模型嵌入机器人物理智能中，以人类工效学指标为目标函数，同时对机器人硬件参数和物理智能（控制策略）进行联合优化；将人机交互建模为硬件配置的函数。

Result: 具体实现了人形机器人ergoCub，其形态与控制均针对人类协作任务进行了工效学优化。

Conclusion: 该框架为在硬件和物理智能层面优先考虑人类工效学的人形机器人设计提供了方法论，适用于工业与辅助机器人领域。

Abstract: Collaboration is central to human behavior, enabling tasks beyond individual capability. This ability arises from coordinating actions through internal representations of others, a concept known as shared intelligence. Additionally, humans are characterized by physical bodies and cognitive abilities that are optimized in response to their environment, a phenomenon referred to as embodied cognition. Designing humanoid robots that collaborate safely and effectively with people requires unifying these principles. Here we propose an architecture that integrates shared intelligence and embodied cognition to enable robots to physically collaborate with humans, where robot hardware and control are optimized for human metrics, using representations of the human body and motion intelligence. The ultimate goal is to achieve a form of shared embodied intelligence. Specifically, our architecture optimizes robot hardware and physical intelligence parameters with respect to human ergonomic metrics. This is accomplished by modeling human-robot interaction as a function of hardware configurations and embedding human models into the robot's physical intelligence. As a concrete implementation, we present the humanoid robot ergoCub, whose morphology and control have been optimized for collaborative tasks with humans. Our approach provides a framework for designing humanoid robots that prioritize human ergonomics at both the hardware and physical intelligence levels, with applications in industrial and assistive robotics.

</details>


### [186] [TPS-Drive: Task-Guided Representation Purification for VLM-based Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27038)
*Jiaxiang Li,Yumao Liu,Ke Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TPS-Drive提出任务引导的表示净化框架，通过Agent-Centric Tokenizer将有限码本容量从静态背景重分配到动态智能体，结合解耦推理流水线和三阶段渐进训练，在nuScenes和NAVSIM基准上实现了精准的3D空间状态预测和更低碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM驾驶规划方法存在两类表示策略的固有限制：文本对齐方法将连续空间状态扁平化为符号，破坏几何结构并导致"空间幻觉"；密集视觉方法保留空间拓扑但冗余背景纹理淹没标准分词器，引发"表示干扰"。需要一种能同时保持几何完整性并消除背景冗余的表示方法。

Method: 提出Task-Guided Representation Purification框架：1) Agent-Centric Tokenizer采用任务引导的向量量化机制，由冻结的3D检测头监督，将有限码本容量从静态背景重分配到关键动态智能体；2) 解耦推理流水线依次执行场景理解、未来预测和动作生成；3) 渐进式三阶段训练范式，最终通过奖励驱动精炼超越纯模仿学习。

Result: 在开放循环nuScenes评估中实现精准的智能体空间状态预测并降低碰撞率；在严格封闭循环NAVSIMv1和NAVSIMv2基准上建立了新的安全记录。

Conclusion: 任务引导的表示净化策略有效缓解了VLM在驾驶规划中的空间幻觉和表示干扰问题，通过显式码本重分配实现了更安全、更准确的3D空间预测与规划。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) provide a promising foundation for autonomous driving planning, yet bridging semantic reasoning and precise 3D spatial forecasting remains a critical challenge. Existing representation strategies generally follow two paths: text-aligned methods flatten continuous spatial states into symbols, which compromises geometric structure and induces "spatial hallucinations"; dense visual methods preserve spatial topology but overwhelm standard tokenizers with redundant background textures, leading to "representation interference". To address these limitations, we introduce TPS-Drive, a novel framework centered on Task-Guided Representation Purification that empowers VLMs to Think in Purified Space. At its core, an Agent-Centric Tokenizer utilizes a task-guided vector quantization mechanism supervised by a frozen 3D detection head, which explicitly reallocates limited codebook capacity from pervasive static backgrounds to critical dynamic agents and effectively isolates spatial redundancy. Leveraging this purified spatial vocabulary, TPS-Drive employs a decoupled reasoning pipeline that sequentially performs scene understanding, future forecasting, and action generation. The framework is optimized via a progressive three-stage training paradigm, culminating in reward-driven refinement that surpasses pure imitation learning. Extensive experiments validate our approach: TPS-Drive achieves accurate agent spatial state forecasting and reduces collision rates in open-loop nuScenes evaluations, while establishing new safety records on the rigorous closed-loop NAVSIMv1 and NAVSIMv2 benchmarks.

</details>


### [187] [Learning to Balance Motor Thermal Safety and Quadrupedal Locomotion Performance with Residual Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27046)
*Yuhang Wan,Weixian Lin,Letian Qian,Yiqi Zou,Weiwei Wu,Shengwei Wu,Chuanlin Zhao,Xin Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对电驱动四足机器人的电机热管理问题，提出了一种将全身热模型集成到强化学习框架中的两阶段训练方法。首先预训练一个标称策略作为基础运动控制，再训练一个残差策略根据热状态提供修正动作，在保证低温高性能的同时防止高温过热。仿真和Unitree A1实物实验（3kg负载下稳定运动超13分钟，而标称策略约5分钟过热）验证了该方法在电机热安全与运动性能之间的有效平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电驱动机器人（尤其是足式机器人）的电机热管理常被忽视，但电机过热是限制长时间运动（特别是在负载条件下）的关键因素。

Method: 将四足机器人全身热模型集成到强化学习管线中以更新电机温度，提出两阶段训练框架：第一阶段预训练标称策略作为基础运动基线；第二阶段在标称策略之上训练残差策略，根据机器人热状态提供修正动作，实现低温高性能与防止高温过热的平衡。

Result: 仿真表明该方法有效平衡了电机热安全与运动性能。在Unitree A1实物实验中，3kg负载下机器人可跨多地形稳定运动超13分钟，而标称策略仅约5分钟即过热。

Conclusion: 所提出的残差策略方法能够有效防止电机过热，显著延长四足机器人在负载条件下的持续运动时间，实现了热安全与运动性能的平衡。

Abstract: Motor thermal management is often overlooked in the context of electrically-actuated robots, particularly legged robots, but motor overheating is a key factor that limits long-duration locomotion especially under payload conditions. This paper integrates a whole-body thermal model of a quadruped robot into the reinforcement learning pipeline to update motor temperatures, and proposes a two-stage training framework for motor thermal management. In this framework, a nominal policy is first pre-trained as a locomotion baseline capable of traversing diverse terrains. A residual policy is then trained on top of the nominal policy to provide corrective actions based on the robot's thermal state, ensuring high performance under low-temperature conditions and preventing motor overheating under high-temperature conditions. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed policy achieves an effective balance between motor thermal safety and locomotion performance. Real-world experiments on a Unitree A1 quadruped robot further validate the approach: under a 3 kg payload, the robot achieves stable locomotion across multiple terrains for over 13 minutes, while the nominal policy alone leads to motor overheating in about 5 minutes.

</details>


### [188] [VR-DAgger: Immersive VR for Dexterous Data Collection and Uncertainty-Guided On-Policy Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27114)
*René Zurbrügg,Tifanny Portela,Arjun Bhardwaj,Aravind Elanjimattathil Vijayan,Maximum Wilder-Smith,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文指出机器人从示范中学习面临数据收集瓶颈，分布偏移导致小错误累积、性能下降，专家时间浪费在低价值修正而非关键失败案例上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决机器人操作任务中从示范学习时数据收集成本高、分布偏移导致性能退化、专家纠正效率低的问题。

Method: （摘要中未明确提及具体方法）

Result: （摘要中未明确提及具体结果）

Conclusion: 需要更高效的数据收集策略，使专家专注于关键失败案例而非冗余的低价值修正。

Abstract: Learning from demonstrations is effective for robotic manipulation, but collecting sufficient task-specific data remains a major bottleneck. Under distribution shift, small errors compound, performance degrades, and expert time is often spent on redundant, low-value corrections instead of the few critical failure cases.

</details>


### [189] [Towards Drone-based Mapping of Volcanic Gases using Gas Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27180)
*Marius Schaab,Niklas Karbach,Antonia Rabe,Thomas Wiedemann,Patrick Hinsen,Dmitriy Shutin,Thorsten Hoffmann,Achim J. Lilienthal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模型的气体层析成像方法，利用无人机搭载开路传感器远程检测火山CO₂排放，解决了无人机旋翼下洗气流对原位传感器的干扰问题，并在泥火山现场验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无人机搭载的原位气体传感器在火山监测中受旋翼下洗气流影响，导致气体羽流在检测前被驱散，无法准确测量；同时，火山气体排放的准确测绘对喷发预测和气候环境研究至关重要。

Method: 采用无人机搭载开路（open-path）遥感传感器进行气体检测，并提出一种基于模型的气体层析成像重建方法，引入拉格朗日模型补偿风致平流效应，从而重建气体分布图。

Result: 在Salinelle dei Cappuccini泥火山现场实验中，无人机原位传感器因空气动力学扰动未能检测到CO₂排放，而开路遥感成功实现了远程气体分布测绘；重建的气体分布图与手动采集的原位测量结果一致。

Conclusion: 基于模型的层析成像方法有效克服了无人机下洗气流的限制，能够实现对火山排放气体的准确定量测绘。

Abstract: Volcanoes emit large amounts of CO2, directly influencing human lives. Mapping volcanic gas emissions helps to forecast eruptions and understand the impact of volcanoes on climate and the environment. Drone-based gas sensing significantly reduces risks in volcanic monitoring but faces technical limitations when measuring gas, as rotor downwash disperses the gas plume before detection. Gas Tomography using remote gas sensing addresses this challenge. At the Salinelle dei Cappuccini mud volcanoes, we demonstrate that while drone-mounted in-situ sensors failed to detect CO2 emissions due to aerodynamic disturbance, open-path sensing successfully enabled remote gas distribution mapping. We present a novel model-based gas tomographic reconstruction approach that incorporates a Lagrangian model to compensate for wind-induced advection. The resulting gas distribution maps align with manually collected in-situ measurements, confirming that model-based gas tomography effectively overcomes downwash limitations and enables accurate mapping of volcanic emissions.

</details>


### [190] [FineVLA: Fine-Grained Instruction Alignment for Steerable Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27284)
*Xintong Hu,Xuhong Huang,Jinyu Zhang,Yutong Yao,Yuchong Sun,Qiuyue Wang,Mingsheng Li,Sicheng Xie,Yitao Liu,Junhao Chen,Yixuan Chen,Yingming Zheng,Shuai Bai,Tao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FineVLA框架，通过细粒度动作级语言标注（如主动臂、接近方向、接触区域等）增强VLA模型的可操控性。构建了含47,159条细粒度轨迹的数据集和对应基准，实验表明细粒度监督不仅不牺牲任务成功率（提升+1.4~+8.1），还与粗粒度指令互补（最优比例FG:Raw=1:2~1:1），在真实双臂操作中显著提升可操控性（姿态+23、颜色+18、接近方向+18）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人数据集通常仅用粗粒度的目标级语言（goal-level）配对轨迹，缺乏执行细节（如主动臂、接近方向、接触区域等），限制了策略的可操控学习（steerable policy learning）和机器人视频理解能力。

Method: (1) 数据构建工具：统一10个开源机器人数据集中的972,247条轨迹/85K任务，构建FineVLA-Data（47,159条人工验证的细粒度轨迹）；(2) 留出基准：500视频、10,816个原子事实、1,030个VQA问题；(3) 机器人专用VLM标注器，用于可扩展的细粒度标注；(4) 使用细粒度与原始目标级指令的受控混合训练可操控VLA策略。

Result: (1) 纯细粒度监督（FG-only）相比纯原始指令（Raw-only）成功率提升+1.4~+8.1个百分点；(2) 细粒度与粗粒度指令互补，呈倒U型趋势，最优混合比例FG:Raw=1:2~1:1，最佳混合设置在RoboTwin模拟中达86.8%/82.5%，真实双臂操作中达62.7/100（Raw-only为49.9）；(3) 细粒度监督提升可操控性，真实世界最大增益体现在姿态（+23）、颜色（+18）和接近方向（+18）。

Conclusion: 细粒度语言应作为目标级指令的增广：在指定"做什么"（what to achieve）的同时，明确"怎么做"（how to execute）。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are increasingly expected to not only complete robot tasks, but also follow human instructions about how those tasks should be executed. However, existing robot datasets usually pair trajectories with coarse goal-level language, leaving execution-critical details such as active arm, approach direction, and contact region unspecified. This limits steerable policy learning and robotic video understanding. We introduce FineVLA, an open framework for action-aligned fine-grained VLA supervision. The framework includes: (1) a data construction tool that unifies 972,247 trajectories across 85K tasks from 10 open-source robot datasets and builds FineVLA-Data, a human-verified dataset of 47,159 fine-grained trajectories; (2) a held-out benchmark with 500 videos, 10,816 atomic facts, and 1,030 VQA questions; (3) a robotics-specialized VLM annotator for scalable fine-grained annotation; and (4) a steerable VLA policy trained with controlled mixtures of fine-grained and raw goal-level instructions. Our experiments yield three findings. First, fine-grained supervision does not sacrifice goal-level success: FG-only improves over Raw-only by +1.4 to +8.1 success-rate points across settings. Second, fine-grained and raw instructions are complementary, following a consistent inverted-U trend peaking at FG:Raw = 1:2 to 1:1. The best mixed setting reaches 86.8%/82.5% in RoboTwin simulation and 62.7/100 in real-world dual-arm manipulation (vs. 49.9 Raw-only). Third, fine-grained supervision improves steerable control: the largest real-world gains appear on pose (+23), color (+18), and approach direction (+18)--factors where goal-level instructions provide no guidance. Overall, fine-grained language should augment goal-level instructions: specifying how to execute alongside what to achieve. Project page: https://finevla.xlang.ai/

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [Generalized Range Filtering Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search: Containment and Overlap [Technical Report]](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26474)
*Yingfan Liu,Tong Wu,Jiadong Xie,Yang Zhao,Jeffrey Xu Yu,Jiangtao Cui*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种支持任意范围-范围（RR）谓词的近似最近邻（ANN）搜索方法——多段树图（MSTG），用于处理每个向量关联数值范围属性的场景。该方法通过避免遍历不满足谓词的节点，在保持与现有方法相当索引规模和构建时间的同时，实现了高达12.5倍的加速，并支持包含、重叠等用户自定义RR谓词。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有带范围过滤的ANN搜索方法不支持任意RR谓词（如包含、重叠或其析取），无法满足广义范围属性（如价格区间、时间区间）上的灵活查询需求。

Method: 提出多段树图（Multi-Segment Tree Graph, MSTG）方法，通过设计树图结构避免遍历不满足RR谓词的节点，高效支持任意用户自定义的RR谓词。

Result: 在真实数据集上，MSTG在RRANN查询中实现高达12.5倍加速且精度不变；在RFANN上性能相当，在IFANN和TSANN上显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: MSTG有效解决了RRANN查询问题，推广了现有带范围过滤的ANN搜索变体，具备高效性和通用性。

Abstract: Approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search with range filters has recently garnered significant attention. This paper delves into a generalized form of this problem, i.e., ANN search with exact range-range (RR) predicates on a range-valued attribute, named RR filtering ANN (RRANN). Specifically, given $n$ vectors in $\mathbb{R}^d$, each vector $v_i$ is associated with a numeric range $[l_i, r_i]$, symbolizing aspects like a price range or time interval. An RRANN query $(v_q, l_q, r_q)$ aims at finding $k$ vectors closest to $v_q$ within the vectors satisfying an arbitrary RR predicate defined between the query range $[l_q, r_q]$ and the object range $[l_i, r_i]$. The RR predicate remains unspecified, enabling user-defined conditions. It may encompass containment ($[l_i, r_i] \subseteq [l_q, r_q]$ or $[l_q, r_q] \subseteq [l_i, r_i]$), overlap ($l_i \le l_q \le r_i \le r_q$ or $l_q \le l_i \le r_q \le r_i$), or a disjunction of them. RRANN has broad applications in queries related to price ranges or time intervals, and it generalizes existing variants of ANN search with range filters. However, existing dedicated approaches for these problems lack the capacity to support queries with arbitrary RR predicates. Hence, we introduce a new approach, labeled multi-segment tree graph. It efficiently handles arbitrary RR predicates by avoiding traversal through non-predicate-satisfied nodes, and keeps equivalent index size and construction time to state-of-the-art methods for RFANN. Extensive experiments on real-world data demonstrate the efficacy of our approach in RRANN queries, achieving up to 12.5x speedups with the same accuracy as the baselines. Moreover, our approach attains comparable RFANN search performance and notably superior IFANN and TSANN search performance compared to the respective state-of-the-art approaches. Our code is available at https://github.com/FanEDG/MSTG.

</details>


### [192] [RT-RkNN: Reverse k Nearest Neighbor Queries as a Graphics Ray Casting Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26671)
*Zhengyang Bai,Peng Chen,Mohamed Wahib*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出将二维空间中的反向k最近邻（RkNN）查询问题重新表述为图形光线投射问题，并在现代GPU上利用专用硬件光线追踪核心实现了首个算法，在传统方法失效的困难场景（小设施数量、密集用户群、大k值）下仍保持强过滤性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于R树及其变体的空间剪枝技术在设施数量少、用户密度大或k值大的挑战性场景下剪枝效果显著下降，亟需一种在这些困难条件下仍能保持高效性能的新方法。

Method: 将RkNN查询问题重新表述为图形光线投射问题：将用户建模为光线，设施表示为几何图元；基于该公式，首次开发了利用现代GPU上专用硬件光线追踪核心的算法。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提方法在各种设置下均优于现有最先进算法，特别是在传统剪枝策略失效的场景中表现出显著优势。

Conclusion: 基于光线投射的硬件加速方法有效克服了传统空间剪枝的局限性，即使在k值大、用户密度高、设施分布稀疏的场景下也能保持强过滤性能，为RkNN查询处理提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Reverse k nearest neighbor (RkNN) queries are fundamental in spatial databases, location-based analytics, and recommendation systems. Existing state-of-the-art techniques rely on spatial pruning supported by R-trees and their variants. However, their pruning effectiveness degrades significantly in challenging scenarios where the number of facilities is small, the user population is dense, or the value of k is large. To overcome these limitations, this work reformulates the RkNN query problem in two-dimensional geometric spaces as a graphics ray-casting problem, where users are modeled as rays and facilities are represented as geometric primitives. Based on this formulation, the first algorithm and implementation exploiting dedicated hardware ray-tracing cores on modern GPUs are developed. This novel approach preserves strong filtering performance even for large values of k, dense user populations, and highly sparse facility distributions. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms state-of-the-art algorithms across diverse settings, particularly in scenarios where traditional pruning strategies become inefficient.

</details>


### [193] [Knowledge Graphs as the Missing Data Layer for LLM-Based Industrial Asset Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26874)
*Madhulatha Mandarapu,Sandeep Kunkunuru*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文通过引入知识图谱层（781节点，955边）替代扁平文档存储，将工业资产运维场景的LLM Agent准确率从65%提升至99%（确定性图处理器）和82-83%（LLM生成Cypher查询），表明数据层而非LLM编排是结构化运维领域的主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的工业资产运维Agent在扁平文档存储（CouchDB、YAML、CSV）上推理准确率有限（GPT-4仅65%），已有工作关注LLM编排范式对比，但数据模型对Agent性能的影响尚未被系统研究。

Method: 基于相同139个工业运维场景，引入知识图谱层（781节点，955边，16种关系类型），对比三种架构：(1) 确定性图处理器（无LLM）；(2) LLM生成Cypher查询（GPT-4）；(3) 原工具增强LLM基线。额外贡献40个图原生场景（多跳依赖、向量相似性、PageRank关键性），并在HuggingFace扩展版AssetOpsBench（467场景，6领域）上验证。

Result: (1) 确定性图处理器达到99%（137/139）；(2) LLM生成Cypher达到82-83%；(3) 原基线仅65%（91/139）。扩展版上确定性处理器达100%（467/467），平均得分0.848。

Conclusion: 对于结构化运维领域，数据层（而非LLM编排）是主要瓶颈；知识图谱可作为原始工业数据与LLM推理之间的集成层。核心发现是"倒置LLM用法"：让LLM基于类型化模式生成结构化查询，而非直接推理原始数据，图层负责确定性执行。

Abstract: LLM-based agents for industrial asset operations show limited accuracy when reasoning over flat document stores. AssetOpsBench (KDD 2026) establishes that GPT-4 agents achieve 65% on 139 industrial maintenance scenarios backed by CouchDB, YAML, and CSV. It compares LLM orchestration paradigms (Agent-As-Tool vs Plan-Execute) on a fixed data layer; we ask a complementary, orthogonal question: how much does the data model behind the tools affect agent performance?
  Building on the same scenarios, we introduce a knowledge graph layer (781 nodes, 955 edges, 16 relationship types) and evaluate three architectures: (1) deterministic graph handlers (no LLM) at 99% (137/139); (2) LLM-generated Cypher over the graph at 82-83% with the same GPT-4 model the baseline uses; and (3) the original tool-augmented LLM baseline at 65% (91/139, matching the published KDD 2026 leaderboard ceiling).
  Our key finding is inverted LLM usage: rather than asking the LLM to reason over raw data, we ask it to generate structured queries from a typed schema. The graph executes deterministically. We additionally contribute 40 graph-native scenarios (multi-hop dependency, vector similarity, PageRank criticality), and evaluate against the expanded HuggingFace AssetOpsBench release (467 scenarios, 6 domains), where deterministic handlers achieve 100% (467/467) with average score 0.848.
  These results suggest that for structured operational domains, the data layer -- not the LLM orchestration -- is the primary bottleneck, and that knowledge graphs serve as an integration layer between raw industrial data and LLM-based reasoning.

</details>
